## Partner link: a patient's file linked to their partner's (her 2026-10-14 "build this"; commit 8f92d88, LIVE in build 20261015-870000, main 6a119ae)
Guide `partner-link-guide-2026-10-14.html` (made-up couple Daniel / Sarah). **Her 8 picks (do not re-ask):** both places; a partner who is not a patient = just a name; ALL of hers on his timeline (egg collection + transfer, pregnancy test, fertile window, her other tests); her own section in her colour that slides in; dates AND results; the gold 74-day sperm-cycle bracket; his semen analysis fills her row; her positive test = a gold line + Mark completed, his plan ends only on her tap.
- **Stored:** only `rec.partner = { name, me, at }` (`me` = this record's own display name, so the other side can name it). Both records get it when the partner is a patient; a partner who is not a patient is only a name on his record: **no record is made for her** (a record would put her in the patient list). Writes: `phPartnerSet(name, pname)` (clears both people out of any old link first, so nobody is ever in two links; returns the Undo snapshot) and `phPtnClearData(name)` (own field, the other side's, and anyone who names them). Undo = `phPtnSnap` / `phPtnRestore` through the body-level `phCycToast` bar.
- **Read:** `phPartnerOf(name)` = own `rec.partner`, else the newest record whose `partner.name` is this person ("who names me"), so **a name-only partner links up by herself** once someone of that name is a patient. "Is a patient" = a record, or a row in `phDirBuild()` (scripts, appointments, intake, log...; memo 2 s, `phPtnDir`). Returns `{ name, key, first, patient, rec, sex, self }`.
- **Patient details > Identity:** a Partner row after Phone / Email (`phPtnRowHtml`, host `data-ptn-host`): her chip in her sex colour (gynae tint for a woman, men's indigo for a man) with her current plan, tap = open her file (`data-ph-dash-patient-open`), ✕ = unlink (Undo); name only = grey chip "· not a patient"; nobody = "+ Partner".
- **One search, two places** (`phPtnPick = { name, ctx: "pd" | "tl", q }`, view state): input `#phPtnQ` (`field-bare`), results only after typing (`phPtnResHtml`: up to 6 patients whose name holds the text, with sex · age and "partner of X" when already linked elsewhere, then `Use "<text>" · not a patient` unless the text is exactly a patient's name); Enter = the first line, Esc / Cancel close it. Typing repaints only `[data-ptn-res]`. Every change repaints in place through `phPtnRepaint` (Partner rows, every timeline, the Care Plan line): never a page render.
- **His Men's fertility Timeline** (`phTlHtml`, `m.ptn = phTlPtnData(name, plan)`): "+ Partner" in the head while none is linked (the search opens under the head). Linked: her section under his items (`phTlPtnSectionHtml`, tinted in her colour `--pc` via `.ph-tlx.ptn-f / -m / -u`, read-only, same five columns, "Open file"); her dates as a fourth row of pins on his ruler (`.pin.ptn`, top 98, dashed lane line); the **sperm-cycle bracket** (`phTlBrHtml`: gold, collection − 74 days to collection, "sperm for ~13 Nov forming (74 days)") with **his Build bar** under it (phase 1 `sinceKey` else the plan start, to its `doneKey` else + 12 weeks, mens tint). Shown only when her collection is dated and not over 120 days ago. Lanes view: a lane for her and a "Sperm cycle" lane (`phTlPtnLanesHtml`). The axis widens to her dates and the bracket (`phTlAxis` reads `m.ptn`). The section slides in and the bracket draws once per open (`phPtnSeen`), none under reduced motion. Not a patient = one quiet line "Mia Lane · not a patient", nothing of hers. The Care Plan side list (`phTlSideHtml`) is unchanged (his items only).
- **What of hers** (`phTlPtnData`, read from a SHALLOW COPY of her record because `phCycleFor` / `phCycFacts` can hang `.cycle` on the record they get): her current timeline plan's whole list (Active and unfinished first, `phPfPlanDone`; IVF / Natural fertility / Pregnancy) minus her "Partner semen analysis" row; her latest IVF round from IVF history when her plan does not place it (collection with egg count, transfer with embryo count); her pregnancy test when her list has no item that reads it; her **fertile window** from her cycle (`phCycFacts`: the 5 days before ovulation, or LH-confirmed; the next one once this one has passed and her period is not late; "Now" / "Coming up" / "Passed"; type chip "Cycle", green). Sorted by date.
- **Her timeline** (Natural fertility AND IVF; the IVF list gained the row `semen` "Partner semen analysis" at the top of `PH_TL_DEFAULTS.ivf`): the row fills from his latest done `men:sa1` / `men:sa2` (`phPtnSemenFor`, `by: "partner"`, shows "from Daniel's file"); a mark she typed on her own row still wins. **Her own edited IVF standard list (`PHARMACY.tlSchedules.ivf`) does not get the new row by itself**: she adds it on the Standard list sheet if she has one.
- **Egg count everywhere:** `phTlModel`'s collection item now carries the linked round's egg count ("13 Nov · 9 eggs") on her timeline and his.
- **His Care Plan** (`phPtnPosLineHtml`, host `data-ptn-pos`, under the band, Men's fertility plans only, not Completed): when her latest positive (`phPtnPositive`: a dated blood / urine test, a "weeks" / "due date" answer counted from the day it was recorded, or a transfer result) is on or after his plan's start (`phTlPlanStart`): gold line "Sarah: positive, 13 Oct · hCG 245" + "✓ Mark completed" (`data-tp-status-set`, the ⋯ menu's own handler). Nothing ends his plan by itself.
- **CSS** block "Partner link" after `.ph-tlx-detail .row` (`.ph-tlx-ptn*`, `.ph-tlx-br`, `.ph-tlx-bld`, `.ph-ptn-*`), reduced-motion block included. Wording ("Partner", "· not a patient", "sperm for … forming (74 days)", "Nothing on Sarah's timeline yet.") is DRAFT.
- **Known / disclosed:** a renamed or merged patient keeps the old name in the other side's `partner.name` (re-link after a rename); `rec.partner` rides the normal patient-record sync (no special merge); a same-sex couple works (her colour follows her sex) but a female partner has no semen row to fill; a natural-fertility partner gets no bracket (there is no collection date to end it at); his Care Plan side list does not list her items.
- Tested headless on the staged file (65 checks: link both ways, reverse read, name-only + link-up, re-link frees the old partner, Undo, semen fill on IVF and Natural fertility + newest wins + her own mark wins, his section / pins / bracket / Build bar / lanes / draw-once, egg count, positive line incl. old positive and Completed plan, fertile window, her record untouched by his render, the real taps: open search, type, pick, toast Undo, Esc, Enter with a typed name, ✕, timeline "+ Partner"; colours; reduced-motion rule; no console errors). Men's fertility checks 58/58 and the links audit 19/19 unchanged. Pictures `partner-link-*-2026-10-14.png`. A tap on her chip reaches the app's own opener (`phPatientProfileOpen("Sarah Reyes")`); the panel actually switching to her file was not seen (the sandbox draws the sign-in screen over the real panel). NOT exercised: a real patient, her phone.

## Today's visit = the complaint compass; Energy 1-10 (her 2026-10-14; PUSHED LIVE on her "push live": main 6d4be50, build 20261014-830000)
Her words (with a patient's Today's visit): "main complaint and chief complaint are the most important thing in the treatment plan because its what the patient wants to focus on, and its like a compass, it will direct the treatment. i need to track it every appointment visit. redesign this page, make all elements fonts colours chips design consistent, add relevant visual animation workflow elements including charts and graphs to show improvement overtime", then mid-build "change energy from 1-5 to 1-10". Guide `complaint-compass-guide-2026-10-14.html` (synthetic patient). **Her 15 picks (do not re-ask):** C + A (chart over rows); "percent and 0-10" = ONE strip, switchable; Re-check folded into the compass; Energy one line under the rows; real dates spaced as they were; start from today (no past score invented); shown everywhere the complaint is read; one note per complaint per visit; the compass replaces the Came in for copy here; one quiet progress line (gold next card + "0 of 5 done" band gone); range = this plan (All a chip away); case colour for main, app blue / teal / grey with their own dash for the others; all four motions; phone = main line only, rows stack.
- **Card** `phCxCardHtml(t, plan, w)` at the top of `phTodayVisitHtml` (host `[data-cx-host]`, `data-cx-name`, `data-cx-plan`, `data-cx-w`): header (caps label, This plan | All when older scores exist, 0–10 | % better, "7 visits · since 22 Jul"), the chart (`phCxChartSvg`, viewBox = the card's width; `setTimeout(phCxChartRepaint, 0)` after the first paint and a debounced window resize redraw it when the width differs by 24px+), legend chips (`data-cx-pick`, light one line and dim the rest: `phCxLit`, attribute `data-cx-lit` + `.lit`), one `.ph-cx-row` per complaint (`phCxRowHtml`: Main tag, since, pills `data-cx-pick-v="<key>:<n>"`, change `data-cx-delta`, note `data-cx-note` / `data-cx-note-in`), "+ add another" (`phCxAddSecondary`: a secondary with `since` = today), then `phEnergyLineHtml`. No chief complaint at all (no typed one, nothing on file) = the old Came in for card stands in. Clicks: `phCxClick` (first in the click chain, before `phRcClick`); note and add boxes: change + keydown listeners (Enter saves / adds, Esc drops).
- **Keys:** main = `"main"`, a secondary = `"s:<id>"`; a pill value is split on the LAST colon (`lastIndexOf`), never `split(":")`.
- **Stored** `rec.ccTrack = { v:1, scale:"ten"|"pct", days:{ "YYYY-MM-DD": { main:{v,sc,n}, sec:{ [id]:{v,sc,n} }, at } } }`, written only by `phCxWrite` (tidies empty entries, stamps today's `at`, then `phCxMirrorRecheck`). Reads: `phCxHist(rec, key)`, `phCxVal(entry, hist, scale)` (an entry in the other scale converts through the FIRST 0-10 score as the baseline; no 0-10 score at all: 100% = 0, 0% = 10, my call), `phCxChange` (today vs the last scored day before it: "↓ 1 better" / "→ same" / "↑ 2 worse"; "first score" when nothing earlier), `phCxModel`, `phCxPlanStart` (earliest phase `sinceKey` or `createdDate`). The verdict text counts up from 0 after a tap (`phCxAfter`), the new dot drops with a gold ring, the pill pops, the main line draws once per session (`phCxSeen`).
- **Re-check folded in:** every write mirrors the main verdict into `rec.recheck.change` (+ note) and the secondaries into `rec.recheck.other`; `phRcDoneToday(rec)` = the old pick OR a main score today (read by `phTodayDonePart`, `presSecDone`). The Log lists one "Re-check · Perimenopause 5 → 4 (note) · insomnia 3" line per scored day (`presLogEntries`; the old record only for a day the compass has not scored). Patient details' small chart (`presSparkHtml` -> `phCxSparkHtml`) draws the main complaint's last 8 scores once one exists, else the old Better / Same / Worse. The Treatment tab's Response: `presAcuDraftRestore` pre-fills `presAcuOutcomeDraft` from `phCxOutcomeToday` and `presAcuOutcomeCx` marks it as the compass's, so a later tap moves it and her own pick (compact Response row, Objective roll-up) never is. The old Re-check card's code (`phRcCardHtml`, `phRcRefresh`, `phRcWrite`, `phRcOtherWrite`, `phRcOpen`, `phRcClick`, the `data-rc-note-in` change listener, the `.ph-rc` / `.ph-rc-chips` / `.ph-rc-fold` / `.ph-rc-other` / `.ph-rc-minichip` / `.ph-today-gate` / `.ph-today-tile.recheck` CSS) was REMOVED on her word 2026-10-14 (0a7f892; PUSHED LIVE on her "push live": main 9e6b1da, build 20261014-840000). Kept: `PH_RC_OPTS` (the vocabulary of `rec.recheck.change`, read by the Log), `phRcDoneToday`, `phTodayChromeRefresh`, and the `.ph-rc-chip` CSS, which the Signs / Medical history / Linked symptoms / Quick assess / Timeline sheets reuse as a plain pill chip (a naming wart, not re-check code).
- **Progress line** `phTodayProgHtml(t, rec)` (`[data-today-prog]`: bar + "2 of 5 done" + "next: Tongue" chip from `presVisitNext`), repainted by `phTodayChromeRefresh`. The gold Up-next card's code went too, on her word 2026-10-14 ("remove the dead up-next bare branch too"): `presUpNextHtml(t)` now returns the last-visits chart alone (no `bare` argument, no card markup, no `data-pf-bare`), and `phUpClick`, `presUpSkip` (with its read in `presVisitNext` and its clear on open), `PRES_UP_WHY`, the `[data-pf-bare]` repaint in `presFlowRailRefresh`, the `.ph-pf-up` / `.ph-pf-uprow.two` / `.gone` CSS and the `phPfUpPulse` keyframe are gone. Kept: `presUpSeen`, `PRES_UP_FILE`, `presVisitNext` (the rail's Next up picks the same step it did; nothing can be skipped any more, which is what the card's removal already meant). The `.ph-today-gate` CSS went with the Re-check code.
- **Energy 1-10:** `PH_EN_MAX`, `PH_EN_NUMS`, `phEnV(d, k)` is the ONE read (a day without `s10` is a 1-5 day, read doubled); `phEnWrite` converts an old-scale day before writing and stamps `s10:1`; `phEnLvl` lo <= 5 / mid < 7 / hi; signs: Bing `fatigue_now` <= 5, Ren `fatigue_tend` <= 5, `vitality_intact_tend` >= 9 ("Energy 4 of 10"); Patterns fatigue clue <= 5; words by pairs (`phEnWord`). All three Energy shapes (card, Bing-column group, the compass line) draw 1-10; `phEnRefresh` repaints each in its own shape (`data-en-line`).
- **CSS** block "Complaint compass" after `.ph-today-dispgate`: one type scale (16 / 13 / 12 / 11), pills = numbers she picks, `.ph-cx-btn` square = acts, `.ph-cx` is a size container (under 620px: rows use grid areas nm / sn / dl over pl over nt, secondary lines hide unless lit); reduced-motion block turns every animation off.
- **Disclosed:** conversion rule above; "visit" = one record per calendar day (a second tap the same day replaces); a % entry does not light a 0-10 pill (and the reverse); the chart's y axis is 0-10 "how bad" (10 at the top) or 0-100 "% better" (100 at the top), so "better" goes DOWN on 0-10 and UP on %; the Energy line is three wrapped rows on a phone.
- Tested in the sandbox (Browser pane + headless Edge, synthetic patient, 1280 / 400 px): taps on main and secondaries (write, mirror, verdict, count-up, drop), clear, worse, scale switch both ways with conversion, range switch, legend light / unlight, note open / save / Esc, add another (new row, since = today), empty patient (rows only, chart from the first score), Patient details spark, Log lines, done stamps, Response pre-fill, Energy taps on a day with old 1-5 values, no console errors, no sideways scroll at 400px. NOT exercised: a real patient, her phone, reduced motion on a real device, the Treatment tab rendered with the pre-filled Response.
- **Restored 2026-10-14 ~22:05 (her pick "do it in my Chrome for me"):** 227 photos (211 from 10-09 + 14 from 10-12 + 5 from 10-13, same ids overwritten) for 34 patients are back in the Chrome Default store, `navigator.storage.persisted()` = true. Recipe, because `file_upload` caps at 10 MB and the 10-09 file is 57.7 MB: the scratch HttpListener (`serve.ps1`) answers CORS + `Access-Control-Allow-Private-Network` and serves the app folder, so the live tab `fetch`es `http://localhost:8977/<backup>.json` and runs the app's own put loop (`phRenDataUrlToBlob` → `phRenPhotoPut({id, patientKey, kind, mime, createdAt, blob})`, then clears `phRenPhotoCache` / `phRecPhotosCache`) kicked asynchronously and polled via `window.__rest` (a hidden tab must not run one long call). No cloud write, no reload, nothing deleted (her firm rule, same night). Photos added on the laptop after 10-13 12:54 are not in any backup.

## Records → Photos: label with one tap (her 2026-10-14 "make it easier for me to categorise photos. let me do that with less clicks and faster")
Measured first: one photo took 3 clicks (open the viewer, chip, close) and the one-by-one sorter lived only inside a patient's own Photos section. Her 8 picks (memory `project_pharmacy_photo_labelling_2026_10_14`, do not re-ask): **A + B + C** all on this page (chips under the photos, a Sort strip, select several then one label); chips on **every** photo, always; after a tap "saved, then the next one opens" = **the next photo still needing a label is rung and scrolled to**; **taps only, no keyboard**, no key hints; a tongue tile = **Natural · Flash · Sublingual · Side (two per row) + one "Tongue ▾" chip** that unfolds the other types, any other tile = its type chip only; the sorter walks **newest day first**; **150 px tiles**; desktop-first (the phone keeps today's viewer). LIVE in build 20261015-880000; the keyboard + no-jump round (next bullet) PUSHED LIVE on her "push live" 2026-10-15: main 28508a5, build 20261015-890000.
- **Tile** (`phRecPhotosThumbHtml`): `[data-recphotos-tile=id]`, class `need` while unsorted or a tongue photo with no shot (`phRecPhotosNeeds` = `phRenNeedsSort`), `cur` on the tile rung after a tap, `sel` / `ticked` in select mode. Chips = `phRecPhotosChipsHtml(r)`: `data-recphotos-shot="id|shot"` (the lit one tapped again = shot off, the viewer's own rule), `data-recphotos-type-open="id"` (one tile unfolded at a time, `phRecPhotosTypeOpen`; the list = `phRenTypesFor` minus Unsorted and the current type, two columns, plus "Keep <type>"), `data-recphotos-type="id|kind"` (a non-tongue kind drops the shot). Amber `.need` chips while the tile still wants a label; the patient's name is a quiet pill, not an underlined link (her chips-not-links rule).
- **One write** `phRecPhotosLabel(id, fn, msg)`: `phRenPhotoPut`, patch the flat record in place, refresh the patient's own cache ONLY if it is warm, all under `phRecPhotosQuiet` so `phRenLoadPhotosFor`'s "reload the Records flat cache" hook does NOT rebuild the page; repaint ONE tile (`phRecPhotosTileRefresh`; the whole body only when grouped By type and the kind moved), then `phRecPhotosHeadRefresh` (count line, "Sort N" / "All labelled ✓", Select / Done), `phRecPhotosFocusNext` (next `.need` tile in page order, wraps, `scrollIntoView` centre, smooth unless reduced motion; none left = "all labelled ✓" in the toast), `phCycToast` with Undo (never `phFlashShow` here).
- **Select** (toolbar "Select" ↔ "Done", `phRecPhotosSelect`): a tick on every tile (`data-recphotos-pick`), a tap on the picture ticks too (nothing opens), chips hidden; the bar at the top of the body (`phRecPhotosBarHtml`, `data-recphotos-bulk="tongue:<shot>"` / `"<kind>"`, common types first, "More types ▾" = `phRecPhotosBulkMore`, `phRecPhotosBarRefresh` repaints only the bar). `phRecPhotosBulk` → `phRecPhotosWriteMany` (writes from the flat records, so no patient has to be loaded; warm per-patient caches refreshed after), one toast, one Undo for the lot.
- **Sort N** (toolbar, `data-recphotos-sort`) = the shared viewer's sort flow made clinic-wide, NOT a second strip (disclosed): `phRenSortFlowStart("*")`; `phRenSortFlowAllNeeds("*")` = `phRecPhotosNeedList(true)` (newest day first; a warm per-patient record wins over a stale flat one, since the flat cache reloads asynchronously after each viewer write); `phRenSortFlowOrder` keeps that order for "*" (a patient's own flow stays oldest first); `phRenSortFlowOpen` loads the patient then opens with `fromLibrary`; done → `presSavedToast("All labelled ✓")` + `phRecPhotosRefresh()`. The viewer's own shot / type / Skip handlers are unchanged.
- **CSS** at `.ph-recphotos-grid` (150 px minimum tiles; `.ph-recphotos-chips`, `.types`, `.cur` ring + `phRecRing` pulse, off under reduced motion; `.ph-recphotos-tick`; `.ph-recphotos-bar`). No delete anywhere on this page (her firm rule). The duplicate finder is untouched.
- Tested in the Browser pane with synthetic photos in IndexedDB (chips per kind, a shot tap writes + rings the next tile + leaves the scroll alone, the lit chip toggles off, the type list opens one tile at a time, retype + Undo, the store persists, select + bulk + Undo, the Sort flow newest first through a label / Skip / Skip / done, no console errors) and headless screenshots at 1280 px. NOT exercised: a real patient, the phone, labelling while grouped By type, a real smooth scroll.
- **Her 2026-10-15 corrections, using it live on 237 photos:** (1) "screen keeps popping down" while she worked along the top row: `phRecPhotosRing(el)` now moves the page ONLY when the ringed tile is out of view, and then by the least amount (`block: "nearest"`), never centred; `phRecPhotosFocusNext` rings through it. (2) **"let me use keyboard to organise the photos" — reverses her 2026-10-14 "taps only, no key hints".** `phRecPhotosKey` (document keydown, after `phRecPhotosKeyOn`: Records → Photos on screen, no box focused, no viewer open, Select off): ← → ↑ ↓ move the ring (↑ ↓ = the nearest tile in the row above / below by centre), Enter = the next photo still needing a label, 1 2 3 4 = Natural / Flash / Sublingual / Side on the ringed tile (any kind becomes Tongue · shot; the lit shot again = off), T unfolds its type list, then 1–9 pick and Esc keeps, Esc alone lets go of the ring; a first key with no ring just rings the first photo needing a label. The key letters are `<kbd>` inside the chips, shown on the ringed tile ONLY (the question cards' rule; `.ph-recphotos-thumb.cur .ph-recphotos-chips kbd`), plus a hover title on the type chip. Tested in the Browser pane (every key above, the search box swallowing keys, Undo toast) and headless pictures. (3) "screen keeps glitching" while keying through: `phRecPhotosTileRefresh` now KEEPS the `<img>` node (a rebuilt one blanked while it re-decoded) and swaps only the classes, caption, tick and chips; the chips have a fixed 24px height so the key letters appearing on the ringed tile never reflow the rows; the ring's pulse animation is gone (it re-fired on every key); a needed scroll is instant (`behavior: "auto"`), smooth scrolls per key stacked into a judder. Checked: tile height identical ringed / unringed, img node identity kept across a label, no animation.

## Find the Ren: look first, then two questions at a time (her 2026-10-15 "develop a quick question algorithm to find the patient ren category in the fastest way possible ... so i can save time during consults"; committed on `session-a` — LIVE on main (audited 2026-10-18: origin/main has since advanced past this build))
Her screenshot: the Ren page of a 20F acne patient with 0 ticks. Mock `ren-quick-find-mock-2026-10-15.html` (A look-then-ask, B questions only, the measured table). **Measured first on the real tables** (28 families, 286 Ren signs, a woman, stop at 85% sure, 336 made-up patients built the way the engine expects a family to look): questions alone one at a time = 10.1 asked, four at a time (today's Quick assess) = 14.5, **six looks then one at a time = 4.8**, then two at a time = 6.2, then four = 7.7; the six looks alone leave a 44% lead. **Her 8 picks (do not re-ask):** look first; top of the Ren page only (Quick assess on Fang Bing Ren stays); **two questions at a time**; the six look rows; **Ren only**; found = fold to one line + the lists show that family's signs first; keys 1-9 / 0 on the gold row and Y / N on the question; open until found, then folded.
- **Card** `phRqCardHtml(name)` (`.ph-qa-card.ph-rq`, `data-rq-card`), drawn by `phRenTabHtml` between the heading and the lists; every tap is handled by `phRqClick` (first branch of `phRenTabClick`) and repaints through `phRenTabRefresh`. All view state (`phRqSlots`, `phRqUndo`, `phRqKeep`, `phRqOpen`, `phRqLookOpen`, `phRqLast`, `phRqGlow`), nothing new on the record.
- **Look** = `PH_RQ_LOOK`: six rows of the Ren signs whose subject is Build, Complexion (label "Face"), Skin, Lips, Eyes, Muscles (`phRqRows`, sex-filtered, 41 signs for a woman), drawn as the question cards' own rows (`.ph-pfw-q`, 78px label, `.ph-pfw-opt` pills, gold `.cur` on the first row without an answer, `kbd` 1-9 / 0 on that row only). A tap (`data-rq-look`) = `phTriSetYes` on that sign, then every other sign of the row with no answer and no chart Yes reads No with `auto[o] = { from: <the Yes>, link: "look" }` (so Who answered what lists it and `phTriClearYes` takes it back when the Yes goes; pick several by tapping more). **"none of these"** (`data-rq-none`) = her own plain No on the row (`phTriSetNo(..., true)`); a second tap lets the row go blank. A row is done when every sign has an answer; with all six done the rows fold to one "Looked:" line (Change / Fold = `data-rq-change`).
- **Ask** (only after the six looks, only while not found): `PH_RQ_AT_ONCE` = 2 rows from `phQaPickRow(S, skip, slots, ["p"])` over `phNqState(rec, name, "p")` (both gained the extra argument; the Fang Bing Ren Quick assess is unchanged), the same leave-and-refill as Quick assess (`phQaSettleLater(name, phRqSlots, phRenTabRefresh, ":rq")`, which gained those arguments). Yes / No = `data-rq-ans` + `data-rq-key` through `phTriSetYes` / `phTriSetNo`; Not sure = the shared `phQaSkipSet`. The first open row wears `.first` and the Y / N key letters.
- **Found** (`phRqState`: Ren-only lead >= `PH_RQ_SURE` 0.85): the write that crosses it sets `phTriFilt` to that family, folds the card (`phRqOpen` cleared) and plays the gold glow once (`phRqGlow`, off under reduced motion). The folded line: tick, colour dot, family, "95% sure · 6 looks, 1 answer", **Show / Showing its signs** (`data-rq-filt`, toggles `phTriFilt`), **Keep asking** (`data-rq-keep`, rows come back). The lists under the card (`phRqListsHtml`) show the picked family's own signs first, strongest first, in its colour (`.ph-rq-fam`, "Dang Gui Shao Yao San · 5 of 17"), then the usual grouped board for the rest; no family picked = the board as before. **Undo** (`data-rq-undo`) restores both maps, the link bookkeeping, the dates, the audit, the rows on screen and the family filter.
- **Bars** = the four likeliest families from the Ren answers (shown once a look or an answer exists; `data-rq-ev` opens `phQaEvidenceHtml`); the "what the last answer changed" line is `phNqChangeLine` as on Quick assess.
- **Keys**: one capture-phase keydown listener; off while a box has focus or a photo is being filed (`phRenCropState`).
- Sandbox-tested with a synthetic 20F through the real handlers and real key events: six looks (taps and keys 8 / 0 / 3), none-of-these on and off, the Looked line, two ask rows, Y on the first row -> found at 95% (fold, glow, filter, family block 17 rows), heading reopen, Keep asking, Undo (lead back to 72%, filter cleared), the filter chip both ways, 360px with no sideways scroll, the Fang Bing Ren card still builds. NOT exercised: a real patient, the Who answered what panel by eye, her phone.

## One toast (audit step 3, 2026-10-15; LIVE in build 20261015-880000, main d245597)
The audit found seven message mechanisms. Now: **`presSavedToast(msg, opt)` is the one body-level pill** (`#presSavedToast.ph-saved-toast`, styled outside `#pharmacyPage`): `opt` = a number (how long it stays) or `{ ms, undo }`; with an Undo function the pill grows an Undo button and stays 6.5 s; `presSavedToastHide()` takes it down. `phCycToast(msg, undo)` is an alias of it; `phTpPlanToastShow` (session planner "Planned ... Undo") and `phRfcShowToast` (Refill workbench Undo) call it; their own elements, timers and CSS are gone. **Still its own thing on purpose:** the Appointments dialog's in-dialog bar `phApptToast` (positioned inside the dialog, with an icon).
- **`phFlashShow(msg)`** on a patient's profile or plan page repaints only the panel (`renderPresPanel`, which keeps her scroll on the same page) and says the message as the toast; it no longer rebuilds the whole pharmacy page there. Where a page template places `phFlashHtml()` (the Price list screens, Stocktake, the Appointments grid, the Refill workbench, the Dispense confirm zone, a new draft) the green banner still draws there, as before. A message no slot drew (Dashboard, Inventory, Restock, Communications, Settings, Photos: it used to be lost and pop up later on some other page) is said as the toast after the render. Those non-patient tabs still rebuild on `phFlashShow` as they always did. Message markup is read as text for the pill (`phFlashText`).
- **A new message:** call `presSavedToast` directly where the page must not rebuild (a patient page, a sheet, a dialog); call `phFlashShow` only where the rebuild is wanted anyway. Never add another toast element.
- Tested in the sandbox (synthetic patient): cycle Day 1 Undo, planner Undo, refill Undo, a flash on the Cycle page (toast, scroll kept, same shell node), on the Dispense stage (banner in the confirm zone, no toast), on Inventory (toast), the pill at 375 px. NOT exercised: a real patient, her phone, the Appointments dialog bar.
- Left as it is (found during step 5): the photo-delete toast `#phRenUndoToast` ("Photo deleted · Undo", `phRenUndoToastRefresh`) is still its own element; folding it into `presSavedToast` is a small follow-up, not done.

## Links to chips, arrows off (audit step 5, 2026-10-15; LIVE in build 20261015-900000)
> Superseded in part by TAPPABLE THINGS at the top: the "33 former links read as the outlined pill, 10px corners" look is replaced by the five shapes (chip pill 28px, button 6px). The no-underline and no-arrow rules below stand.
Her universal rule (2026-10-14, "i dont like links, i prefer chips"): no underlined, dotted or arrow text links anywhere; every tap target is a chip or a button. The Cycle page got it first; this step applies it to the whole app.
- **One rule, placed just before the PHONE REDESIGN block** ("audit step 5" comment): the 33 former underlined text links read as the outlined pill of the 10/10 one-button system (1.5px `--ph-herb` line, white, 10px corners, bold, `padding: 2px 9px`, tint on hover), each at its own font-size and in its own place. The list repeats each original selector so it wins by order alone. **A new tap target never gets `text-decoration: underline`; add its selector to that list, or give it an existing chip class.** Media-scoped ones were changed in place: the Photos section's actions on a phone (`.ph-pres-panel .ph-tmpl-sec-act`, <=480px) and the photo-delete toast's Undo (`.ph-ren-undo-btn`, a chip in the toast's own light-teal-on-ink colours).
- **Gone:** every `text-decoration: underline` on a text link (46 rules de-underlined, 23 hover-only rules that said nothing else deleted), and the hover underline on `.ph-name-link`, `.tab-link`, `.ph-os-lnk`, `.ph-ck-lnk`, `.ph-shell-link`, `.ph-dose-link`, `.ph-mc-lnk`, `.ph-tp-book` and the other quiet links (where the hover also set a colour, the colour stays; where it only underlined, the hover now does nothing but the pointer). Words that are not tap targets lost just the underline: `.ph-hx-action` (the action word inside a Medical history row button), `.ph-ccrib-since` (how long, in the complaint ribbon), `.ph-acu-herbchange` ("change" inside the instruct chip), `.ph-apptpop-plan b`.
- **Kept on purpose:** the three selected-state underlines (`#phInvChips .ph-load-btn.on`, `.ph-st-tab-btn.on`, `.ph-tp-pbar .seg.on`) are markers, not links; `.tab-link`'s plain base look never had an underline (73 uses, unchanged); the question sets' Skip is already a round `.ph-pfw-opt` pill, so `.ph-pfw-skip` only lost its dead underline.
- **Arrows:** the trailing › / → came off 77 button and chip labels ("Open profile", "See all N", "To order", "Compare these 2", "Open Patterns", "All scans", "Edit on Cycle", "Make", "Write message", "Finish review", "Back up now", "Settings", the Patterns clue rows' chevron, ...). **Still carry one, by design:** the flow-wide "Next: ..." buttons (`ph-pres-stage-next`, `ph-pfw-next`, the refill "Next:" buttons, the IVF questionnaire's "Next ›"), the earlier / later pair (`‹ Earlier visit` / `Later visit ›`), arrow-only buttons (`ph-wkt-navbtn`, `ph-ren-view-arrow`, `ph-cyc-ib`, the day / month ‹ › and the fold chevrons `ph-tp-pc-chev`, `ph-tl-foldchev`, `.go`), and data arrows ("110 g → 120 g", "Merge → <name>", "→ <type>" on the Ren tag, "old → new" moves).
- Checked: the file boots headless with no console message beyond the known baseline on six pages; a computed-style scan of the rendered Dashboard, Refill, Fang Bing Ren, Care Plan, Treatment, Cycle, Today's visit and Dispense pages finds no visible underlined text and no button label ending in › / → outside the kept set; before / after screenshots of the 25 harness pages at 1280 and 375 px (see the commit). NOT exercised: a real patient, her phone, a real hover.

## Phone rules in ONE block (audit step 6, 2026-10-15; LIVE in build 20261015-900000)
The phone laws say the ≤640px CSS lives in the PHONE REDESIGN block; the file had 86 more plain `@media (max-width: 640px)` blocks beside their desktop rules. Now **85 of them sit at the top of the PHONE REDESIGN block's own 640px @media** (the "folded here 2026-10-15" comment), in the order they had, each under a `/* from line N */` marker (N = its line in the commit before this one, 782ee0c) with the comment that stood above it; the redesign's own rules follow and still win ties. Left in place on purpose: the `.ph-pat-hero` block (the 480px rule right under it must stay later) and, inside the section itself, the small refill-titlebar 640px block that pairs with its 641-900px twin (never a candidate: it already sits after the fold target).
- **Rule from now on: a new phone rule goes INSIDE that block (after the folded part is fine), never in a new `@media (max-width: 640px)` of its own.** Order inside the block is source order, so a rule meant to beat an earlier one goes below it.
- **Source-order trap, the reason the fold needed care:** moving a rule later in the sheet makes it beat every equal-specificity rule it used to lose to. Ten rules therefore lost declarations a later plain / ≤900px / ≤700px rule already beat (dropped, not carried): `#presSearch` phone font-size + padding, `#refSearch` padding, the two `.ph-widget` phone lines, `.ph-shell-titlebar` padding + gap, `.ph-cap-tchip`, `.ph-shell-cell`, `.ph-flag` in the density-notch selector list, and the Visit record heading's `gap: 6px` (`.ph-tp-vr-sec`; the later plain `.ph-tp-sec` 12px wins). Three plain rules a moved rule now outranks are **restated right after it** inside the fold (each marked "fold 2026-10-15"): `.ph-hx-card-inline` (width/max-height/radius, after the full-screen `.ph-hx-card` rule), `.ph-herbform { padding: 0 }` (after the `.add-form` phone rule) and `.ph-days-opt { min-height: 26px }` (after the 40px row rule).
- **Two kinds of trap the static scan cannot see.** (1) **An `!important` on the phone side is no protection when the later rule is `!important` too: then order decides** (`.ph-flag`, caught by the screenshots: Inventory status flags and Visit record filter chips grew to 32px). (2) **Different selector text at equal specificity on the same element** (`.ph-tp-vr-sec` vs `.ph-tp-sec`, `.ph-hx-card` vs `.ph-hx-card-inline`, `.add-form` vs `.ph-herbform`, the shared `.ph-days-opt` row rule vs its plain rule): invisible to a same-selector scan AND mostly to screenshots (hidden drawer, equal rendered height). Found by a computed-style fingerprint of every element (70 properties) on the 25 harness pages at 375px, unfolded against folded. Any future move of CSS across the sheet gets that fingerprint check, not screenshots alone.
- The `.ph-tbm-ic` transition / `:active` transform rules got twins in the LAST `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)` block (the earlier reduced-motion block no longer sits after them). Flagged, unchanged because nothing differs either way: `#phSearch`, `.ph-shell-n` (`!important` on the phone side only), `.ph-tp-fpick-input` and `#presIngTable .del` (same value both sides).
- **Checked:** the family-aware static scan reads 0 clashes after the fold; Chrome parses the folded sheet to exactly 2 fewer style rules and 85 fewer media rules (nothing lost to a parse error); headless boot clean on six pages; the 25-page fingerprint at 375px (identical apart from two DOM-level timing differences); the 25 harness pages at 1280 and 375 px against the step-5 set (43 of 50 identical, the rest clock / now-line / sub-pixel jitter). **Coverage:** 129 of the 484 moved selectors match an element on those pages at rest; the other 355 (appointment / IVF / pregnancy dialogs, stocktake, schedule rows, guide pages, prep + weigh rows, price review, merge, relocation, letters, cycle history) rest on the static scans only: same-selector (0 clashes) + different-selector (`fold-check2.pl`: equal specificity, same property, different value, classes that sit together in the markup, same feature family; 14 candidate pairs on 5 selectors left, each checked against the markup and judged two features that never nest). NOT exercised: a real patient, her phone, reduced motion on a real device, those 355 selectors' states in a browser. Scripts in the 2026-10-15 scratchpad: `fold640.pl`, `fold-check.pl`, `fold-check2.pl`, `fp-wrap.html` + `fp-run.sh` + `fp-diff.pl`, `cov-gen.pl` + `cov-run.sh`.

## Medical history: no jump on a tap, fixed columns, "Allergies only" scope (her 2026-10-15 "when i select things, the page jumps. prevent that. also include option to record allergies only"; LIVE in build 20261015-910000: commits 4a3831d + cd7befc, pushed on her "push live")
`phHxGroupLabel(rec, g)` is the one place the Medication & allergies group is renamed "Allergies" in the Allergies-only scope (card heading, band pips, band "next"); any new place that prints a history group name must read it.
Scope = the inline card (`phHxScreenInnerHtml` in `#presMedHxHost`, the only live path; the `#phHxModal` popup is never shown any more and its code is untouched). Measured first; three causes, all fixed:
- **The Yes handler focused the wrong note.** It took the first `[data-hx-note]` in the page = the flags strip's `.ph-hx-flag-note` at the top of the card, and `focus()` scrolled `#presPanel` to 0. Now it focuses the tapped row's own note (`#presMedHxHost [data-hx-row="k"] [data-hx-note]`, `preventScroll`, caret at the end) and `phHxRevealNearest` scrolls only when the box is off screen. The ghost fly is unchanged.
- **Every rebuild moved the rows.** `renderHxScreen` rebuilds the host with innerHTML on every tap (Yes, clear, preset, Asked all, sex, scope) and the flags strip above the body grows a line when a flag is added. Now `phHxAnchorTake(host)` measures the tapped row (`presHxAnchorKey`, set by the Yes / clear handler), else the card head when it is on screen, else the first visible row, BEFORE the rebuild, and `phHxAnchorKeep` puts it back after (`phHxScrollerOf` finds the scroller; its `overflow-anchor` is `none` meanwhile, because Chrome's own anchoring adjusted on top of the compensation on clear taps). A shrink at the bottom of the page would clamp the scroller, so the keep adds `min-height` to the host for the shortfall (`phHxHold`, blank room under the card) and `phHxHoldListen` releases it from a passive scroll listener once the room is wholly below the viewport. Any new rebuild path of this card must go through `renderHxScreen` (or call take / keep itself).
- **The desktop columns were CSS multicol**, which re-balances on every repaint: a note box opening moved a whole group to another column. `phHxColumnsHtml(groupHtml)` now wraps the groups in three `.ph-hx-col` wrappers, cut where the row counts (rows + 1 per group) come out most even (3 groups or fewer = one per column); `.ph-hx-body.ph-hx-cols` is flex at 901px and up, the wrappers are `display: contents` below (one column on a phone, as before). The first-group 8px margin is scoped `.ph-hx-col:first-child > .ph-hx-group:not(.rel):first-child` (a bare `:first-child` hit the first group of EVERY wrapper, on the phone too, and beat `.rel`'s margin 0). Reproduces the old balance exactly on the harness patient.
- **Scope pills** `Full history | Allergies only | Not needed` (`.ph-hx-seg.ph-hx-scope`, `data-hx-scope`, `phHxScope(rec)`) replace the "Not needed for this patient" checkbox (the `[data-hx-notneeded]` handler and the `.ph-hx-notneeded*` CSS are gone). `rec.historyScope = "allergies"` (absent = full) sits beside `rec.historyNotNeeded` (= "none"); `PH_HX_ALLERGY_KEYS` = known_drug_allergy, known_food_allergy, allergic_to_herbs; **`phHxApplicableItems(rec)` is the ONE list every count reads** (progress "n of 3", rail tick, band "allergies only · 2 still to ask", `presMedHxSubLabel` "… · allergies only", flags strip, `phHxMarkRestNo`); the group heading reads "Allergies"; `phHxPrioNoteHtml` returns nothing; `historyScope` is in the patient merge list. A new history count must read `phHxApplicableItems`, never `PH_HX_ALL_ITEMS`.
- **Disclosed / DRAFT:** "record allergies only" was read as a scope beside Not needed (not a page of its own); the pill labels are Claude-drafted; the pills wrap to a second line of the card head; the hold leaves blank room under the card until she scrolls up; the "No Start over / bulk-reset" rule is untouched.
- Tested in the Browser pane with the harness patient (1280 and 375): every tap holds its row at 0px (Yes, clear, bottom clear, Asked all, preset), focus on the row's own note, columns never change, scope switches hold the head, phone margins identical to before, no sideways scroll; Allergies only = 3 rows / rail "2 left" / band 1 of 3; Not needed = "marked not needed" + tick. NOT exercised: a real patient, her phone, a real scroll gesture (the hidden pane fires no scroll events; the hold release was checked with a synthetic one). Patch script `hxfix.pl` in the 2026-10-15 scratchpad.

Grep-confirmed clean: no leftover `ph-cc-hl`/`ph-cc-dt`/`ph-cc-q\b`/`ph-cc-quick`/`ph-cc-orx`/`ph-cc3-done`/`ph-cc-addrow`/`ph-cc-moreinfo` anywhere in the file.

## Dosage & Price: Grams-row overlap, Price-chip height (her 2026-10-15 screenshot of a Dispense panel, "fix overlapping text, keep text terse, make chips font text consistent"; LIVE, PUSHED inside build 20261015-920000, main 9544b00 — the two hunks rode along inside a concurrent session's `c7ea23e2f0` and its later "push live")
Screenshot: patient Lua Barry, eczema plan ("Lung/Stomach heat-clearing — Pi Pa Qing Fei Yin base ..."), Grams 172 g × 2 — the "Lasts 19.1 days · empty ..." text ran behind the ×1/×2/×3/✎ grams-multiplier pills on desktop fit-mode. Two separate causes, both measured and fixed:
- **Grams row (desktop fit only, `.ph-dsp-fit`, window > 900px):** that mode's own rule gives every `.ph-row .ph-v > *` `min-width: 0`, which let `#presGramsSegWrap` (a plain `<span>`, not a flex container, so it carries no `overflow` rule of its own) shrink its box below its pills' real width; the pills themselves never shrank and bled past the box into `#presDoseDurationWrap` beside it — invisible to a `getBoundingClientRect()` check on the span, since that reports the shrunk LAID-OUT box, not the overflowing rendered pills. Fix: `#pharmacyPage .ph-pres-panel.ph-dsp-fit .ph-pres-dose #presGramsSegWrap { flex: none; white-space: nowrap; }` right after the existing `min-width: 0` rule — the pills keep their full width, the duration text (already `max-width` + ellipsis) gets the honest remainder.
- **Price-row chip height (phone, ≤640px):** the phone rule `#pharmacyPage .ph-days-opt { min-height: 40px; height: 40px; }` (shared by Supply/Taken/Grams chips) never listed `.ph-pm-opt` (the Price row's preset $ / gram $ / markup × chips), so Price stayed 26px while every other chip on the sheet grew to 40px — a size/shape mismatch, not a font one (both families already share `--fs-body`). Fix: added `.ph-pm-opt` to that selector, plus a companion rule pinning the embedded rate text-input to `26px` so it sits inset inside the now-taller 40px pill rather than stretching to fill it.
- Verified in the Browser pane with a synthetic script matching her screenshot (eczema plan, 172 g, ×2): at w=901 (the tightest fit-mode width) and w=1280, `#presGramsSegWrap` vs `#presDoseDurationWrap` no longer overlap and a full sweep of every `.ph-pres-dose .ph-row .ph-v` pair on the sheet finds zero overlaps; at w=390 the three Price chips render 58×40 / 112×40 / 64×40 (same height, same top, no overlap) matching Supply/Taken/Grams, and the visible rate input measures 56×26 inside its 112×40 pill. Headless-Edge screenshots confirm both visually (`dsp-901-fixed.png`, `dsp-390-fixed.png` in the 2026-10-15 scratchpad). The Objective-row/"Use"-button area the screenshot also seemed to show tight was checked at 390/460/901/1280 and never overlaps — read as normal wrapped spacing, not a bug; flag to her if she still sees it.
- **Trap hit again:** a concurrent session's `c7ea23e2f0` ("Came in for: chips not links") swept these two uncommitted hunks into its own commit along with its own work (the same `git commit -- <paths>` full-working-tree trap the Fang Bing Ren sleek-card build hit on 2026-10-13). Nothing lost — both hunks are intact inside that commit — but they carry no commit message of their own; this note is their record.

## Dispense: Log silently discarded a manual Grams figure (her 2026-10-15 "i dispense 123 and then when i press log it logs 82"; commit 4a146e3 — DATA-INTEGRITY BUG, fixed; LIVE on main (confirmed ancestor of origin/main as of the 2026-10-18 audit — the early push this note recommended has happened)
Screenshots of her real Dispense panel on a live patient (Xiao Yao San, 8 herbs totalling 82 g): she typed a manual **123 g** into Grams (✎ mode, "set by you · herb total is 82 g"), the Dispense step deducted stock and printed the label at 123 g, then she pressed **Log** — and both the Grams box and the permanent Visit history entry came back **82 g**, not 123 g. The record of what was actually measured out and already deducted no longer matched what got logged.
- **Root cause**, traced in `presCommitTemplateFields` (the function the Save/Log button calls first, before it reads `#presGrams` and writes the log entry): it re-parses the `#presEditIngs` textarea into `ings` on EVERY call and, whenever `ings` is non-empty, unconditionally ran `t.ingredients = ings` followed by `presGramsFollowHerbs(t)` for a patient script — even when the parsed list was byte-for-byte identical to what was already stored. `presGramsFollowHerbs` has no concept of "nothing changed": seeing `gramsMode === "manual"`, it still flips the mode to `"multiple"` and recomputes `totalGrams = herbTotal × multiple` (multiple defaulting to 1), then calls `presRefreshGramsFieldIfAttached(t)`, which overwrites the live `#presGrams` DOM value — all BEFORE the same click handler reads that field to build the log entry. So pressing Log, by itself, with nothing in the ingredient list touched, silently counted as "an ingredient edit" for the one rule (her 2026-09-21 "always auto-update" spec) that was only ever meant to fire on a real one — exactly the gap her 2026-08-25 §3 freeze had existed to close, re-opened by this path never checking for an actual change.
- **Fix:** `presCommitTemplateFields` now compares the freshly-parsed `ings` against the stored `t.ingredients` (same length, same herb name and grams at every index) and only calls `presGramsFollowHerbs(t)` when they genuinely differ. A formula record is untouched (its branch always recomputes — correct, it has no manual-override concept). `t.ingredients = ings` itself still runs every time (harmless when unchanged).
- Verified in the Browser pane with a synthetic script matching her screenshot exactly (8 herbs, 82 g herb total, manual 123 g, already "dispensed"): pressing Log now keeps Grams at 123 and logs `123` (was silently logging `82`). Regression-checked on the same script: a REAL ingredient edit (+10 g on one herb) still correctly re-derives Grams to the new herb total (82 → 92), so the "always auto-update" behaviour for genuine edits is unchanged. No console errors either run.
- **Not yet checked:** whether any OTHER caller of `presCommitTemplateFields` (the debounced template autosave while she's mid-edit, the formula recipe Save button) could have hit the same false-positive before this fix — those all pass through the same gated branch now, so they get the same protection, but none were individually re-verified beyond the two cases above.
- This is a live financial/clinical data-integrity bug (wrong grams on the permanent dispense record, after stock was already deducted at the correct amount) — worth an earlier "push live" than the routine batch, her call.

## Dispense: the OTHER half — an ingredient edit after typing a manual Grams figure (her 2026-10-15 "i can't dispense just 123g. when i calculate, it dispenses 164g ... and it saves as 164g dispensed"; commit c882641 — same data-integrity class as the note above, built on top of it; LIVE on main (confirmed ancestor of origin/main as of the 2026-10-18 audit)
Her screenshot (Acne — Acute active-breakouts formula, 82 g herb total): she typed a manual **123 g**, then (unlike the "press Log with nothing touched" case above) a REAL ingredient-table interaction followed — and Grams silently became **164 g** (= 82 × 2), not a number derived from 123 at all.
- **Root cause**, a second gap in the same two functions the note above fixes: `presGramsFollowHerbs`'s fallback for a non-"multiple" state is `t.gramsMultiple || seedMultiple || 1` — but the `#presGrams` input handler (where typing sets `gramsMode = "manual"`) never touched `t.gramsMultiple` at all. Whatever ×1/×2/×3 pill she'd tapped earlier (or last left on a different script before a Data-wide default) stayed sitting in `t.gramsMultiple`, stale and unrelated to the number she'd just typed. The moment a REAL ingredient edit ran `presGramsFollowHerbs` (now correctly gated to real edits only, by the fix above), it recomputed `herbTotal × that stale multiplier` — 82 g × a leftover 2 = 164 g — discarding her 123 g. The same function also unconditionally relabelled the state `"multiple"` on that edit, so the hint stopped reading "✎ set by you" and the ×1/×2/×3/✎ pills showed none of them picked (an arbitrary `×3.7273` ratio nobody chose).
- **Fix, two parts:**
  1. The `#presGrams` input handler now also recomputes `t.gramsMultiple = typedTotal / herbTotalAtThatMoment` the instant she types, so the fallback the other function reads is always an accurate reflection of her own figure, not a stray leftover.
  2. `presGramsFollowHerbs` keeps describing a script she has explicitly gone manual on (`t.gramsMode === "manual"`, the string this handler sets) as `"manual"` after an edit, instead of promoting it to `"multiple"` — it still resyncs the NUMBER on every edit (her unchanged 2026-09-21 "always auto-follow, never freeze" rule), it just doesn't relabel a number nobody picked as if she'd tapped a ×N pill. A script whose `gramsMode` was never explicitly set either way (`undefined` — the pre-this-feature default) still joins ordinary ×1 multiple mode on its first edit, exactly as before this fix.
- Verified in a sandbox harness (synthetic 33 g-herb-total script, real DOM `input` + click events): typing 123 g then a −1 g ingredient edit now scales to 119.27 g (not a stale-multiplier jump to an unrelated number) and stays described as "✎ set by you · herb total is 32 g", ✎ pill still showing picked; a second edit that cancels out returns to exactly 123 g; a legacy/untouched script still promotes to ×1 multiple mode on its first edit (unchanged); an explicit ×2 pick is unaffected by either fix.
- Same data-integrity class as the note above (wrong grams on the permanent record after stock is already deducted) — same recommendation to push earlier than the routine batch, her call. The two fixes are independent but complementary: the note above stops Log/Save from mis-firing when nothing changed; this one makes the recompute correct on the real edits that still legitimately trigger it.

- **Open by default:** `phQaOpen` (patient keys with the panel open) is now **`phQaClosed`** (patient keys who have explicitly collapsed it) — `open = !phQaClosed.has(pk)`; the toggle handler's add/delete logic is unchanged, just renamed. A patient nobody has touched opens expanded.
- **Bold closed header:** `phQaHtml`'s `rec`/`S`/`order`/`lead`/`clear` computation now sits ABOVE the `if (!open)` branch, so the shut card can show a live subtitle even while collapsed: "Nothing answered yet", "`<Family>` `<lead%>` likely so far", or "Clear lead: `<Family>`" once one crosses 85%. Markup `.ph-qa-card.shut > .ph-qa-shuthd` (solid `var(--ph-herb-deep)` bar, white text, sprite `#i-bolt` in a translucent circle, bold "Quick assess" + the subtitle, a trailing ▸) replaces the old pale 36px strip.
- **Ticked chips (`phFbrRowHtml`):** the row carries `style="--rc:${c.bg};--rfg:${c.fg}"` (the same family colour its mosaic tile wears). `.on` fills the whole row solid in `--rc` with white/`--rfg` text, a translucent `--rfg` pill for the `+N` score, and a new `<i class="ph-fbr-ck">` check badge (sprite `#i-check`, matching the app's existing `<svg><use href="#i-check"/></svg>` convention — never emoji, never a plain unicode "✓"). `.on:not(.auto)` = solid white badge with a `--rc`-coloured check (hand-tapped). `.on.auto` = the same badge hollowed to a ring (`box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 2px var(--rfg)`, transparent fill) — "already known" reads visibly different from a tap with no second glance needed. `.no` rows fill solid `var(--ph-line)` (not just faded text) with a bigger (20px) filled ✕ badge. `.warn` (a negative-weight caution) keeps its own existing red-tint treatment, which still wins over `.on`'s fill (same specificity, later in the stylesheet, unchanged precedent). The thin `.ph-fbr-bar` colour strip is untouched on rows with no answer.
- Sandbox-tested against a synthetic patient through the real `phTriHtml`/`phFbrRowHtml`/`phQaHtml` (not a real patient, not her phone): Quick assess renders directly under the verdict and opens expanded on a fresh patient; collapsing it shows the bold bar with a live "Fu Zi 13% likely so far" subtitle; a hand-tapped Yes (`cold_extrem`) shows the solid white check on its family's own fill colour; an auto-ticked sign (`sex_female`, prefilled from Patient details) shows the hollow-ring check on the same fill; a ruled-out sign (`anxiety_tend`) shows the bolder solid-grey chip + bigger ✕; 375px width has zero overflow on every element this build touches (a pre-existing, unrelated overflow on the Energy card's `.ph-en-chips` row was found and left alone — not part of this change); no console errors. NOT exercised: a real patient, her phone, a real tap (only `phTriWrite` calls), reduced motion on a real device.

## No-plan gate: "Came in for" dropdown unreadable over the plan-type cards (her 2026-10-15 screenshot + "can't see text"; committed on `session-a` — LIVE on main (audited 2026-10-18: origin/main has since advanced past this build))
Her screenshot (a brand-new patient's no-plan gate): the "Came in for" complaint-typeahead dropdown (9 rows: Periods & cycle, Fertility / IVF, Menopause, Pain, Stress & sleep, Weight & energy, Face / nerve, Skin, Cold flu immune) geometrically overlaps the plan-type picker below it (Blank plan + the colour-coded condition template cards). Rows that don't overlap a card (Periods & cycle, Fertility / IVF) were fully legible; rows that do (Menopause onward) lost their white background and showed the card's own text bleeding through underneath — not a clipping/overflow bug, a paint-order bug.
- **Root cause, proved with `getComputedStyle` + `document.elementFromPoint`, not guessed:** `.ph-cc2.ph-cc3.fresh` (the Came in for card; `.fresh` applies only on a brand-new patient, matching her screenshot) carries `animation: phPfwRise .35s ease both` (line ~15163). Under `animation-fill-mode: both`, the animation's end state `transform: none` computes to `matrix(1,0,0,1,0,0)` — NOT the keyword `none` — so Chrome permanently promotes the card to its own stacking context even after the animation finishes and even though it visually does nothing. The card itself is `position: static` (no `z-index` was set, and z-index on a static element has no effect per spec), so its ENTIRE painted content — including the dropdown's own `z-index: 5` — is trapped inside that local stacking context. The later DOM sibling `#phTpComboHost` (the plan-template picker, also static/auto z-index) is OUTSIDE that context, so it wins by plain later-DOM-order paint rules wherever the two geometrically overlap, regardless of the dropdown's z-index.
- **Fix:** gave `.ph-cc2`'s base rule (line 15163) `position: relative; z-index: 2;` so the whole card reliably wins its own stacking context against later static siblings — 2 stays safely below the sticky `.ph-pres-band`'s `z-index: 3` (line 9605, same ancestor stacking context via `#presPanel.ph-pres-full`). Checked the `.ph-pf-onebox .ph-cc2` override (lines 15129-15130, the Patient-details one-box card) — it resets border/background/animation/box-shadow but not position, so the new rule carries through there untouched, no second fix needed.
- Verified in the sandbox with a synthetic "Gate Sandbox Mock" patient (`PHARMACY.patients`, real `presNoPlanGateHtml(t, name)` render, real `phCcMenuPaint(input)` to generate the live dropdown — not a hand-typed copy): `document.elementFromPoint()` on every one of the 9 rows, including the 5 that geometrically overlap `#phTpComboHost`, now resolves to the dropdown row itself (was resolving to the card underneath before the fix); confirmed at both desktop and 375px phone width; screenshot matches — solid white dropdown fully on top, "Neck & shoulder / IVF Protocol / Insomnia" cards cleanly underneath with no text bleed-through.
- **Traps & gotchas addition:** `animation-fill-mode: both/forwards` with a `transform: none` end-state is NOT the keyword `none` once the animation has resolved it — it permanently creates a stacking context on an otherwise `position: static` element, trapping any z-index set on its descendants. Proving this needs `getComputedStyle`/`document.elementFromPoint`, not a visual guess (a plain overflow/clipping read would have missed it, since nothing is actually clipped).
- NOT exercised: a real patient, her phone, a real tap-driven typeahead session (only `phCcMenuPaint` was called directly). Not yet checked for the same trap on any OTHER `.fresh`-animated card with an absolutely-positioned dropdown child elsewhere in the app — a targeted sweep, not done.

## Fang Bing Ren reference sheet, since-dropdown, and dated pain scores (her 2026-10-15, four asks in one message; committed on `session-a` inside `d9b82bb` — LIVE on main (confirmed ancestor of origin/main as of the 2026-10-18 audit))
Her pasted message, with three screenshots (a Fang Bing Ren card, the Musculoskeletal Pain table, a zoomed "How long" pill row): "is thre a way to see all herb families and criteria, and formula criteria? / link pain chart with the what came in for so they are integrated / for the time options, change it to dropdown to save space and include custom input e.g. its missing 2 weeks, or 5 weeks. / give option to give rating to symptom from a date e.g. today its 5/10 but last week it was 3/10". Her 2nd and 4th asks were genuinely ambiguous (several plausible readings) — built on a disclosed, reasonable interpretation rather than stopping to ask, per her "disclosed judgment calls get completed" standing preference; told her plainly so she can correct either reading.
- **Ask 1 — "see all herb families and criteria":** a new read-only **"All formulas & criteria" sheet** (`#phFbrAllSheet`, cloned from the app's existing `.ph-lk-sheet`/`.ph-lk-card` overlay shell), reached from the Fang Bing Ren ⋯ menu (`data-far-open`). Lists all 28 families, grouped by herb (`PH_REN_FAM_GROUPS`), each a `<details>` card showing its Bing (now) and Ren (build) sign tables sorted strongest-first, with a live search box (`data-far-q`) that filters to matching signs/formula names and auto-opens the matching cards. `phFbrAllCardHtml`/`phFbrAllGroupsHtml`/`phFbrAllRefHtml`/`phFbrAllRefOpen`/`-Close`/`-Render`. Read-only — taps nothing, writes nothing.
- **Ask 3 — "How long" dropdown:** the Pain table's old 7-pill quick-pick row under a focused since-box is now one `<select>` (`PH_SINCE_OPTS`, 11 options: 1/2/3 wk, 1 mo, 6 wk, 2/3/6 mo, 1/2/5 yr — closes her named gap, "missing 2 weeks or 5 weeks" — plus a trailing "Custom — type it above" that refocuses the free-text box). Picking a value writes `p.since` straight away via `phCcAgo`; `data-pm-qsel` change handler. **Scoped to the Pain table only** (disclosed call: "Came in for"'s own since-picker already serves a different, wider context and the space pressure that motivated this didn't equally apply there — left untouched).
- **Ask 2 + 4 — a dated score, and linking the pain score into "Came in for":**
  - `phMskWrite(name, fn, planId, day)` gained a 4th, optional, backward-compatible `day` parameter (`const d = day || phMskDay();`) — only 6 call sites in the whole file, 5 of them untouched.
  - The Pain table's Now/Best/Worst 0-10 strip (opened by tapping a score box) now carries a `<input type="date">` (`data-pm-scdate`, capped at today) alongside the number pills. A new `phPmScoreDay` map (key = the same `"planId|idx|k"`/`"planId|all|k"` string `phPmScoreOpen` already uses) holds which date that open strip is reading/writing; it resets to today every time a strip opens or closes. `scoreAt(which, k, day)` (inside `phPmTableHtml`) is the one read for "what's on file for THIS date", so the strip's own number-pills reflect whichever day the date box is set to, not always today.
  - `phPmScoreSet` reads the day from `phPmScoreDay`, passes it through to `phMskWrite`. **Her own live "now" severity on the plan's symptom row only follows a score set for TODAY** — a backdated entry corrects history, not her current state, so `phMskPainRow(...).severity` is left alone when `day !== phMskDay()`.
  - Tapping a number pill no longer auto-closes the strip (it used to): it stays open so she can change the date and log another day's figure in the same sitting — "today 5/10, last week 3/10" in two taps, not two separate open/close cycles.
  - **The "integration" half of ask 2** (read-only, nothing written): `phCcAuto`'s Pain branch now appends today's Musculoskeletal "All pains" Now score when one is on file — "Pain: shoulder (both sides) · aching, tension · now 5/10" — read straight from the same `rec.msk.visits` the pain table itself writes (the app's "one source, shown everywhere" rule), not a second score store. A backdated entry never shows here (by design — this line is "where things stand right now", not a history log); the existing "last N" indicator beside today's box already surfaces the single most recent PRIOR score, dated or not, which is as far as this build goes toward a fuller trend view.
- **Disclosed interpretation, ask 2:** read as "surface the pain's current score on the complaint card", not as a second, separate severity-trend chart — the app already has one dated 0-10/% tracker (the Complaint compass, `rec.ccTrack`, built 2026-10-14) and building a SECOND one for MSK pain specifically risked exactly the kind of parallel-mechanism duplication the app's own standing rules warn against. If she wants a fuller "see every dated score for this pain" view, that's a clean follow-up (the data is already there, day-keyed).
- **Disclosed interpretation, ask 4:** read as "the MSK Pain table's Now/Best/Worst scores", the strongest match to her screenshot context — not the separate Complaint compass (which also only writes today currently, untouched by this build).
- Verified in the Browser pane (synthetic patient, a dummy formula-less script so the real MSK page could be opened the way the app opens it, real click/focus/change events dispatched through the real document-level delegated handlers, not direct function pokes): the "All formulas" sheet opens with 28 cards, search narrows to matching cards and reopens them; the since-dropdown appears on focusing a pain's date box and a "3 wk" pick writes the right date; a real score tap opens the strip defaulting to today; changing the date to 8 days ago and tapping "3" writes `rec.msk.visits["<8 days ago>"].pains` without touching today's entry or today's live severity; the strip stays open and reflects "3" as picked for that date, flips back to showing "5" when the date box is set back to today; the most-recent-prior-visit read correctly surfaces the backdated entry; `phCcAuto` reports "now 5/10". No console errors. **Testing note for next time:** `phPmScoreSet`'s number pills TOGGLE (tapping the same value twice clears it) — a second test run against the same patient name/day with the same score value will silently flip it back off; use a fresh name (or a fresh value) per run, not a symptom of a real bug (confirmed via an isolated single-write harness that the underlying write is perfectly stable; the flakiness was purely the test re-running against its own leftover localStorage state).
- **Trap hit again (same pattern noted on 2026-10-13 and 2026-10-15 above):** all of this rode into main's sibling session's commit `d9b82bb` ("No-plan gate: fix Came in for dropdown painting under the plan-type cards") via the same `git commit -- <paths>` full-working-tree sweep — confirmed intact there (`git show d9b82bb -- index.html | grep phPmScoreDay` etc.) — since confirmed pushed: `d9b82bb` is an ancestor of `origin/main` as of the 2026-10-18 audit.

## Find the Ren: 13 compound chips split into their two halves (her 2026-10-15 "some of these chips need to be split into 2", committed on `session-a` — LIVE on main (audited 2026-10-18: origin/main has since advanced past this build))
Her screenshot `50.png`: the Find the Ren card's Build/Face/Skin/Lips/Eyes/Muscles LOOK rows, each showing a sign chip whose label bundled two separate observations with "or"/",". Visual review widget + 4 `AskUserQuestion`s, all her answers the Recommended option: build all of them, Lips' two compound signs share one new "Lips: dark" sign, Muscles' two compound signs share a "tight" half + reuse "spasms" (**corrected below — that reuse target does not exist on this card**), build the splits at the base Ren-sign-pool level so every surface that reads `PH_REN_SIGNS` (this card, the Fang Bing Ren board, the Ren tab, Quick assess, Ask her) gets them, not just this card.
- **Correction disclosed to her before implementing:** the "tight or spasming" sign I asked her about for Muscles (question 3) is `tight`, which lives in `PH_BING_SIGNS` only (Ban Xia Xie Xin Tang's sleep/mood list, 2026-10-13) — it is not a Ren sign and never shows on this Ren-only card. The real Muscles compound signs are `heavy_sore_muscle_tend` and `muscle_hard_tend`, both Ren-only with no Bing-only "tight" sign to share. Her Muscles split needed no sharing or reuse after all — a clean two-way split of each.
- **The 13 compound signs and their halves** (`PH_REN_SIGNS`, right after `lowback_abd_heavy_cold_tend`): `robust_build` **kept its key** (now "Build: robust" only) + new `build_heavyset_tend` ("Build: slightly heavy"); `complexion_sallow` **kept its key** ("Complexion: yellow, sallow") + new `complexion_dark_slight_tend` ("Complexion: slightly dark") — both relabelled, not retired, since one half of each original compound sign already matched the key's own meaning closely enough that renaming the other sign everywhere it is used (the BMI function `phBodySigns`, the body-archetype painter, the "Thin vs robust build" link) would have been pure churn for no behaviour change. Every other compound sign is fully **retired** and replaced by two fresh keys, reusing an existing pool sign for one half wherever the fact already existed (precedent: her many prior pulse-sign splits): `build_top_heavy_thin_legs` → `build_upper_heavy_tend` + `build_thin_legs_tend`; `build_slim_fair` → `build_slim_tend` + new shared `skin_fair_tend`; `fine_skin` → `skin_finetext_tend` + `skin_fair_tend` (same shared sign); `complexion_dark_green` → `complexion_dark_yellow_tend` + `complexion_greenish_tend`; `complexion_red_oily` → existing `complexion_red_tend` + new `complexion_oily_tend`; `complexion_pale_lusterless` → `complexion_lusterless_tend` + existing `complexion_pale_tend` (in Dang Gui Shao Yao San only, that formula already scored `complexion_pale_tend` as its own separate line at a different weight, so there the lusterless half stands alone with no `or` group — disclosed in a code comment at that formula); `complexion_dark_lips_tend` → new `complexion_dark_tend` + new shared `lips_dark_tend`; `lips` → existing `lips_pale_tend` + `lips_dark_tend` (shared); `lips_dark_purple` → `lips_dark_tend` (shared) + new `lips_purplish_red_tend`; `lips_red_swollen_tend` → existing `lips_red_tend` + new `lips_swollen_painful_tend`; `skin_oozing_tend` → `skin_weepy_tend` + `skin_blisters_tend`; `vision_blur_tend` → `vision_blurdouble_tend` + `vision_floaters_tend`; `heavy_sore_muscle_tend` → `muscle_sore_tend` + `muscle_heavy_tend`; `muscle_hard_tend` → `muscle_hard_only_tend` + `muscle_tight_tend`.
- **Every formula that weighted an old key now weights both halves, in a new or extended `or.ren` group, so the line still scores exactly once** — her established convention for a one-line-split-into-two-signs ("A or B" pattern used throughout the 28 families already). Touched: Gui Zhi (fine_skin, lips), Chai Hu (complexion_dark_green), Da Huang (complexion_red_oily, lips_dark_purple), Ma Huang (robust_build, heavy_sore_muscle_tend, complexion_sallow, lips_dark_purple, complexion_red_oily), Huang Qi (complexion_pale_lusterless), Huang Lian (complexion_red_oily), Wu Ling San (vision_blur_tend, skin_oozing_tend), Chai Hu Jia Long Gu Mu Li Tang (heavy_sore_muscle_tend), Xiao Yao San (complexion_pale_lusterless), Xue Fu Zhu Yu Tang (complexion_dark_lips_tend, muscle_hard_tend), Fang Ji Huang Qi Tang (heavy_sore_muscle_tend), Huang Qin Tang (build_slim_fair), Shen Qi Wan (complexion_dark_lips_tend — extended its existing 2-way `or` with `complexion_red_tend` to 3-way; build_top_heavy_thin_legs), Dang Gui Shao Yao San (complexion_pale_lusterless — no `or`, see above; complexion_red_oily — simple swap inside its existing minus-weight `or` with `complexion_red_tend`).
- **Everywhere else a retired key was wired**, updated to the new keys: the non-formula body-archetype outline/colour painter (`buildKey`/`faceDef`/`fair`/`lipKey` chain, just after `phBodyBuild`) now reads either half with `||`; `PH_REN_DRAW` body-chart mark rows (heavy sore muscles, blurred-vision/floaters, red-gums-and-lips) list the new keys; `PH_FBR_TWINS_DRAFT` retargeted (`eczema_vesicular`→`skin_blisters_tend`, `lips_red_swollen_now`→`lips_swollen_painful_tend`, `vision_blur_now`→`vision_blurdouble_tend`, `heavy_sore_muscle`→`muscle_sore_tend` — the Bing side stayed one sign, so `muscle_heavy_tend` is left without a Bing twin, disclosed open item); `PH_LINKS_BUILTIN`'s "build"/"muscle"/"complexion" link groups now reference the new keys; `PH_TRI_PREFILL_PM.p` (`muscle_hard_tend`→`muscle_tight_tend`, `heavy_sore_muscle_tend`→`muscle_heavy_tend`, both reading the Pain table's "tension"/"heavy" words); `PH_BING_ALIAS` entries renamed/split to match (`build_top_heavy_thin_legs`→`build_upper_heavy`+`build_thin_legs`, `build_slim_fair`→`build_slim`+`skin_fair`, `complexion_dark_lips`→`complexion_dark`, `lips_red_swollen`→`lips_swollen_painful`); the Ren group listing "Build, skin & complexion" (and "Pain & tension" for the two muscle signs, "Subjective — how they feel" for the two vision signs) list every new key. The Fang Bing Ren body chart (`phFbbPlaceOf`) needed **no changes at all** — it places a sign by its SUBJECT, and every new key keeps the same subject ("Build:"/"Complexion:"/"Skin:"/"Lips:"/"Eyes:"/"Muscles:") as its retired parent, so it is placed automatically.
- **Not touched on purpose:** historical prose comments elsewhere in the file that name a now-retired key in passing (e.g. "Pale/sallow complexion -> complexion_sallow, Anemic appearance -> complexion_pale_lusterless" on Xiao Yao San's own intro comment) — left as a record of what was true when written, since nothing in the code matches a sign by scanning comment text.
- Verified: file boots in the Browser pane with zero console errors; every new/retired key name checked by hand against its pool definition, every formula weight table, every `or` group, the links table, the alias table, the twins table, `PH_TRI_PREFILL_PM`, the two body-chart tables and the three Ren groups it touches — no retired key remains in any live (non-comment) code path, no new key is defined without being used or used without being defined. **Not exercised:** a real patient, a real tap through the UI (the sandbox's synthetic-patient technique needs a signed-in session this pass didn't set up), a sandbox score-and-rank run confirming every touched formula still reaches its old ceiling score, her phone.

## Fang Bing Ren: verdict + Quick assess merged into one compact header, "mock 2" (her 2026-10-15, commit b5af6aa — LIVE on main (confirmed ancestor of origin/main as of the 2026-10-18 audit))
Her screenshot of the "Which family fits" verdict + Quick Assess card, with: "improev this ui, make it less tall and eveyrthing fits on one line. make 2 mocks" -> two mocks sent (`fbr-compact-mocks-2026-10-15.html`: Mock 1 kept the verdict and Quick assess as two separate pieces, one-line each; Mock 2 merged them into one dark header bar with an ultra-compact row style) -> her pick, verbatim: **"mock 2."** Built exactly to it (her "mock = build exactly" rule).
- **The merged header** (`phQaHtml`'s `head()` closure, `.ph-qa2-hd`): dot (the leading family's colour) + name (ellipsis-capped at 150px) + % (gold) + a small progress track + a separator + "Quick assess · N questions" + Undo, one dark bar (`var(--ph-herb-deep)`), open or shut. It replaces BOTH the old standalone `.ph-fbr-verdict` block (used to sit above Quick assess in `phTriHtml`, now gone) and the old plain-strip Quick assess header. `phTriHtml` no longer builds `verdictHtml` at all — `phQaHtml` recomputes `phFbrLeadOf(ranked)` itself (it already received `ranked`) so the two never have to agree through a shared variable.
- **What got dropped to fit one line, and where it went:** the verdict's Bing %/Ren % split, the next-closest family and the Bing/Ren "agree" sentence are no longer printed — they're in the header bar's own `title` (a hover/tap), the same "hidden until hover" move the row-level why-text got. Disclosed to her with the mocks; her "mock 2" pick is an implicit sign-off on this trade (she was shown and picked the version that drops this text).
- **Compact rows** (`.ph-qr` inside `.ph-nq` only — Find the Ren's rows, `.ph-rq .ph-qr`, are a different, untouched layout that happens to share the wrapper class): the Bing/Ren/Bing+Ren tag pill and the always-visible why-sentence are gone, replaced by a thin 3px bar in the leading family's own colour plus a small circular "i" glyph whose `title` carries both (tag wording + the why-sentence, `<b>` tags stripped since the text is already `esc()`-safe and must not be escaped twice — a raw `"` in that text would break the attribute, which is why the one spot that still prints a quoted phrase, "Usually means: …", keeps the ORIGINAL curly quotes “ ” rather than straight ones). The question runs one line with an ellipsis; the answer is Yes/No + "N/S" (was "Not sure", kept as the button's `aria-label` for screen readers); a twin (Bing+Ren) row adds "N"/"U" labels before each Yes/No pair.
- **Scoping, so Find the Ren is untouched:** all new classes (`.ph-qa2-*`, `.ph-qr-bar`, `.ph-qr-info`, `.ph-qr-q`, `.ph-qr-lbl`) are either brand new or scoped under `.ph-nq` (Quick assess's own card class); `.ph-qr-tag`/`.ph-qr-w`/`.ph-qr-t`/`.ph-qr-m` (the old tag-pill/why-line layout) and `.ph-qa-hd`/`.ph-qa-t`/`.ph-qa-shuthd`* were left alone where Find the Ren still reads them, and deleted only where nothing else used them any more (`.ph-qa-shuthd*`, the old `.ph-fbr-verdict`/`.ph-fbr-vtop`/`.ph-fbr-vtrack`/`.ph-fbr-vsub` rules — `.ph-fbr-dot`'s BASE rule stays, it's shared with the body chart's header). `.ph-fbr-layout`'s grid-template-areas lost the `"v"` row (three breakpoints) now that nothing renders into it.
- **Known tightness, disclosed:** at a narrow card width a twin row's question text can lose a character or two to its ellipsis (measured ~5px short at a 407px card in the sandbox) — the twin row's N/U-labelled double Yes/No pair takes real width; single-sign rows don't truncate at the same width. This is the same kind of trade-off already disclosed for the header's long-name truncation, inherent to fitting two Yes/No pairs on one line, not a bug to chase.
- Verified in the sandbox (Browser pane + the `zz-dump-fbr.html` harness, scratch PowerShell HTTP server, real `phPatientRec`/`presOpenScript`/`renderPresPanel` calls and two real clicks on `.ph-qr-seg button[data-qa-ans="yes"]`, not direct function pokes): merged header renders with a real computed verdict (Dang Gui Shao Yao San, 7%, 21%-wide track, title "Bing 5% · Ren 9% · next closest Huang Qi 5% · Bing leans Huang Qi, Ren leans Dang Gui Shao Yao San"), single-sign and twin rows both render and tick/disable correctly after a real click, the answered row fades (`.gone`) while the next slides in, family bars + caution text still work, toggling shut/open preserves the merged header + undo, no console errors. Find the Ren (`presAssessTab = "ren"`) renders its OLD unchanged header/row layout, confirming the scoping held. NOT exercised: a real patient, her phone, the clear-lead card's merged header by eye (only read from source), reduced motion on a real device.

## To Order: a 3-column board, desktop (her 2026-10-15 "improve visual, its requiring too much scrolling down. i think 3 columns better with animated visual interactive elements. design well, make mock before building"; committed on `session-a` — LIVE on main (audited 2026-10-18: origin/main has since advanced past this build))
Her screenshot of the old desktop sheet (stacked "ON THE WAY" / "ARRIVED IN OCTOBER" / "RUNNING LOW, NOT ON THE LIST" bands in one tall scrolling table). Mock `zz-order-mock.html` (toggle between the old long list and the new 3-column board, real sample data, animated jar fills, a working "+ Add" fly-to-"On the way" demo) + a headless-Edge screenshot sent for confirmation, then 8 questions in two popup batches — **all eight of her answers were the Recommended option**: merge Running low into Needs-ordering (same action, one column); fixed-height columns with their own inner scroll (no whole-page scroll); tap-to-expand cards (compact by default: jar, name, level); a fly-to-On-the-way animation on "+ Add"; keep On-the-way's existing supplier grouping (Copy for email / Mark all ordered / All arrived); Arrived shows the last 14 days + a "+N older" reveal; **mobile (≤900px) left completely untouched**; "Yes, build it."
- **DESKTOP ONLY** (`isDesk = window.innerWidth > 900`, unchanged gate): `renderPhRestock()`'s `desktopHtml` is now `${shellHtml}` + one `.ph-osb-board` of three `.ph-osb-col`s — **Needs ordering** (To order, grouped by supplier exactly as before + Running low, merged, flat as before, + the Snoozed strip), **On the way** (grouped by supplier, unchanged bulk actions), **Arrived** (last 14 days, `+N older, not yet settled` reveal via `data-ph-os-arr-older`, never drops anything — her "recent only, + see older" pick). `mobileHtml`'s `osmRow`/`osmBand`/`osmSupGroups` and the whole stacked-card layout are byte-for-byte untouched, per her confirmed answer.
