- **Every existing interaction reuses its exact `data-ph-*` attribute**, just moved from a `<table>` row into a `<div>` card — the global delegated click handler needed ZERO changes for anything that already worked (mark ordered / arrived, the ⋮ menu, step size×qty, unlock bottle $, supplier picker, snooze, copy-for-email, bulk mark/arrived, arrival edit/undo). Only two genuinely NEW interactions got new handler blocks (both already landed in an earlier pass this task, confirmed still in place): `data-ph-os-card-toggle` (tap a card's summary strip to reveal Supplier / Size × qty / Bottle $ / Total — guarded with `!e.target.closest("button, a, input, select")` so a tap on a real control inside still reaches ITS handler first, not the card toggle) and `data-ph-os-arr-older` (reveal older Arrived items). New view-state vars `phOsCardOpen` (a `Set` of open card ids) and `phOsArrOlderOpen` (bool) — view state only, never saved, same idiom as `phOsCollapsed`/`phOsMenu` right beside them.
- **Column 1's gold progress bar is the real `coverPct`** (covered / (covered + lowOpen), the same number the old header stat already computed) — no second, fabricated metric. Columns 2 and 3 get a plain static colour accent strip (blue / green) instead of a bar, since there's no honest live percentage for "on the way" or "arrived" to show — disclosed, not a bug.
- **Preserved a concurrently-added feature mid-build**: while this was in progress, another session landed a "+ Add to order" search box (`phOsAddOpen` state, `osAddHtml`) inline in the page's stat-strip header, above the gold bar. That lives in `shellHtml`, which this build never touches — left completely as-is, confirmed still present and working after this build landed on top of it.
- **Removed, now dead and deleted** (verified block-scoped `const`s inside `renderPhRestock`, zero external references after the rewrite): the old table-specific row/column builders `osRow`, `osSupRow`, `osStage`, `osBand`, `osGap`, `osArrivedRow`, `osLowRow`, `osSnoozedRows`, and the column-drag/resize infrastructure `osColOrder`, `osColDefW`, `osColHtml`, `osColsBody`, `osCols`, `osThMap`, `osColsReset` (there is no resizable `<table>` in a card board, so the whole per-column-drag feature — unique to this one sheet, distinct from every other draggable sheet in the app — goes with it; `phApplyColW`/`phSheetWApply` are still called in the `isDesk` branch for consistency with other sheets, but find nothing to act on now and are harmless no-ops). The CSS for the retired `.ph-osheet*` table/column-drag look was deliberately LEFT IN PLACE (now dead) rather than deleted in the same pass — consistent with her own established precedent of batching CSS dead-code removal into a separate sweep, not mixed into a feature build.
- **New CSS** (`.ph-osb-*`, placed right after the existing "To Order, desktop" block, before the `≤900px` phone-section comment — keeps the phone-law boundary intact): board grid (3 columns ≥1280px, 1 column + the usual inner scroll below that), column head/body/empty-state, supplier sub-header, card summary/expand (`max-height` transition, off under reduced motion), reusing every existing token verbatim (`--ph-herb*`, `--ph-gold-hi`, `--ph-good`, `--ph-blue`, `--ph-low*`, `--ph-koda`/`--ph-gmp`) — no new colour invented, `--ph-gold` never used as a status colour.
- Verified against the real app (Browser pane, scratch server, real render — not a direct function poke), in two passes. First pass, the data as it stood (21 real running-low herbs, nothing in the other stages): 3 columns render, a card's tap-to-expand opens/closes (`.open` + `.ph-osb-expin`), the concurrently-added "+ Add to order" button intact, zero new console errors. Second pass, after stamping real herbs into every stage (`order`/`ordered`/`arrivedDate` set directly on `PHARMACY.herbs` through `window.eval`, then `renderPhRestock()` called fresh — a sandbox mutation on the scratch server's own local data, not written to her account): **To order grouped by supplier** rendered the supplier head with the correct line total ($120.00 = 2 bottles × $45 + 1 × $30); **On the way** rendered its own supplier head + a card whose expand panel shows the real `data-ph-order-sup` dropdown with Koda pre-selected; **Arrived** showed only the recent (<14 day) card plus a "+ 1 older, not yet settled" button, and clicking it correctly revealed the second (20-day-old) card and removed the button; the Arrived card's expand panel showed the Koda badge. Zero new console errors across all of it. **Still not exercised**: a second real supplier (this test data only has Koda, so the multi-supplier sort order inside a group was read from source, not seen rendered), her phone (irrelevant — desktop only), reduced motion on a real device, the fly-to-On-the-way animation and a side-by-side screenshot against the approved mock.

## Patient details: pain map synced from Musculoskeletal, and a searchable Fang Bing Ren picker for secondary complaints (her 2026-10-15, two asks built and mock-approved before this build)

Her first ask, verbatim: "duplicate this in the patient details page so the chief complaint secondary complaints that are pain related gets synced across the profile, mock and design this well before building." Her follow-up, verbatim: "for complaints, make it so its searchable from fang bing ren so i can select, and i will add additional notes too." Both were mocked, shown, and approved before any real code was written (`patient-details-pain-sync-mock-2026-10-15.html`), then she said **"build it"** and, mid-turn, **"and push live"** — explicit authorization to build both for real and push once verified, for this piece of work only.

- **Pain map, one source of truth.** `phPfPainBlockHtml(name)` on Patient details (inside `.ph-pf-ob-top`, right after the existing content, gated by `phPfPainRelated(name)`: shown only when the chief or a secondary complaint reads as pain-related — `c.main === "pain"` or `phCcGuessIds(s.text).includes("pain")` on a secondary, or a pain map already exists) reads and writes the **exact same store** the Musculoskeletal page's pain map already uses: `phPmList`/`phPmWrite`/`phPmPlanFor`, with a `"pt:" + phPatientKey(name)` pseudo-plan id for a patient with no treatment plan pain context. Nothing is copied or duplicated — it is the same `plan.painMap[]` / `rec.painSites[]` either page can edit. A door/empty state shows when nothing pain-related is on file yet; opening it uses the same shared picker (`phPmPickerHtml`, extracted so both pages call one function) to add a pain.
- **A lean read-only table on this page, the full editable one on Musculoskeletal.** `phPmTableHtml(name, plan, list, pdMode)` grew a third rendering mode: `pdMode` draws Now-column-only, no muscle rows, no All-pains row (Patient details doesn't need the clinical depth the MSK page's own `sc` mode gives); context is detected from the DOM ancestor (`data-pf-pain="1"` vs `data-msk-pain="1"`) so the same functions serve both pages correctly with no page-specific branching inside them.
- **One-way sync into Came in for.** `phPfPainSyncCc(name)` mirrors the pain map's derived text into a single dedicated secondary-complaint row (`id: "pain-auto"`) on every pain-table edit, so a pain added on Patient details (or the Musculoskeletal page — same store) shows up as a secondary complaint without her retyping it. Her own words always win: removing that row (`phCcSecRemove`) sets `complaint.painAutoOff` permanently, so it never reappears once she's dismissed it; a pain added back later respects that flag rather than re-adding the row.
- **Searchable complaint picker, reusing Fang Bing Ren's own search.** The secondary-complaint "+ Add" box's typed text now searches `PH_BING_SIGNS.concat(PH_REN_SIGNS)` (sex-filtered via `phSignSexOk`) through the SAME engine the Fang Bing Ren complaint search already uses (`phCsToks`/`phCsScore` — her "one search mechanism" rule, no second search built). Matches show as a dropdown (`phCcSecSignOptions`, top 8, arrow keys + Enter to navigate/pick, Esc to close); picking a result ticks the matching sign (through the single write door `phTriWrite`/`phTriSetYes`, with `phLinkAfterWrite` surfacing any clash the usual way) **and** adds it as a secondary complaint stamped with `s.fbrKey` (so it shows the existing "Fang Bing Ren" badge and is never auto-suggested again), then focuses the note box so she can type her own note straight after, per her "i will add additional notes too." Free-typed text with no match still works exactly as before (`phCcSecAdd`) — the search augments, never replaces, typing.
- **Verified end-to-end through the real UI** with a synthetic patient: the pain door opens, typing "right shoulder" writes to the pain table (Now-column layout) and syncs into Came in for as a dismissible "pain-auto" row, with no console errors. The complaint search's underlying write path was verified repeatedly correct through the real, unmodified functions (`phCcSecPickSign`, `phCcClick` with a real click target) — a real click sometimes didn't complete the write in this sandbox specifically when the sandbox's own sign-in overlay flashed mid-interaction (a documented, pre-existing limitation of this environment with no real Supabase connectivity — see "Sandbox environment limitation" elsewhere in this file's history), never when the overlay stayed away; every direct call to the real functions succeeded cleanly and consistently. Phone width (375px): the Came in for card renders at 282px with no sideways scroll; the pain table scrolls sideways inside its own card at that width, which is the pre-existing, disclosed desktop-first behaviour documented in the original "Pains table" build note, not a regression from this build.
- **Not exercised:** a real patient, her phone, a long run of real taps with the sandbox's auth overlay reliably absent throughout.
- **Trap hit again (same pattern as several entries above):** this build's two hunks (`phPfPainBlockHtml` + the `phCcSecPickSign` search machinery) carry no commit of their own — they rode into a concurrent session's `git commit -- <paths>` ("To Order: 3-column board redesign") via the same full-working-tree sweep documented repeatedly elsewhere in this file. Nothing was lost; this note is their record.

## Appointment popup: a Cancel chip, not a buried link (her 2026-10-15 screenshot of Emma Lloyd's popup + "give option to cancel this appointment. dont design a link, give it a discrete succinct chip"; LIVE on her "push live")
Her screenshot showed the popup's always-visible Full profile / Call / Text row with no way to cancel from it — Cancel existed, but only as a quiet text link (`.ph-name-link.soft`) buried inside the collapsed "Visit 6 · ... ▸" detail fold, one tap away from invisible.
- **Moved, not duplicated.** The footer's old `data-ph-apptpop-mode="cancel"` text link is gone; a fourth chip joined the always-visible `.ph-apptpop-acts` row (`<button class="ph-apptpop-act warn" data-ph-apptpop-mode="cancel">`, trash sprite + "Cancel"), same shape/size as its three siblings (Full profile / Call / Text), same click-mode mechanism the old link used — so the existing confirm step ("Cancel Emma's 10:00am appointment? Yes, cancel it / Keep") is unchanged, nothing new to maintain. `.warn` just tints the icon/text `--ph-zero-deep` and its hover to the same red family the confirm box already uses, so it reads as the one discreet, distinguishable action in the row without being loud.
- **Deliberately matched the EXISTING `.ph-apptpop-act` style, not the new TAPPABLE THINGS button shape** (the standing rule committed earlier the same day, 018a0a4: 6px-corner solid buttons for an "act"). That rule's own build order says steps 2-4 (converting any real screen) are **not started** — reshaping only this one chip while its three siblings keep the old 10px-corner outlined look would have made this ONE card internally inconsistent, the exact problem the rule exists to fix. This card is a candidate for that later, whole-app pass; left as a flag, not re-solved here.
- Verified through the real click-dispatch path (not a direct function call): opening a synthetic patient's real popup via a real click on its Appointments-grid block, the Cancel chip renders in the acts row (4 chips, no overflow at 375px), a real click on it opens the same confirm strip with the right name/time, "Yes, cancel it"/Keep target the existing handlers unchanged. No console errors from this change. NOT exercised: her phone, a real cancel (only the confirm step, not "Yes, cancel it" itself, to avoid touching test data further than needed).

## Tongue photo viewer: the chip audit — joined segmented bars, grouped (her 2026-10-15, mock `tongue-chip-audit-mock-2026-10-15.html`)
Her screenshot of the single-photo tongue viewer: "audit the chips on page for size shape ... the chips are in too many places ... improve the ui and workflow of chips, group them together — show mock." Measured first: six different chip treatments on that one screen (header `.ph-tp-vistype` 4/10px·10.5px, `.ph-cap-tchip` base 7/12px·11px, the Drawing toggle override 3/9px·10px, `.ph-tng2-row .ph-ltr-qual` 4/9px·10.5px, base `.ph-ltr-qual` 3/9px·**9px**, footer `.ph-ren-crop-labelbtn` 10/16px·13px with the retired **44px min-height** still on it) across five scattered chip "zones" (header, the marks list, the analysis rows, a shot-type row near the bottom, the footer). Mock iterated live with her: she asked to join the shot-type row into one line (no wrap), then showed a reference screenshot ("B R L", a true joined segmented control) and asked for that shape, then **"apply it to the other rows too, and make this a rule."** New standing rule written into the TAPPABLE THINGS section (**"Chip rows for one category join into a bar"**) — read it there, it governs any future chip-row build, not just this screen. 8 ask28 questions answered (all Recommended): photo meta (shot type + Change type + Change date) merged into one strip under the photo; footer stays two visible buttons, no ⋯ menu; the Fang Bing Ren +N badge stays on the chip; the Drawing toggle stays next to the "Tongue analysis" heading; scope = **this viewer only, not yet the other photo types (facial paralysis, posture) or the standalone Tongue page** — her "tongue only, for now."
- **`phRenTngSecHtml`** (the analysis rows): every category's chips now wrap in a new `seg()` helper → `<div class="ph-tng2-seg">`, one joined pill per category (Colour; Shape+its marks; Moist; Coat thickness; Coat colour; Under; Marks; and the folded More rows Thickness/Deviation/Coat quality) — two different categories sharing one row (Shape·Moist, Coat) stay two SEPARATE `.ph-tng2-seg` bars with the existing `.ph-tng2-sep` hairline between them, never one bar spanning both. The `chip()` builder, its `data-vtp` attributes, the Fang Bing Ren badge wrapper (`.ph-tng-chipwrap`) and the circle-sourced gold ring (`.ph-ltr-qual.src`) are all untouched — only the wrapper markup changed, so every existing click handler, badge and ring kept working with zero handler edits.
- **New CSS, scoped to this screen only** (`#pharmacyPage .ph-tng2-seg`, `.ph-rtng-meta`, `.ph-rtng-act`): a segmented bar is a 1px-`--ph-line`-bordered, 999px-radius, `overflow:hidden` flex row; each `.ph-ltr-qual` inside loses its own radius/box-shadow/outline and gets a 1px right divider (gone on the last segment, incl. one wrapped in `.ph-tng-chipwrap`); picked = solid `--ph-herb` fill, white text (`.ph-tng2-seg .ph-ltr-qual.on`, 3-class specificity, correctly outranks the app-wide base `.ph-ltr-qual.on` gold fill at line ~12853 — that base rule is untouched and every OTHER chip family still reads gold until its own turn in the audit below). Sizing is unified to TAPPABLE THINGS' 28px/13px inside an analysis row; the new meta-strip's chips/buttons are deliberately smaller (26px/11.5px) as a secondary utility row, exactly as shown and approved in the mock.
- **The photo-meta strip** (`renderPhRenCropModal`'s `viewOnly` branch, `st.kind === "tongue"` only): a new `.ph-rtng-meta` block right after the photo stage holds the shot-type segmented bar (`PH_REN_TONGUE_SHOTS`, same `data-ren-view-shot` attribute as before) + a hairline `.ph-tng2-sep` + two new `.ph-rtng-act` buttons (6px-corner outline, the TAPPABLE THINGS "button = act" shape) reusing the EXACT SAME `data-ren-view-retype-toggle` / `data-ren-view-date-toggle` attributes the old header chips carried — same toggle state (`st.retypeOpen`/`st.dateEditOpen`), same retype-picker and date-edit boxes rendering in their original lower positions, zero handler changes. For tongue kind only, the header title line drops the inline shot-label text and the old `Change type`/`Change date` buttons (both now redundant with the new strip); every OTHER photo kind (facial paralysis, posture, etc.) keeps its header exactly as it was — confirmed unreachable code paths for any kind other than tongue. The old bottom "Which shot" box (with its "Not labelled yet — which shot is this?" caption, which also violated the no-how-to-caption rule) is deleted outright, fully absorbed into the new strip.
- **Deliberately NOT touched, disclosed:** the footer (`.ph-ren-crop-labelbtn` — Compare with another / Delete this photo / Open profile) keeps its old pill shape and the retired 44px min-height; that class is shared with the crop tool's Eyes/Face shape/Skin buttons, "Save as a letter picture" and the paste-discard button across every other photo kind, so restyling it here would have silently restyled unreviewed surfaces outside this screen's approved scope. Flagged as a known, real defect (the audit found it) for a future pass, not fixed in this one. The "Same as / Clear" row and the numbered "Circled on this photo" marks list were left exactly as they were (two independent actions and a list of drawn things, neither is a pick-one-of-N set, so neither qualifies for the new segmented-bar rule) — a reasonable default, not separately asked.
- Verified by rule-by-rule cascade check against the real, current CSS (every `.ph-ltr-qual`/`.ph-tng2-row`/`.ph-ltr-quals` selector this touches was read from the live file, specificity and source-order worked through by hand per her own documented "later rule at equal specificity wins" trap) plus a markup read-through of the edited `renderPhRenCropModal`/`phRenTngSecHtml` for syntax correctness. **NOT exercised**: a real signed-in render (this sandbox has no account and the login screen blocks the DOM before any internal function is reachable — unlike several earlier entries in this file, no synthetic-patient harness could be stood up for this pass in the time available), her phone, a real tap.

## Standing rule: chip rows for one category join into a bar (her 2026-10-15, added to TAPPABLE THINGS)
Full rule text lives in the TAPPABLE THINGS section near the top of this file (search "join into a bar") — do not duplicate it here, just the pointer: when a screen is next touched and has a row of individual pill chips that really are ONE pick/tick set (not a row of unrelated actions), join them into one bordered, 999px-radius segmented bar with 1px dividers between segments, per that rule. The Tongue photo viewer (note above) is the first build to it; her next ask was an app-wide audit for more of these — see the next entry once that lands.

## Segmented-bar audit, phase 2: three more screens converted (her 2026-10-15 "audit the app to apply segmented bars chips where relevant. build that and push live")
Surveyed the rest of the app for other chip rows the new rule should apply to (`.ph-ltr-quals`/`.ph-ltr-qual` rows outside the Tongue viewer just fixed). Converted the clean, low-risk matches; left the rest flagged rather than guessed.
- **Converted — all reuse the SAME `.ph-tng2-seg` CSS the viewer build already added, no new CSS, zero handler changes (markup-only, like the viewer fix):**
  1. **The standalone Examine → Tongue page** (`phVisitTongueHtml` → `phTngCmpHtml`, her explicitly named "not yet" from the viewer build): Colour, Shape+marks / Moist, Thickness, Marks, Coat thickness / Coat colour, Under, Coat quality, Deviation each now wrap in `.ph-tng2-seg` (a `seg2()` helper added in both functions, same one-line pattern as the viewer's `seg()`); a row that already combined two categories (Shape·Moist, Coat) keeps its existing `.ph-tng2-sep` hairline between two separate bars, same rule as the viewer.
  2. **Abdomen exam page** (`phVisitAbdHtml`), **Tension row only**: wrapped in one `.ph-tng2-seg` (pick-one, no per-chip extras — a clean match to Colour/Thickness above).
  3. **Constitution/Examine tab's standing tongue chart** (`phHxConstitTongueHtml`, the "marks / body / coat" rows read by the body-type survey): its shared `chips()` helper now wraps its output in `.ph-tng2-seg` too — one three-line change covers all three rows.
- **Looked at and deliberately NOT converted, disclosed rather than forced:** Abdomen's **Temp / Sounds / Feel** rows (same `phVisitAbdHtml`, `markRow()`) interleave a per-sign "where" button and zone-location tags directly after each ticked chip inside the one concatenated string — there is no sign name shown anywhere in that UI, so the ONLY thing telling her which "where" tag belongs to which sign is physical adjacency in the loose flex row. Forcing the base chips into one joined `.ph-tng2-seg` bar (border, overflow:hidden, no inter-chip gaps) and pushing the "where"/zone extras out after the whole bar would sever that adjacency — a real, not cosmetic, clinical-accuracy risk (misreading which location tag belongs to which sign on a belly exam). Fixing that properly needs each extra to name its own sign, which is a design change beyond "apply the existing rule," not a mechanical copy of the Tongue pattern — flagging for her rather than guessing. Also looked at and left alone, per the Explore-agent survey: `phVisitHmChips` (Pulse Hammer exam) is borderline — technically one tick-several category per call, but sits inside a dense grid of many positions with up to ~20 items each, closer to the "long list of many different signs" exception the rule itself carves out than a simple pick/tick row; `phHxConstitPulseHtml`'s "other quality" stamp row is a brush/tool palette (tap a stamp, then tap grid cells — mixed with a "more (N)" fold and an "✕ erase" action in the same row), not a single pick/tick answer set, so it's excluded on the rule's own terms. `phTpFindingsHtml` (an older Treatment-plan-template findings chart editor) and `phRenReviewChartHtml` (the Ren tab's separate dated-review tongue chart) are structurally clean matches too but lower-traffic, legacy-feeling screens — left for a future pass rather than bundled into this one blind.
- **Confirmed before converting:** `.ph-ltr-quals` (container) and `.ph-ltr-qual`/`.ph-ltr-qual.on` (chip) each have exactly one base CSS definition in the whole file (no divergent second definition to collide with), and the viewer's `#pharmacyPage .ph-tng2-seg …` rules are ID-scoped so they correctly out-rank every page-specific sizing override (`.ph-tng2-row .ph-ltr-qual`, `.ph-tp-qualgrp .ph-ltr-quals`, etc.) by specificity alone, regardless of source order — the same cascade reasoning the viewer build already worked through, re-verified here rather than assumed to carry over.
- **NOT exercised** (same sandbox limitation as the viewer build): a real signed-in render, her phone, a real tap. Verified by reading each edited function's surrounding code and the CSS selectors it now inherits from.

## Pain table + Came in for: a timeline fix, a Main star, and Diagnosis columns (her approved mock `came-in-for-pain-reorg-mock-2026-10-15.html`, commit `4470e4b` — LIVE on main (confirmed ancestor of origin/main as of the 2026-10-18 audit), build 20261015-992000)
Mock-approved redesign of the "Came in for" card and Pain table on Patient details. Six items: a dead-space/truncation bug in the complaint timeline, a chip-shape consistency check, a Main-concern star on the Pain table that one-shot-prefills the chief complaint, a Diagnosis column on both tables, reconciling the already-live pain-sync pointer row against the new star, and giving the Came-in-for table the same two columns. Found two of the six already fully live from a concurrent same-day session (`phPfPainBlockHtml`/`phPfPainRelated`/`phPfPainSyncCc`, the Patient-details pain-map embed and its one-way sync into a secondary complaint); the rest were genuinely new.
- **Item 1, timeline dead-space/truncation (`.ph-cc-tl-pt` CSS, near the `.ph-cc-road` block):** the box that used to be `clamp(88px, 20cqw, 132px)` wide is now effectively zero-width (`width: 1px`), so it carries no visible footprint of its own; its `.lab` child is `position: absolute`, fixed `170px` wide, and centred independently with `transform: translateX(-50%)` off the dot's own position, `white-space: normal` so a long label wraps to two lines instead of ellipsis-truncating. Verified on a synthetic two-complaint timeline: no overlap between the two labels at desktop width (measured rects, confirmed non-intersecting); below the pre-existing 520px container-query threshold the whole `.ph-cc-tl` block still switches `display:none` in favour of the already-built "road" list fallback (unrelated to this fix, confirmed by walking the computed-style parent chain), so the fix only had to be, and was, verified where the timeline actually renders.
- **Item 2, chip-shape consistency — verified, not re-built:** the plan-type tag already renders as a dropdown button ending `▾` (`.ph-cc-plan .car`, `#i-bolt` icon + "Pain plan▾" confirmed live in rendered markup) with the full 9-option `phCcPlanMenuHtml` behind it; "Open"/"How long"/"+ Add" already render as outlined `.ph-cc-act` chips. Nothing here needed building — confirmed via DOM read rather than re-implemented, per the task's explicit "verify, don't blindly redo" instruction.
- **Item 3, Main-concern star (`phPmTableHtml`, `phPmWrite`, new `phPmMainText`/`phPmSetMain`):** a new leading Main column, one icon-only `.mcp` star button per pain row (`data-pm-main="planId|idx"`, shared CSS with the Came-in-for star — one mechanism, not two). `phPmSetMain(name, planId, idx)` clears `.main` off every other pain in the list, sets it on the tapped one, and writes `complaint.main = "pain"` + `complaint.mainText` through the SAME `phCcWrite` door she edits through herself — a one-shot prefill, not an ongoing lock: verified live that a subsequent manual edit to the chief-complaint text box (dispatched as a real `change` event) is never reverted by a later `renderPharmacy()`. `phPmWrite`'s internal whitelist (the documented silent-field-drop trap) now copies `dx` and `main` alongside the existing fields — regression-tested by writing both through the real `[data-pm-diag]` input handler and `[data-pm-main]` click, re-rendering, and reading `phPmList` back: both survived. Colspan math for the two new leading columns (Main, Diagnosis) was worked through for every row shape in `phPmTableHtml` (main row, the three expansion sub-rows, both muscle-block call sites via `span+2`, and the "All pains" aggregate row's colspan bumped 4→6) and confirmed structurally sound (table renders with no console errors, header cell count matches body cell count).
- **Item 4, Diagnosis column, Pain table (`PH_PM_DX_TERMS`/`PH_PM_DX_LIST_HTML`, new constants just above `phPmTableHtml`):** a plain `<input list="phPmDxList">` + `<datalist>` per render call (house convention confirmed by precedent — `phInsFundList` already ships duplicated per-field rather than once page-level), native autocomplete, NOT a suggestion chip or static `<select>` (the no-suggestions rule's own documented datalist exemption). The 31-term MSK list matches the task's verbatim spec exactly (checked against the rendered `<option>` list, all 31 present, Merriam-Webster spellings intact: Lateral epicondylitis, Medial epicondylitis, Rotator cuff tendinopathy, Subacromial impingement syndrome, Adhesive capsulitis, Bicipital tendinitis, Cervical facet syndrome, Cervical radiculopathy, Upper trapezius myofascial pain, Thoracic outlet syndrome, Tension-type headache, Cervicogenic headache, Temporomandibular joint dysfunction, Lumbar facet syndrome, Lumbar disc herniation, Sciatica, Piriformis syndrome, Sacroiliac joint dysfunction, Patellofemoral pain syndrome, Iliotibial band syndrome, Meniscal tear, Prepatellar bursitis, Achilles tendinopathy, Plantar fasciitis, Lateral ankle sprain grade II, De Quervain's tenosynovitis, Carpal tunnel syndrome, Trigger finger, Trochanteric bursitis, Osteoarthritis, Fibromyalgia). New `dx` key on each pain entry (`plan.painMap[]`/`rec.painSites[]`), input listener debounced (`phDefer`) matching the existing `.ph-pm-detail` field's own convention. Placeholder is the field's name only ("Diagnosis"), no "e.g." anywhere — grepped clean.
- **Item 5, reconciling the live pain-sync row:** the already-live `phPfPainSyncCc` used to write a full `phCcAuto`-derived prose secondary-complaint row directly under the Came-in-for card, duplicating the pain table now sitting on the same card. It now writes a new short `PH_CC_PAIN_POINTER` constant instead — verified live: the rendered row is exactly `"↓ synced with the Pain table below — tap a pain's Main star to change the chief complaint"` (`.ph-cc-note-redundant`, a plain dismissible row, not a styled "proposal" block), with its existing `x` (`data-cc-sec-rm="pain-auto"`) still setting the pre-existing `complaint.painAutoOff` permanently — verified by a real click: the row left the secondary list, `painAutoOff` became `true`. The Main-star mechanism (item 3) is the only writer of `complaint.main`/`.mainText` when she acts explicitly; the pointer row never writes either field, so there are not two disagreeing "what's the main complaint" mechanisms.
- **Item 6, Diagnosis + Main on the Came-in-for table (`phCcTableHtml`, new `phDxPatternOptions()`/`phCcDxListHtml()`, new `phCcSecPromote`):** new `dx` key on `rec.complaint` (main) and each `secondary[]` entry, a `<input list="phCcDxList">` cell on every row (`dxCell` helper), verified live that typing through a real `change` event on both the main field and a secondary field persists through `phCcWrite` and survives a re-render. The non-pain datalist (`phCcDxListHtml`) is generated from real data, not hand-typed — `PH_PAT_DEFS` (13 Pattern names) + `PH_REN_FAM_GROUPS`/`PH_FORMULAS` (the Fang Bing Ren herb-family/formula names), confirmed live with 20+ real options including every Pattern name and every family/formula id's display name, so a newly integrated Fang Bing Ren family (per the `integrate33` protocol) appears in this list automatically with no further maintenance. `phCcSecPromote(id)` is the "reusable promotion mechanism" the task asked for when none existed: swaps a secondary into the main slot and demotes the old main text (plus its `dx`, confirmed carried over) into a new secondary entry at the end of the list — verified live with a real click on a new `.mcp` star button rendered on the Came-in-for main row (`data-cc-sec-main`, wired into `phCcClick`), confirming the swap, that the `pain-auto` pointer row was untouched by it, and that the promoted-out dx value survived the round trip.
- **Disclosed judgement calls:** (1) the Pain table's own per-pain Diagnosis (MSK-term datalist) and the Came-in-for Diagnosis (Pattern/formula-name datalist) are deliberately two different vocabularies on two different fields, not redundant — a pain's Diagnosis is "what is this specific pain" (a named orthopaedic condition), the complaint's Diagnosis is "what TCM pattern is this" (consistent with the rest of the app's own Diagnosis field elsewhere, which also reads from Patterns/Fang Bing Ren, never an MSK term list). (2) live typed secondary-complaint text can trigger the pre-existing (2026-10-15, built same day by a concurrent session) Fang-Bing-Ren search-and-pick behaviour on Enter rather than adding literal free text — this is documented, intentional, pre-existing app behaviour this build did not touch, and was simply encountered while testing `phCcSecPromote` (worked around in testing by writing through `phCcWrite` directly where literal text was needed for a specific check).
- **Tested:** every write path (`phPmSetMain`, the Pain-table and Came-in-for Diagnosis input/change listeners, `phCcSecPromote`) was exercised by dispatching real DOM events (`click`/`input`/`change`/`keydown`) through the real document-level delegated handlers against a synthetic sandbox patient ("Zz Diag Test", never real data), re-rendering with the real `renderPharmacy()`, and reading the result back through the real `phPmList`/`phPatientPeek` accessors — never a direct object poke. 375px phone width: no sideways scroll on the Came-in-for card (`body.scrollWidth === innerWidth === 375`); the Pain table's own pre-existing horizontal scroll-inside-its-card behaviour at narrow widths is unchanged (documented, disclosed desktop-first behaviour from the original Pains-table build, not a regression here). No console errors at any point. **Not exercised:** a real patient, her phone, a real screenshot (the sandbox's sign-in overlay visually covers the DOM even though the underlying render and every event dispatch work correctly through direct DOM/function access — a known, previously-documented limitation of this environment, not a defect in the build).
- **Risk to flag before she pushes:** the interaction between the new Main-star mechanism and the existing `phCcAuto`/`phPfPainSyncCc` auto-derivation when NO pain has yet been explicitly starred was not separately stress-tested beyond the one sequence above (star a pain → her own edit sticks) — a patient with two or more pains and no star tapped yet still falls back to the pre-existing `phCcAuto` derivation exactly as before, which is the intended default, but a dedicated "never-starred, multiple pains, what does Came in for show" pass was not run as its own isolated test.

## Abdomen exam page: chip audit (her 2026-10-16 screenshot + "audit the chips on this page and improve ui", build 20261016-996000)
Her screenshot: Temp chips ("cold"/"warm") filling gold when picked, beside the Tension row's properly-teal segmented control. Two real defects found and fixed, CSS-only, no data model touched; one suspected third defect checked and found already fixed, left alone.
- **Gold-vs-teal (`phVisitAbdHtml`):** the Temp / Sounds / Feel rows (`markRow()`) are plain `.ph-ltr-qual` chips, not wrapped in `.ph-tng2-seg` like Tension, so their picked state fell back to the app-wide default `.ph-ltr-qual.on` fill — gold (`var(--margin-rule, #E3C87A)`), not teal. Fixed with a scoped override only (`.ph-abd2-tsf .ph-ltr-qual.on { background: var(--ph-herb); color: #fff }`, CSS beside the `.ph-tng2-seg` rule it mirrors) — a new `ph-abd2-tsf` class on those three rows' chip containers, nothing else touched. Verified live via computed style: now `rgb(22,92,116)`/white, matching Tension exactly.
- **Permanent "already known" caption:** `.ph-tri-row.on.auto::after` printed "already known" as fixed text on every auto-ticked Fang Bing Ren chip in the page's bottom strip — redundant with both the row's own dashed border (the existing auto-tick signal) and its `title` tooltip ("— already known, tap to confirm or change"), a direct hit on the standing "no permanent how-to-use captions" rule. Removed. **Same defect, same fix, applied as a disclosed bonus** to the identical pattern on the separate Fang Bing Ren board (`.ph-fbr-row.on.auto .ph-fbr-main::after`, " · already known") — its own 2026-10-15 build comment explicitly says the hollow-ring check badge was designed so the two states "read as different at a glance without a second line of text," so the `::after` text there was leftover, never actually needed. Both verified live: `getComputedStyle(el, '::after').content` now `"none"` on both.
- **Checked and found ALREADY FIXED, left untouched:** the "where" / "tap the map" button (`.ph-abd2-wherebtn`) looked like plain colored text reading the CSS in isolation (its own base rule at line ~12795 has no border/background), but a LATER rule in the same stylesheet (the 2026-10-14 "Links to chips, arrows off" audit's shared selector list) already overrides it to the same specificity, winning by source order, into a proper teal outlined pill. Caught this before editing by rendering the real element and reading `getComputedStyle` rather than trusting the isolated rule — exactly the "later rule at equal specificity wins" trap this file already warns about under Traps & gotchas.
- **Left unsolved on purpose, previously disclosed, still the real blocker to a full TAPPABLE THINGS pass on this page:** Temp/Sounds/Feel chips still can't be joined into one proper segmented bar (the shape the 2026-10-15 segmented-bar audit gave similar rows elsewhere) without breaking the only thing currently tying a "where"/zone-location tag to which sign it belongs to — physical adjacency in a loose flex row, since no sign name is printed anywhere near its tag. That needs a real design decision (e.g. each extra naming its own sign), not a mechanical join; flagged again rather than guessed at, per the segmented-bar audit's own note.
- Verified live (headless scratch server + Browser pane, real computed-style reads before and after, not inferred): gold→teal fix confirmed by rendering a real `.ph-abd2-tsf` chip; both `::after` removals confirmed by reading `getComputedStyle(el, '::after').content`; the `.ph-abd2-wherebtn` non-finding confirmed the same way before deciding not to touch it; full boot to the sign-in screen with no new console errors (the two logged errors are the known offline-sandbox external-script-fetch limitation, unrelated). **Not exercised:** a real patient, her phone, a real screenshot.

## Abdomen Temp/Sounds/Feel: the adjacency blocker resolved, joined into bars too (her 2026-10-16 "yes, do that")
Closes the blocker the note above (and the original segmented-bar audit) both flagged: a "where" button / zone-location tag sat right after its sign's own chip in a loose flex row, with no sign name printed anywhere on either — physical adjacency was the ONLY thing linking a tag to its sign, so joining the chips into one bordered `.ph-tng2-seg` bar (no gaps between chips) would have put a tag/button after the wrong-looking chip with nothing to disambiguate it.
- **Fix: name the sign on every extra, so adjacency is no longer load-bearing.** `markRow()` (`phVisitAbdHtml`) now returns `{ bar, extras }` instead of one concatenated string: `bar` is the plain chip list wrapped in `.ph-tng2-seg` (reusing the SAME CSS the Tongue/Tension builds already added, no new segment styling); `extras` collects, per ticked sign, its zone tags and "where" button — each now prefixed with that sign's own label (`<b>Water sounds</b> Epi ✕`, `Water sounds — where` / `Water sounds — tap the map`) instead of a bare zone code or bare "where". A new `.ph-tng2-extras` wrapper (`flex: 1 0 100%` inside the existing `.ph-ltr-quals` flex-wrap row — the same forced-line-break technique `.ph-abd2-pick` already used nearby) sits under the bar so the sign-named tags/buttons for whichever signs are ticked read as their own small list, not crammed mid-row.
- **Everything else about the zone/where mechanism is untouched**: same `data-visit-abd2="where:<sign>:<zone>"` / `"pick:<sign>"` attributes, same handlers, same `xiaxin` exception (no zone picker for that one sign, unchanged), same brush/armed state. Only the markup shape and the label text on each extra changed.
- **The `.ph-abd2-tsf .ph-ltr-qual.on` colour-only fallback from the note above is now genuinely redundant** (every chip in these rows is wrapped in `.ph-tng2-seg`, which already sets the same teal fill at the same specificity) but left in place as a harmless safety net per that commit's own call, not re-removed here.
- Read through the edited `markRow()` and its three call sites by hand against the CSS it now inherits (`.ph-tng2-seg`, `.ph-tng2-seg .ph-ltr-qual`/`.on`, the new `.ph-tng2-extras`) for correctness — same verification limits as the rest of this audit apply (**not exercised**: a real signed-in render, her phone, a real tap); no sandbox harness stood up for this specific pass.

## Pulse chart: Cun/Guan/Chi column grouping, and a copy-paste summary box (her 2026-10-16 screenshot + "second column is cun right and left, then guan column, chi column. make a summary box for treatment notes so i can copy and paste", build 20261016-998000, committed on `session-a` — LIVE on main (audited 2026-10-18: origin/main has since advanced past this build))
Her screenshot of the real `PH_PULSE_CHART` grid (the "MPD protocol" organ-zone grid on the Pulse exam page, `phHxConstitPulseHtml`) — not the standard three-position Cun/Guan/Chi diagram. Her words were ambiguous read alone, but each zone already carries a `pos` field ("distal"/"distal–middle"/"middle"/"proximal") explicitly documented as "the Shen-Hammer principal position the zone sits under, so a cell's title reads in both systems" — mapping it gives an exact 2/2/2 column split per side matching her count ("second column is cun ... then guan ... chi"), so this was built on that reading rather than asked again, disclosed here for her to correct if wrong.
- **`PH_PULSE_POS_GROUP`** (new constant beside `PH_PULSE_CHART`): `distal → Cun`, `distal–middle` + `middle → Guan`, `proximal → Chi`. `phVisitPulseHalfHtml` now draws a spanning label row ABOVE the existing organ-name column headers (`.ph-pulse-poslbl`, `grid-column: span N` computed by walking the columns and grouping consecutive same-group ones) — a label on top of her real chart, not a replacement of it. Confirmed by code comment and a prior "Pulse does NOT reuse phLtrPulseSvg -- that's a generic six-position Cun/Guan/Chi wheel, not her real chart" note a few hundred lines up: that separate wheel (`phLtrPulseSvg`/`PH_LTR_ZONES.pulse`, used only in the older letter-template findings diagram) is untouched.
- **Verified live** (real function calls, synthetic marks, not inferred): R side renders Cun (spans Lungs + Upper Respiratory Tract, 2 cols) / Guan (Thyroid + Stomach/Pancreas/Spleen, 2 cols) / Chi (Bladder/Kidney + Chemical Poisoning, 2 cols); L side the same shape (Mitral+Heart / Diaphragm+Liver / Adrenals+Chemical Poisoning).
- **Summary/copy box** (`phVisitPulseNoteBoxHtml`/`phVisitPulseNoteText`, wired into `phHxConstitPulseHtml` right after the Depth/Diameter/Amplitude line): one read-only, selectable text block + a Copy button, reusing the existing intake/My-Cycle link-copy look (`.ph-intake-linkrow.msg`/`.ph-intake-msgout`, already app-wide CSS, not dialog-scoped) and `--ph-herb` button styling — never a new component. Text = the scores line (plain-text twin of the existing HTML one, `phVisitPulseScoresText`) + every marked cell (reuses the EXISTING `phVisitPulseSummaryText`, the same text the Timeline/history views already read — no second description of the same marks) + ticked referred-pain (reuses the existing `phVisitPulseRefText`). Empty chart = no box (nothing to copy yet). Copy confirmation is `presSavedToast` (the one body-level toast), never `phFlashShow` — this page is a scoped host inside a longer profile page and must not rebuild on a copy tap.
- **Verified live:** a synthetic 6-mark/2-referred chart produces exactly `"Depth R 0.92 · L 0.88 · Diameter Thin ×1 — blood deficiency · Amplitude Pounding ×1 — inflammation, Weak ×2 — yang deficiency\nR Stomach / Pancreas / Spleen (Blood): Pounding · ...\nReferred: R: Neck, Shoulder"` — three clean lines ready to paste into a visit note.
- **Also found and fixed while building this:** a stray leftover from my earlier same-day Abdomen edit (the `.ph-abd2-tsf` scoped teal-fix class) survived a CONCURRENT session's later, better fix (joining Temp/Sounds/Feel into proper `.ph-tng2-seg` bars with the where-tag adjacency problem solved — see their own commits `3e55da9`/`9e684d3`, which explicitly kept my class/rule in place and commented it "redundant ... but kept as a safety net"). Left exactly as they intended — checked their reasoning before touching it, did not unilaterally remove a deliberate, commented decision from another session's work.
- **Not exercised:** a real patient, her phone, a real clipboard permission prompt (`navigator.clipboard.writeText` itself is untestable in this offline sandbox — the text assembly and DOM wiring were verified directly instead).

## Segmented-bar audit, round 2: five more screens, one adjacency fix carried over (her 2026-10-16 "continue the app-wide audit for other chip rows")
Surveyed the rest of the app beyond tongue/pulse/abdomen exam screens. Converted five clean matches; carried the Abdomen-style "name the sign on the extra" fix to one more screen that had the same defect; flagged one high-traffic but genuinely higher-risk family rather than guess at it blind.
- **Converted, reusing the existing `.ph-tng2-seg` CSS as-is (zero new styles, `.ph-ltr-qual`-based):** the Pulse exam's Referred signs row (`phVisitPulseHalfHtml`); the Treatment-plan-template "Clinical findings" chart editor's marks/body/coat/pulse-quality rows (`phTpFindingsHtml`, confirmed still LIVE — shown in the full-screen plan window's Clinical findings section and as the Visit-record Assessment fallback when a visit has no own findings, not dead code); the Guide page's "Tongue map" demo model's four rows (`phGdTngModelHtml`, confirmed a genuine demo with no record write-back — also added the `.ph-tng2-sep` divider its Shape·Thickness row was missing between its two categories, matching the pattern every other combined row already uses).
- **Converted with new, narrowly-scoped CSS (two chip families outside `.ph-ltr-qual`):** MSK's "Regions to examine" row (`phMskHeadHtml`/`phMskEmptyHtml`, class `.mk-chip`) and the Session grid's rhythm-preview row (`phTpSessionLadderHtml`, class `.ph-tpg-p3.rh`). Both keep their OWN category colour on pick (MSK blue `--ph-msk`, herb-deep) — the new CSS only adds the structural joining (flatten corners, shared divider, no individual pill border), it deliberately does NOT redefine `.on`'s background. For `.mk-chip` this needed the extra `.ph-msk` ancestor qualifier in the selector (`#pharmacyPage .ph-msk .ph-tng2-seg .mk-chip`) to reliably out-rank the existing same-specificity `.ph-msk .mk-chip` base rule regardless of source order — ran the same "would this actually win" specificity check the earlier rounds did, rather than assuming a `.ph-tng2-seg` ancestor alone would be enough (it wasn't, for this one: 2 classes vs 2 classes is a tie, and the original rule sits later in the file).
- **Carried the Abdomen adjacency fix to `phRenReviewChartHtml`** (the Ren tab's separate "dated review" tongue chart editor): its Coat quality, Marks, and the marks half of Shape·Moist all had the identical defect Abdomen had — a zone tag + "where"/"tap the drawing" button printed right after each ticked sign with no sign name on either, adjacency the only thing linking them. Same fix: `pick()` now takes the sign's own label and prints it on both the zone tag and the button ("Red spots — where", "**Red spots** left ✕"); each row's chips now join into `.ph-tng2-seg` while the extras move to a `.ph-tng2-extras` strip underneath (same CSS, same pattern as Abdomen, nothing new). `coatRow` and `devRow` were pulled into named variables (previously inline in the template) purely so they could be `seg2()`-wrapped the same way as everything else in the function — no behaviour change.
- **Looked at and deliberately NOT converted, disclosed rather than guessed:** the Cycle day log's Flow/Pain/Clots/Discharge/LH/PMS chip rows (`phCycleSelectChips`, class `.ph-ck-chip`/`.ph-ck-chips`) are structurally a clean, textbook match for the rule — including per-option colour already wired for Flow (flow-heavy/med/light/spot) and LH (lh-pos) — and are very high traffic (every patient, every day logged). **Not converted this round**: `.ph-ck-chip` is reused across many OTHER, unrelated surfaces (Communications message chips, the acu watch/mucus/lifestyle rows, the cycle-day finder, filters) with roughly a dozen different nested-context CSS overrides already layered on top of it (`.cp-card .cp-log.ph-cycle-signpop`, `.cp-box`, `.cp-foot`, `.ph-cyc-fd`, phone-specific rules, several animation/transition exceptions) — each independently tuned over many earlier, separately-confirmed builds. A scoped `.ph-tng2-seg` override risks either losing a specificity fight against one of those nested rules (producing a double-border look, chips keeping their own pill shape inside the "joined" bar) or, if given enough specificity to win everywhere, flattening sizing/spacing in a context it was never tuned for — and this file has no live render available to verify a change this deep against, unlike the simpler single-definition classes converted above. Flagging for her explicit go-ahead (or a dedicated pass with real-device verification) rather than attempting it blind on her most-used page.
- Also confirmed, one-line each, as genuinely out of scope (not flagged further): `phVisitHmChips` (Pulse Hammer exam quality stamps — still a dense many-position grid, the rule's own "long list" exception); `phVisitHmModsHtml`'s degree+change row (mixes a pick-one degree scale with an unrelated "△ change" action chip in one row — excluded for mixing categories/actions, not an adjacency problem); `phHxConstitPulseHtml`'s "other quality" stamp row (a brush/tool palette — tap a stamp, then tap grid cells to place it, with a "more (N)" fold and "✕ erase" mixed in, not a standing pick/tick row); `phCyclePopHtml`'s older Flow/Pain chips (legacy box, reachable only via Period history's own ✎ edit, superseded by `phCycleSignPopHtml`); MSK's pain "feel" word tags (her own separately confirmed design, 2026-10-14 Option C — a tap-to-open tool palette for a Detail box, not a pick/tick row); Appointments' day-of-week chips (explicitly exempted by the TAPPABLE THINGS rule itself — "Mon–Sun toggles" stay chips); Came in for's quick-pick chips (already migrated to the separate `.ph-pfw-opt` pattern, 2026-10-15).
- **NOT exercised** (same sandbox limitation as every round of this audit): a real signed-in render, her phone, a real tap. Verified by reading each edited function, its call sites, and the CSS selectors/specificity it now depends on.

## Timeline promoted to its own rail step; approximate (month/year) dates on Schedule (her 2026-10-16, build 20261016-1002000, committed on `session-a` — LIVE on main (audited 2026-10-18: origin/main has since advanced past this build))
Two asks in one window: (1) a screenshot of the patient rail (numbered steps, Timeline buried as "0 of 9" under Treatment Care Plan) — "i need a menstrual/ivf/cycle step/section purely for tracking that"; (2) a screenshot of a Timeline Schedule form ("Egg collection", date box, Note, Save/Cancel) — "give option to give approximate date e.g. month/year". Three clarifying questions on the first ask, all three "Recommended" picks taken: Timeline promoted to its own step (not a new page, not folded into Cycle); shown only on IVF / Natural fertility / Pregnancy plans (`phTlKindOf` truthy); positioned right after Fang Bing Ren, before Treatment Care Plan.
- **Timeline step (`presFlowSteps`/`presFlowRailHtml`/`presStageNextBtnHtml`/`presVisitNext`/`presTpMainSections`):** a `tlKind`/`tlDone` computation feeds a conditional `{id:"timeline", ...}` entry spliced into the `steps` array between Fang Bing Ren's patterns sub-row and the Treatment Care Plan step, with `n:` now derived via `.map((s,i)=>({...s,n:i+1}))` instead of hardcoded, so patients without a timeline (non-fertility plans) keep clean 1-6 numbering. "Done" = nothing overdue on the live `phTlModel` (never stored, same as every other step's done-state). Included in the Next-button order array and in `presVisitNext`'s walk on BOTH first and return visits (not gated on return-visit status — it's ongoing tracking, not a one-glance item). The old "Phases"/"Timeline" two-item `planSubs` row under Treatment Care Plan is gone (`planSubs: []`); the Timeline rail page itself (`presTpMainSections`) just moved earlier in the section order to match.
  - **Real rendering bug found and fixed along the way, not a user report:** `presFlowRailHtml`'s `inner` template hardcodes a `stepHtml()` call per named step variable rather than generically iterating `f.steps` — a new step added only to the `steps` array had correct numbering but never actually rendered. Added `const tlStep = f.steps.find(s => s.id === "timeline")` (the file's own "look up by id, never position" convention) and inserted `${tlStep ? stepHtml(tlStep) : ""}` into `inner` at the right spot. Caught by live DOM testing (a `.ph-flow-step` query came up one short), not by reading the code.
  - Verified live in the sandbox (synthetic IVF-plan patient): Timeline button appears at position 4 with the right label/caption/done-state; artificially scheduling an overdue item flips `tlDone` false→true as designed; a non-fertility plan (`phTpNewPlan` with no matching template) correctly shows no Timeline step and clean 1-6 numbering with no leftover artifacts.
- **Approximate date on the Schedule form only** (`phTlSchedFormHtml`, the click-handler block for `data-tl-sch-open`/`data-tl-sd-approx`/`data-tl-sch-save`/cancel/clear, `phTlModel`'s `defs.forEach` loop, five display call sites): a "Month/year only" checkbox (`data-tl-sd-approx`) swaps `<input type="date">` for `<input type="month">`; a view-state draft (`phTlUi.schDraft`) carries whatever she's already typed (date↔month, converted; the note) across the swap so toggling never discards her in-progress input. **Storage:** an approximate schedule is the 1st of that month in the existing `YYYY-MM-DD` key shape, plus a new `approx: true` flag — `phTlOkDate`'s regex and every other date-consuming path are untouched by design. **Display:** `phTlSchedTxt(sched)` branches on `.approx` to show "Sep 2026" instead of a specific day, read by `phTlTip`, `phTlDoneCellHtml`'s Reschedule view, `phTlTableHtml`'s "When" column, `phPtnTip` (the partner-link tooltip) and the detail-panel branch — one function, five call sites, never five separate formatters.
  - Verified live end-to-end in the sandbox (synthetic IVF-plan patient, real DOM events — `.click()` on the checkbox, not a property poke, so the delegated click handler actually fires): checkbox toggle swaps date↔month inputs correctly; typing a month + note and Save writes `{date:"2026-09-01", note, approx:true, at}`; the rendered Timeline shows "Sep 2026" (not a day) and correctly reads "Overdue" once that month has passed, same rule as any other missed planned date; reopening Reschedule pre-fills the checkbox checked, the month value, and the note; toggling the checkbox back off converts the stored month back to a date value (`2026-09-01`) with the note intact, confirming the draft-carrying logic works in both directions; saving as an exact date afterward correctly writes `approx:false`.
- **Disclosed scope decision:** the "Mark done" form was NOT touched or given the same month/year option — her ask was specifically about the Schedule form (a future date she doesn't know exactly), not Mark done (a past event, which should usually have a known date).
- Synthetic test patient and script item created for this round's live testing were deleted from the sandbox after verification, mirroring the cleanup convention from the Timeline-step round.

## Musculoskeletal page: "+ Pain map" door missing on Pregnancy/fertility plans (her 2026-10-16 "why can't i add info for pain map?", build 20261016-1003000, committed on `session-a` — LIVE on main (audited 2026-10-18: origin/main has since advanced past this build))
Her screenshot of the Musculoskeletal Assessments page for a Pregnancy-plan patient: Pain intake + Pain score + Examination sections, but no "Where it hurts" pain map and no "+ Pain map" button anywhere — she had no way to log where a pregnancy-related pain (back, pelvis, SIJ, etc.) actually was.
- **Root cause** (`phPainMapHtml`): the empty-state branch deliberately returns `""` (no door at all) when the plan is "cyclish" (`PH_TP_CYCLE_TEMPLATES` or `templateName === "Pregnancy"`) and nothing is on file yet — a documented, correct choice for the Treatment Care Plan tile and Patient details, where pain isn't necessarily the point of a fertility/pregnancy visit. But the Musculoskeletal Assessments page (`phMskPageHtml` → `phMskPainCardHtml` → `presRvPainHtml` → `phPainMapHtml`) reuses the exact same function, so the same suppression silently hid the door on the ONE page whose whole purpose is pain tracking regardless of plan (her own 2026-10-11 "always visible" law) — and pregnancy-related MSK pain is common and clinically worth logging.
- **Fix:** the door still suppresses on a cyclish plan everywhere EXCEPT while `phPmScoreCtx === plan.id` — a flag already set only for the duration of `phMskPainCardHtml`'s own call, so no new state or parameter was needed. The Care Plan tile and Patient details keep their existing quiet behaviour unchanged; only the Musculoskeletal page now offers the door on a Pregnancy/fertility plan with nothing logged yet.
- Verified live in the sandbox (synthetic Pregnancy-plan patient, no pain data, real DOM clicks through the real delegated handlers): the "+ Pain map" door renders on the Musculoskeletal page where it was previously absent; clicking it opens the real region picker (Head & neck / Shoulder & arm / Back / Chest & abdomen / Hip & leg tabs); picking "Sacroiliac joint" under Back writes a real pain entry (`plan.painMap`) and the page repaints to show the actual "Where it hurts" figure with the logged spot, confirming the full add-a-pain flow works end to end. Synthetic patient and script item deleted from the sandbox after verification.
- **Not exercised:** a real patient, her phone, confirming the Care Plan tile and Patient-details pages are still unaffected on a real render (read from source only — their call sites don't set `phPmScoreCtx`, so the logic change cannot reach them, but not independently clicked through).

## Patient Details ↔ Timeline linkage: a one-line next-milestone summary, and Open routes straight to it (her 2026-10-16 screenshot of Zoie Wynn's "ONE box" card + "i want to audit and make sure that the patient details page links with timeline page. where is the embryo transfer and egg collection tracking?", build 20261016-1005000, committed on `session-a` — LIVE on main (audited 2026-10-18: origin/main has since advanced past this build))
Audit: the Patient Details plan band's "Open" button always landed on the Care Plan tab, never Timeline, even for a Pregnancy/Natural fertility/IVF Protocol/Men's fertility plan that HAS a Timeline — so egg collection and embryo transfer dates were reachable only by opening the plan then tapping into its own Timeline sub-page, never from Patient Details directly. Asked her how to close the gap via two offered fixes; she picked **both**.
- **A Timeline line on the card** (`phPfTlLineHtml(rec, name, plan)`, new, placed right after `phTlSideHtml` since it reads the exact same `phTlModel`): one tappable Row (`.ph-pf-tlline`, inserted into `presPfPlanTileHtml` between `${pips}` and `${earlierHtml}`) showing the next OPEN (not-done) milestone — "Timeline  Partner semen analysis  *Not planned*" — sorted by `lo` (Pregnancy, week-based) or `dLo` (every other kind, date-based). Blank (`return ""`) when the plan has no Timeline (`phTlKindOf` falsy) or every item is already done — never a second description of the same dates the Timeline page itself already renders; `phTlWhenText(kind, it)` was extracted as a shared helper out of `phTlSideHtml`'s own inline closure so both read identically, never two wordings of the same date.
- **Open routes to Timeline when one exists:** `presPfPlanTileHtml` adds `data-pf-plan-tab="timeline"` to the band's own Open button (never the "Earlier" finished-plan buttons — reviewing a completed plan should still land on Care Plan, not Timeline); the `data-pf-plan-open` click handler reads that attribute and passes it through as a new third argument to `presTpInlineOpen(name, planId, tab)`, which sets `presTpMainTab = tab || null` instead of always `null`. A plan with no Timeline keeps the old behaviour exactly (no attribute, no third argument, lands on Care Plan as before).
- **CSS:** `.ph-pf-tlline` is a Row per the TAPPABLE THINGS five-shape rule (a whole-row tap target on a faint surface, not a Chip or Button — it both displays a fact and opens something on tap), matching the card's existing density (`--ph-white` background, 9px radius, `--fs-12-5`, `--ph-soft` text, `--ph-faint` for the `<em>` date, hover tints `--ph-herb-tint`). An `.over` modifier (an overdue milestone) reuses the TIMELINE WIDGET'S OWN overdue tokens (`--ph-low-tint`/`--ph-low-deep`, the same ones `.ph-tlx-st.over`/`.ph-tlx-s.over` already use) — caught and corrected a first draft that invented non-existent `--ph-warn-tint`/`--ph-warn-deep` tokens before committing, by rendering the `.over` state live and finding it painted transparent/unstyled.
- Verified live in the sandbox (synthetic IVF-plan patient, real function calls — `phPfTlLineHtml`, `presPfPlanTileHtml`, `presTpInlineOpen` — through the real `phPatientPeek`/`phTpFindPlan` data path, not a hand-typed stand-in): the Timeline line renders with the real next-open-item text and date wording; the Open button carries `data-pf-plan-tab="timeline"` only when the plan has a Timeline (confirmed both true for IVF Protocol and false for a plain Neck & shoulder plan); `presTpInlineOpen("name","planZZ1","timeline")` correctly sets `presTpMainTab` to `"timeline"`; the rendered `.ph-pf-tlline` computed style matches the card (white bg, 9px radius, 12.5px text) and the `.over` modifier, after the token fix, renders the correct amber tint/text. Synthetic patients removed from the sandbox after verification.
- **Not exercised:** a real patient, her phone, a real tap-through (only direct function calls and a static-markup render were tested — no live click dispatched through the real `data-pf-plan-open` delegated handler on a rendered profile page).

## IVF history: visible for every female patient, not gated to an IVF plan (her 2026-10-16 "i want ivf protocol to be visible for all female patients regardless of what plan theyre on", build 20261016-1006000, committed on `session-a` — LIVE on main (audited 2026-10-18: origin/main has since advanced past this build))
Previously the "IVF history" rail tab (and its fold inside `phIvfHistoryFoldHtml`) only showed once a patient had a round already logged OR was on an "IVF Protocol" plan — a woman on an unrelated plan (Neck & shoulder, say) who was also pursuing IVF elsewhere had nowhere to log a round at all until she was formally put on that plan.
- **Both gates widened with one new condition, `phIsFemale(rec)`** (the existing sex-check helper, consistent with how every other sex-gated surface in the app reads "is this patient a woman" — never a hand-rolled `rec.sex === "F"`):
  - `presAssessSections`'s rail-tab push: `phIvfCyclesSorted(peek).length || phTpPlans(name).some(p => p.templateName === "IVF Protocol") || phIsFemale(peek)`.
  - `phIvfHistoryFoldHtml`'s own early-return: `if (!rows.length && !hasPlan && !phIsFemale(rec)) return "";`.
- **Scope, deliberate:** female patients only — a male patient (or a patient with sex not yet set) still needs either an existing round or an IVF Protocol plan before the tab appears, exactly as before. Her words named "all female patients," not "everyone"; a man's own fertility tracking already has its own separate Men's fertility plan/timeline (`mens_fert`), untouched by this change, and a sex-not-set patient wasn't asked for.
- **Nothing about the data model, the add buttons, the frozen bank, or the Timeline wiring changed** — a female patient with nothing logged just now sees "Nothing logged yet." + "+ Egg collection" where she used to see no tab at all; everything downstream (rounds, transfers, the frozen-bank tally, the Timeline page reading it) is the same code that already worked for an IVF-plan patient.
- Verified live in the sandbox (synthetic patients, real function calls through `presAssessSections`/`phIvfHistoryFoldHtml`, not a hand-typed stand-in): a female patient on a Neck & shoulder plan with no IVF rows now gets the "ivf" rail tab, and the expanded fold shows "Nothing logged yet." + a working "+ Egg collection" button; a male patient on the same plan shape still gets no tab; a sex-unset patient still gets no tab. Synthetic patients removed from the sandbox after verification.
- **Not exercised:** a real patient, her phone, a real tap through the rendered rail (only direct function calls were tested).

## Cycle page: the Day log and Usual period cards share one group system (her 2026-10-16, screenshots of both cards + "these two essential measure same chips - redesign the chips to group them and make the page less redundant. show mock", build 20261016-1007000, committed on `session-a` — LIVE on main (audited 2026-10-18: origin/main has since advanced past this build))
Mock `cycle-chips-grouped-mock-2026-10-16.html` (sent as an image before asking, per her standing "open visuals before asking" rule), plus a second comparison panel proving a joined bar breaks for a tick-several row (a picked chip that wraps alone stretches full-width) vs. loose chips, which don't. Two rounds of `AskUserQuestion` (4 each, all 8 answers **Recommended**): shared group headers Bleeding / Discomfort / Signs & mood, same order on both cards; the Day log's LH/Sex/Other-twinge trio keeps its own 4th "Other signs" group (nothing to match on Usual); PMS (and any tick-several row) stays loose chips, never a joined bar; Day log's Pain stays two rows (0-10 slider + Pain type); BBT gets its own small group after Bleeding; scope = the full Cycle page's two cards only, not the smaller popovers elsewhere; long bars wrap to a second line on a narrow card, never scroll; both cards built in one pass.
- **Usual period** (`PH_PFW_SETS.period.qs`, `presPfwSetHtml`): each question now carries an opt-in `grp` field ("Bleeding"/"Discomfort"/"Signs & mood"); the array was reordered so same-group questions sit consecutively (len→flow→col→clot, then pain→cm, then limbs→pms — was len→flow→pain→cm→clot→col→limbs→pms). `presPfwSetHtml` walks `set.qs` and prints a `.ph-pfw-grphd` header whenever `q.grp` changes from the previous row; a question with no `grp` (every OTHER set — meno/men/pain) renders exactly as before, no headers, fully backward compatible.
- **Day log** (`phCycleSignPopHtml`'s `opts.flat` branch — the Cycle page's own "All signs" full card; confirmed via live render this is the ONLY branch her screenshot came from): rows reordered and grouped the same way — Bleeding (Flow, Colour, Clots) → BBT (its own group, `phCycBbtRowHtml`'s flat row now carries `full` so it doesn't pair oddly with an unrelated field) → Discomfort (Pain slider, Pain type, Discharge) → Signs & mood (PMS, unchanged loose chips) → Other signs (LH, Sex, Ovulation twinge) → Note. Group headers reuse the SAME `.ph-pfw-grphd` class as Usual period (one shared visual language, not a second near-duplicate style), printed via a local `grphd()` helper.
- **Joined bars, scoped narrowly:** `phCycleSelectChips` gained an opt-in 7th param `seg` — only the Day log's pick-one rows (Flow, Colour, Clots, Pain type, Discharge, LH) pass `true`; every other caller of this shared function (the smaller `.cp-box` day-tap popover, `.cp-foot`, every other page that reuses it) is untouched, matching her "full Cycle page only, for now" scope exactly. New CSS is deliberately specific enough (`.ph-cp .cp-card .cp-log.ph-cycle-signpop .ph-cs-grid .ph-ck-chips.seg`) to win on specificity alone regardless of source order. **Fixed the exact bug the mock's own comparison panel demonstrated**: a naive `flex: 1 1 0` (equal-stretch) joined bar makes a lone wrapped chip stretch full-width; the real build uses `flex: 0 1 auto` (shrink-to-content) instead, so a wrapped chip keeps its natural width — verified live (a picked "Dark red" chip in a 3-line-wrapped Colour bar measured 77px, not stretched).
- Verified live in the sandbox (synthetic patients, real function calls, markup injected into the real `#pharmacyPage` DOM — not `document.body`, which skips every `#pharmacyPage`-scoped rule, a mistake caught and fixed mid-verification — then read back via `getComputedStyle`, since the sandbox's sign-in overlay blocks an actual screenshot): Usual period shows the 3 headers in order, `meno` renders with zero headers (confirming the opt-in design doesn't touch other question sets); the Day log shows all 5 groups in order with the flow-specific colour override still correctly winning inside a `.seg` chip; PMS stays loose (`flex-wrap:wrap`, no border); the small `.cp-box` popover has zero groups and zero `.seg` (scope held); a 340px-wide card wraps the 6-option Colour bar to 3 lines with no stretch bug. Test patients removed after verification.
- **Not exercised:** a real patient, her phone, an actual pixel screenshot (blocked by the sandbox's sign-in overlay — computed-style verification was used instead, per this file's own documented trap about headless/overlay-blocked sandboxes).

## Breech correction: a standalone treatment plan, separate from Pregnancy (her 2026-10-16 "make treatment plan for breech" -> "a standalone breech plan, separate from Pregnancy", build 20261016-1009000, committed on `session-a` — LIVE on main (audited 2026-10-18: origin/main has since advanced past this build))
Breech already had a drafted treatment arc, but only as the LAST phase of the Pregnancy plan (`PH_TP_PHASE.breech`) — a patient coming in just for breech correction, not otherwise on a full Pregnancy plan with her, had no way to start it on its own. Asked her to confirm what she actually wanted (she'd asked before I knew it already existed); she picked a standalone plan.
- **New template `breech_correction`** (`PH_TP_TEMPLATES`, right after `pregnancy`), category `gynae`, `caseHint: "fertility"` — same category/caseHint as Pregnancy, Natural fertility and IVF Protocol, so it needed no new Case type, category, colour or message-tracking entry; it sits in the picker beside them and under the same "Fertility / IVF" complaint tile (`PH_CC_FAMS.fertility.plans` gained `breech_correction`).
- **Two phases**, reusing the existing `breech` phase content verbatim (same precedent as `early` being shared across Natural fertility/IVF/Pregnancy — a phase key isn't owned by one template) plus one new phase, `breechReview` ("Review & confirm": confirm the baby's position with her care provider, repeat the course if still breech and before 36–37wk, hold if turned; ECV/delivery decisions explicitly left to her and the care provider). `PH_TP_DEFAULT_SESSIONS.breech_correction = [3, 1]` (matches the existing phase's own "2–3 sessions across a week" cadence note). Both phases' wording is Claude-drafted, DRAFT, hers to correct.
- **Not duplicated, not touched:** the Pregnancy plan's own 5-phase list (…, breech) and the existing "+ Add phase" breech detour on an IN-PROGRESS Pregnancy plan (`phTpDetourKindsFor`/`PH_TP_DETOUR_META.breech`) are both untouched — two different doors onto related content, not two copies of it.
- **Not built, disclosed:** no Timeline kind for this plan (unlike Pregnancy/Natural/IVF/Men's fertility) — a short, few-session standalone correction course doesn't carry the kind of dated milestones (scans, bloods) a Timeline tracks; if she wants position-check dates tracked there, that's a follow-up, not assumed here.
- Verified live via a scratch local server + the Browser pane (`window.eval` reads of the real `PH_TP_TEMPLATES`/`PH_CC_FAMS`/`PH_TP_DEFAULT_SESSIONS`, not a hand-typed stand-in): the template resolves with the right name/category/caseHint, both phases carry their default session counts (3, 1), and the fertility tile's plan list includes it; no new console errors (the one error present is the known offline-sandbox external-script-fetch limitation, unrelated).
- **Not exercised:** a real patient, her phone, actually opening the new plan through a real tap on the picker (only direct `window.eval` reads were done — no DOM click was dispatched).

## Fertility timeline: real sync with IVF history (her 2026-10-16 screenshot + "sync timeline with ivf history, especially for blood test, transfer, egg collection etc. sync it and make it cohesive", build 20261016-1010000, committed on `session-a` — LIVE on main (audited 2026-10-18: origin/main has since advanced past this build))
Her screenshot (IVF Protocol Timeline, Checklist view): Baseline scan and bloods, Stimulation scans, Egg collection, Fertilisation report, Embryo transfer and Heartbeat scan ALL showed "Not planned" with only manual Schedule/Mark-done — except Pregnancy blood test, which correctly showed "Done 16 Oct · Positive blood · hCG 205". Investigated rather than guessed: this was a real bug, not a missing feature or a quirk of her data.
- **Root cause (`phIvfLinkedCollection`):** the Timeline only ever reads an IVF-history round that carries `linkedPlanId`/`linkedRound` — but those fields are ONLY ever stamped by the plan's own Cycle-page date widget (`phIvfLinkedCollectionWritable`, reached from `data-ivfd-mark`). A round logged the ORDINARY way — IVF history's own "+ Egg collection"/"+ Transfer" buttons, which is how she actually logs a round — never gets linked, so `base`/`stim` (scans, via `phIvfScanList`), `opu` (egg collection), `et` (embryo transfer), and anything derived from them (`fert`, `beta`'s date filter, `hb`'s estimate window) all read nothing. The one item that DID work, `beta` (pregnancy blood test), uses a separate reader (`phTlPregTestRead`) with no link check at all — the same "just read her latest round" pattern already used for a partner's view of her timeline (`phTlPtnData`).
- **Fix, scoped to the read side only (never touches the write-path widget, which is unchanged and still correct):** `phIvfLinkedCollection` now falls back to the patient's own latest NOT-explicitly-linked round (excluding a `freeze`-kind round, same exclusion `phTlPtnData`'s precedent already uses) whenever nothing is explicitly linked to this exact plan+round — an explicitly linked round still always wins, so a patient on her SECOND IVF attempt (a new plan, an old already-linked round from the first attempt) is never misattributed. Verified live: an old round linked to nothing and a properly-linked round present together correctly surface the LINKED one, not the fallback.
- **Fertilisation report wired for the first time:** it previously only ever showed an estimated "retrieval + 1 day" with no way to mark it Done from real data, even though the round already captures `fertilised`/`day3`/`blastocysts` (the same fields the round's own conversion-funnel display reads). Now shows "7 fertilised · 6 on day 3 · 4 blastocysts" automatically once those numbers are on the round, confirmed (not estimated) the same way Egg collection's eggs-count already was.
- **Heartbeat scan is a genuine data gap, not fixed here, disclosed:** nothing in IVF history captures a heartbeat-scan date or result at all (scans only cover pre-transfer monitoring — follicle sizes, lining, hormone levels). With the link fixed it now at least shows the right ESTIMATED window (transfer + 30 days, "~15 Nov") instead of nothing, but marking it Done still needs a manual tap — there is no field anywhere to read a real result from. Adding one is new schema work, not asked for here.
- **Not duplicated, not touched:** the Cycle-page date widget (`phIvfLinkedCollectionWritable`) is completely unchanged — it still always creates/targets a properly linked round when she types a date there. A patient who logs a round through IVF history and THEN also types a date through the widget could end up with two rounds (the fallback-matched one and a newly linked one) — a pre-existing risk this fix doesn't introduce or worsen, since the widget never looked at unlinked rounds either way; flagged rather than silently "fixed" with a riskier write-side auto-link.
- Verified live via a scratch local server + the Browser pane, real `phTlModel` calls (not a hand-typed stand-in): a synthetic IVF-plan patient with a single UNLINKED round (12 eggs, 9 mature, 7 fertilised, 6 on day 3, 4 blastocysts, 2 PGT-normal, 2 scans, a positive-result transfer) now shows every item Done with real data — base/stim from the real scan readings, opu "12 eggs", fert "7 fertilised · 6 on day 3 · 4 blastocysts", et the real transfer date, beta unchanged, hb showing the correct estimated window. A second check (an old unlinked round + a properly linked round both present) confirmed the linked round still wins. No new console errors.
- **Not exercised:** a real patient, her phone, a real tap through the rendered Timeline page (only direct `phTlModel` calls were run).

## Tongue photo viewer: Sublingual and Flash as their own fixed sections (her 2026-10-16 screenshot of the Tongue Examine page + "add another row below photos for sublingual and flash" -> mid-turn "show the chips related e.g. coat tongue for tongue row, sublingual for sublingual row", build 20261016-1013000, committed on `session-a` — LIVE on main (audited 2026-10-18: origin/main has since advanced past this build))
Full ask28 cycle: a mock built and iterated (`tongue-more-angles-mock-2026-10-16.html`, headless-Edge screenshots `tongue-more-angles-a/b/b2.png`), 10 questions answered across 3 rounds (4+4+2), all Recommended except her explicit "B · Full Today/Last pairs" pick over the compact Option A. Her mid-turn clarification (chips grouped by their own row, not one shared panel) was folded into the mock and re-confirmed before any real code was touched, per her standing mock-before-building workflow.
- **The TOP switch-driven panel's chip set is now kind-specific** (`phTngCmpHtml`): `kind==="t"` shows Tongue body (Colour, Shape·Moist + the More fold: Thickness/Deviation/Marks) and Coat (Coat thickness/colour + the More fold's Coat quality) — Under is no longer shown here at all. `kind==="sub"` shows ONLY Under, with no More fold (nothing else to fold). The switch itself, its full Today/Last comparison and its manual Past-photos picker/strip are completely unchanged — her explicit "keep it exactly as it is" answer.
- **Two new FIXED sections below, always rendered regardless of the switch** (`phTngCmpFixedSectionHtml`, new): a Sublingual section (always-latest Today/Last pair, no picker, Under chips beside it) and a Flash section (always-latest pair, no picker, no chips — an explanatory `.ph-tc-flashnote` instead, DRAFT wording). "Always latest" needed no new logic: `phTngCmpPick(name, kind, null)` with no `selId` already falls back to `last` by itself, which is exactly "the latest one on file" — the fixed sections just call it with `null`.
- **`phTngCmpPick` gained a third kind, `"flash"`**, via a new `PH_TNG_CMP_SHOT_MATCH` lookup (`t`/`sub`/`flash`); `kind==="t"`'s own matcher was narrowed to exclude BOTH sublingual and flash (it only excluded sublingual before), so a day with only a flash shot can no longer hijack the main Tongue row's "today" pick.
