## "How long" everywhere, plus month/year precision (her 2026-10-18: "based on the how long button change, do the same for all how long buttons in the app" then "allow me to put in 08/2026 or just 2026"; committed on `session-a` — LIVE on main (confirmed ancestor of origin/main as of the 2026-10-18 audit), build 20261018-1130000)
Follows directly from the Pain table's "one combobox" build above. Two asks in one session: apply the same datalist-combobox pattern to every other "how long" mechanism in the app, then extend the shared parser to also accept a bare year or "mm/yyyy" when she doesn't know the exact day.
- **Three mechanisms found app-wide, all converted:** the Pain table (already done above), the Came in for card's chief/secondary-complaint since-editor (had its own separate `<select>` + a Day/Month/Year precision bar built earlier the same day), and the Treatment table's symptom-row Started-date editor (`phTsSinceEditHtml`, a 7-chip row — Today / 5 durations / First assessment — plus a separate free-text box). **Deliberately scoped OUT**, not matching this instruction's shape: the Usual-period cycle-length range question and the contraception duration amount+unit toggle (neither is a quick-pick-plus-date-box).
- **Shared parser, one pair of functions, used everywhere:** `phCcParseDatePrec(t)` returns `{iso, prec, est}` or `null` — a duration ("2 yr") still resolves via the existing `phCcParseDate` with `prec:"d", est:true`; a bare 4-digit year (1900–this year) gives `prec:"y", est:false`; "mm/yyyy" gives `prec:"m", est:false`; anything else falls through to `phCcParseDate` for a full date, `prec:"d", est:false`. `phCcFmtDatePrec(s, prec)` is its display twin (year alone / "mm/yyyy" / full "dd/mm/yyyy"). Both sit right after the existing `phCcParseDate`/`phCcFmtDate` pair and are reused by the Pain table, Came-in-for's day box, and nowhere else yet (the Treatment table's since-editor was a disclosed, deliberate scope cut — see below).
- **Pain table**: a new `sincePrec` field on each pain entry (`p.sincePrec`, `"m"`/`"y"`, absent = day), added to `phPmWrite`'s write-whitelist (the documented past trap — a field left off that list is silently dropped on the next write) alongside the pre-existing `dx`/`main`/`causes` protections. The since box's `change` handler now reads through `phCcParseDatePrec` and stores both `since` (always the ISO date) and `sincePrec`; the box's displayed value uses `phCcFmtDatePrec(p.since, p.sincePrec)` so a year-only or month-only entry is never expanded into a fake full date. Deliberately did NOT re-normalize the box's text immediately after a typed duration or datalist pick (e.g. typing "2 yr" still shows "2 yr" until the next full repaint) — matches the pre-existing behaviour, a self-caught scope-discipline correction rather than a new feature.
- **Came in for card**: the day box's own separate `<select>` + "Custom" chip (built earlier the same day, alongside the Day/Month/Year precision bar) is gone — the day box itself now carries `list="phCcSinceList"`, the identical mechanism. Its `change` handler reads through `phCcParseDatePrec` too, so typing "2026" or "08/2026" directly into the day box works without switching to the Month/Year tab first; `phCcMainSinceSet`/`phCcSecSinceSet` (which already take a `prec` argument from the earlier precision-bar build) needed no changes — confirmed by reading them, not assumed.
- **Treatment table (`phTsSinceEditHtml`)**: the old 7-chip quick-pick row is gone; the dd/mm/yyyy box now carries `list="phTsSinceList"` with a `<datalist>` of `["Today", ...5 of PH_CC_QUICK, "First assessment"]` (same options the chips used to offer). The now-dead `act === "since-q"` branch in the click dispatcher was removed. The `data-ts-since-in` `change` handler now special-cases the literal strings "today" and "first assessment" (case-insensitive) before falling through to `phCcParseDatePrec`, taking only `.iso` and discarding `.prec`/`.est` — **a disclosed, deliberate scope cut**: this field has no existing precision-storage equivalent to `sincePrec` (its `rec.tri`-backed store only ever held a resolved date), and adding one was judged a separate design decision (parallel precision store vs. silently dropping it) rather than something to improvise mid-build; she can ask for it explicitly if the month/year typing matters here too.
- Verified live via a real dev-server preview + direct calls to the real, unmodified functions (not reimplemented stand-ins): `phCcParseDatePrec`/`phCcFmtDatePrec` resolve all four shapes correctly (year, mm/yyyy, duration, full date) and correctly reject an out-of-range year/month; `phTsSinceEditHtml` renders with `list="phTsSinceList"` and all 7 datalist options including "Today" and "First assessment"; `phCcTableHtml`'s rendered output carries the `phCcSinceList` datalist with all 11 `PH_SINCE_OPTS`; the app booted with zero new console errors after every edit (the dead `since-q` removal, the Treatment-table change-handler rewrite). Confirmed via grep that no reference to the old `data-pm-qsel`/`data-cc-main-qsel`/`data-cc-sec-qsel`/`act === "since-q"` branches remains anywhere in the file.
- **Not exercised:** a real patient, her phone, a real tap through any of the three native browser datalist pickers themselves, a real screenshot, the Treatment table's "Today"/"First assessment" special-casing through an actual dispatched `change` event (verified by reading the handler and by the shared-parser unit checks above, not by a live click).

## Chip groups: no side bracket, joined into a bar (her 2026-10-18 screenshot of the Usual Period card's Flow/Clots/Pain rows + "fix it now"; LIVE on main, build 20261018-1150000)
Her exact words: "i do not like this design of solid line on side of the chip, and that the chips are not according to my preference for chip groups." First documented as a dated flag in the TAPPABLE THINGS section (search "Chip groups: no side bracket" above), then built on her explicit "fix it now".
- **Identified the real screen first, not guessed twice.** The screenshot's FLOW / CLOTS / PAIN labels + exact option wording ("Cramping, Stabbing, Severe, Dragging, Eased by warmth, Skip") matched `PH_PFW_SETS.period.qs` word for word — this is the **Usual Period card** (`presPfwSetHtml`), not the Cycle day log's "flat" card the 2026-10-16 segmented-bar audit round 2 had separately flagged and left unconverted (that one uses a different chip family, `.ph-ck-chip`/`phCycleSelectChips`, untouched by this build). Confirmed before editing anything, since the two screens share similar vocabulary but different markup.
- **The side bracket** was `.ph-pfw-q.done { box-shadow: inset 3px 0 0 var(--ph-herb); }` — a 3px solid teal (crimson on the gynae-themed Cycle page) bar on the left edge of every ANSWERED question row, shared by every screen built on `presPfwSetHtml` (Usual Period, meno/men's sets, Pain intake) and, before 2026-10-16, Find the Ren too (already stripped there on her earlier "i dont like the side box" ask — see the `.ph-rq` override just above it in the stylesheet). The rule is now gone from the shared base entirely, not scoped to one screen — an answered row needs no separate mark of its own; the picked chip(s) already fill solid. **A second, harder-to-spot copy of the same bar also lived inside the "just answered" settle-flash animation** (`@keyframes phPfwSettle`, which faded FROM the identical `inset 3px 0 0 var(--ph-herb), 0 0 0 2px var(--ph-herb)` down to transparent) — found only by waiting out the .7s animation in a live render and comparing the resting state against the mid-flash state; fixed the keyframe to fade a plain background tint only, no line, no ring. A one-off duplicate of the dead `.done` rule (same selector, same declaration, sitting near unrelated Quick-assess CSS) was removed too. The `.cur`/`.done.cur` gold RING (`0 0 0 2px`, a symmetric outline, not a one-sided bar) was left completely alone — that's a different, legitimate "this is the current question" indicator, not the side-line she named.
- **The joined bar**: `.ph-pfw-opts` (the one wrapper div every question's own option buttons, including Skip, already share — no markup change needed, just CSS) now renders as a single bordered, no-gap bar (`border: 1px solid var(--ph-line); border-radius: 10px; overflow: hidden`, matching the Day log's own `.ph-ck-chips.seg` 10px-corner treatment rather than a true pill, since a question can carry 6-7 options and will wrap to a second line at a normal card width — a true 999px pill reads oddly once wrapped). Each `.ph-pfw-opt` inside flattens to `border-radius: 0` with a 1px divider on its right edge (`.ph-pfw-opts > .ph-pfw-opt:last-child` drops its own divider); picked state is unchanged (already filled solid `--ph-herb-deep` with no shadow). This reuses the exact `.ph-pfw-opts`/`.ph-pfw-opt` classes already in place — no new component, no markup edit, CSS-only.
- **Find the Ren explicitly excluded, on purpose.** Its own Look-card rows (`phRqCardHtml`) reuse these same two classes, but she separately confirmed on 2026-10-16 ("left alone, as asked") that those chips should stay loose individual pills, not join a bar. A scoped override (`.ph-rq .ph-pfw-opts`/`.ph-rq .ph-pfw-opts > .ph-pfw-opt`) restores the original 4px-gap, 999px-pill, inset-border look there, same specificity-override technique already used for the `.done`/`.cur` exception right beside it. Grepped to confirm `.ph-pfw-opts` has exactly two renderers in the whole file (`presPfwSetHtml` and `phRqCardHtml`) — nothing else silently caught by either rule.
- **Trap hit mid-build, documented so it isn't re-hit:** a CSS `transition` on `box-shadow` (the base `.ph-pfw-q` rule already carries `transition: background .25s, box-shadow .25s`) does not reliably settle to its end value when a class is toggled via script in this sandbox's Browser pane and read back immediately or after a `setTimeout` — `getComputedStyle` can report a stale or mid-transition value with no warning, the same family of issue this file's own Traps section already documents for `requestAnimationFrame` in a hidden/background tab. Worked around by rendering the row with the target class already baked into the initial HTML (no transition to begin with) rather than toggling classes post-hoc and waiting.
- Verified live via a real dev-server preview + the real, unmodified `presPfwSetHtml`/`phRqCardHtml` (not reimplemented stand-ins), on a synthetic patient: the Usual Period Flow row's `.ph-pfw-opts` computes to a real 1px bordered, 10px-radius, 0-gap bar; its picked chip computes `border-radius: 0`; the Pain row's 7 chips (6 options + Skip) show a 1px divider on every chip but the last; a `.done` row's resting (post-animation) box-shadow computes `none`; the `.cur` row (rendered with the class present from the start, avoiding the transition trap above) still shows the correct gold ring; Find the Ren's own rows, rendered through the real `phRqCardHtml`, still compute a 0-border `.ph-pfw-opts` wrapper, 4px gap, and 999px-radius inset-bordered individual chips — unchanged. Zero new console errors throughout.
- **Not exercised:** a real patient, her phone, a real screenshot, a real tap (every check above read computed styles on a script-rendered, script-inserted synthetic row — not a live click through the delegated handler).

## Abdomen exam diagram: realistic organic anatomy, bigger, animated (her 2026-10-19 "improve the abdominal charting page... too small and doesn't feel intuitive" → mid-build "make the abdomen diagram more realistic" → her "looks right, build and push" on the approved mock, `abdomen-redesign-mock.html`; build 20261019-1210000)
Her chain of asks: enlarge and animate the existing 9-box abdomen chart (`phAbdChartSvg`) first, then — mid-build, on seeing the first pass — make the torso diagram itself (not just its size) anatomically realistic. Mocked standalone, iterated with headless-Edge screenshots, approved with "looks right, build and push" — the explicit authorization both to port the mock into the real app and to push it live.
- **New organic geometry, module-level, right before `phAbdChartSvg`:** `PH_ABD_CX`, `phAbdHw(y)` (piecewise-linear half-width over 5 control points — an hourglass/hip-flare silhouette, not a trapezoid), `phAbdSkinL`/`phAbdSkinR`, `phAbdArchY` (generic arch-curve generator) driving `phAbdTopGridY`/`phAbdCostalY`/`phAbdIliacY`/`phAbdBotGridY` — the 4 horizontal zone-row boundaries; `phAbdCostalY`/`phAbdIliacY` are ALSO the two visible "bone" landmark lines drawn on the torso (same functions, so a zone and the rib-arch/pelvic-brim line it's named after can never drift apart), `PH_ABD_DIVL`/`PH_ABD_DIVR` (fixed vertical dividers from a `PH_ABD_COLFRAC=.34` fraction of the half-width at the umbilicus row), `phAbdArcControl`/`phAbdEdgePath` (quadratic-bezier curve builders through 3 sampled points), `PH_ABD_OVER=1.3` (a small overlap fudge on every zone edge so no seam gap ever shows), `PH_ABD_ZPOS`/`PH_ABD_ROW_Y`/`phAbdZoneGeom` (resolves each of the 9 zone ids to a real curved `<path>` + its label anchor).
- **`phAbdChartSvg` rewritten, mechanisms unchanged:** zones are now `<path>`s built from `phAbdZoneGeom` instead of `<rect>`s on a fixed grid; a hand-tuned organic belly silhouette (`outerD`) replaces the old trapezoid outline; a radial gradient (`#phAbd2Shade`) gives it soft shading; faint "anatomy under the skin" organ shapes (liver, stomach, colon, bladder, linea) reuse the exact treatment the Ren figure's own `phRenAnatomyHtml` already established; the costal/iliac "bone" landmark lines are drawn from the same geometry functions that cut the zones. Every existing mechanism is untouched: `data-visit-zone`/`data-visit-abd2` attributes (only when `!ro`), `esc()` escaping, `.on`/`.legacy` classes, `phAbdZoneLabel`/`PH_ABD_ZONE_SHORT`, `ro` read-only mode, all 7 glyph types (water/gas/pulsation/tender/xiaxin/bulge/sunk) with identical trigger logic, just reading zone positions from the new `Z[k]` geometry instead of the old fixed grid. The viewBox is now a literal `"0 0 110 140"` — deliberately NOT touching `PH_LTR_ZONES.abd.vb`, which a separate ellipse-based diagram renderer elsewhere in the file still reads.
- **Disclosed adaptation, fill-opacity:** `fillOp` is new — `.88` once a zone has a real tension reading, `.48` on the neutral/untouched default — so an unpicked zone reads as faint background rather than a flat opaque block, consistent with the mock's layered organic look. Not asked for explicitly; a reasonable extension of "make it look realistic," disclosed here for her to correct if she'd rather every zone stay fully opaque.
- **CSS, three edits:** (1) desktop figure enlarged 200×240px → 280×356px, with a new scoped `#pharmacyPage .ph-tng2.abd .ph-tng2-left { flex: 0 0 480px; }` — the shared `.ph-tng2-left`'s fixed 400px flex-basis (also used by the Tongue page) would have been overflowed by the bigger figure + the existing 176px photo tile + gap, so the Abdomen page gets its own wider left column via its existing `.ph-tng2.abd` scoping hook, never touching the shared base rule Tongue also reads. (2) interaction polish on `.ph-abd2-zone` (`cursor: pointer`, a `transition` on fill/filter/transform, a new `:active { transform: scale(.97) }` press-state) plus a new `@keyframes phAbd2Breathe` infinite slow pulse-opacity animation on `.ph-abd2-pulsering` (the pulsation glyph's ring, which used to be a static `opacity=".6"` attribute) — both disabled under `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`, the app's standing rule for every animation. (3) phone breakpoint enlarged 136×163px → 150×191px, matching the desktop proportion.
- **Deliberately NOT built — a one-shot "pop"/ripple animation on tap** (the mock had one): adding it would need either an unconditional mount keyframe on the base `.ph-abd2-zone` class — the exact "unconditional entrance animation replaying on every scoped repaint" anti-pattern this file's own app-wide audit (2026-10-17, "when I select chips, the screen flashes") documents fixing elsewhere — or new JS state-tracking plumbing to gate a one-shot class per tap, judged too risky/slow to get right safely in this pass. The `:active` press-scale plus the `:hover`/transition polish were judged the safe subset; the breathing pulsation ring (an existing, already-safe infinite-loop precedent, `.ph-osm-row.glow`) was kept.
- **Dead CSS left in place, disclosed, per the project's own established convention of batching CSS cleanup into a separate sweep:** `.ph-abd2-guide` (the old dashed overlay, no longer emitted by the new geometry) and `.ph-abd2-bone.faint` (no longer emitted — one solid line per landmark now) both remain, unused.
- **Verified:** structurally, via direct `phAbdChartSvg`/`phVisitAbdHtml` calls against the real window-exposed functions in this sandbox (the sign-in overlay blocks a real screenshot of the signed-in app, the documented sandbox limitation) — zero JS errors, correct DOM (9 zones, correct labels, correct `.on`/`.legacy`/`data-visit-zone` application for a soft default, an active umbilical zone, a hard right-hypochondrium, every glyph type present with correct fill colours/opacities, correct viewBox, correct read-only suppression of interactive attributes in `ro` mode). Visually, via a headless-Edge screenshot of the exact real, extracted SVG markup + the real CSS rules written into a standalone verification file (the established technique for this sandbox): confirmed the hourglass silhouette, costal/iliac arcs aligned with zone boundaries, faint organ shading, and all 7 glyph types reading correctly in position and colour.
- **Not exercised:** a real patient, her phone, a real screenshot of the signed-in app, the `:active` press-scale and the pulsation breathing animation actually animating (static screenshot only confirms the rule exists and the keyframe is well-formed, not live motion), the `prefers-reduced-motion` override actually suppressing them on a real device.

## Saved-toast ("Filed as …") text unreadable: a missing colour fallback, not a style guess (her 2026-10-19 screenshot of a Records → Photos label-toast, "tis is hard to read", build 20261019-1260000)
Her screenshot: a dark teal pill reading "Filed as Skin  Undo" — "Undo" crisp white, the message text dim/near-illegible against the dark background.
- **Root cause, traced, not guessed:** `presSavedToast` (`.ph-saved-toast`, THE one body-level toast since the 2026-10-15 "One toast" audit) is appended with `document.body.appendChild(el)` — genuinely OUTSIDE `#pharmacyPage`'s DOM subtree. Its CSS sets `color: var(--ph-cream)` with **no fallback value**, but `--ph-cream` is only ever defined inside the `#pharmacyPage { ... }` rule block (line ~358), never on bare `:root`. A CSS custom property is scoped to the DOM subtree of the selector that defines it — at a body-level element outside `#pharmacyPage`, `var(--ph-cream)` is invalid at computed-value time, so `color` (an inherited property) silently falls through to whatever `<body>`'s own ambient text colour is, rather than the intended near-white `#EEF5F7`. The pill's `background: var(--ph-green-deep, #0B3B4B)` on the very same line already carries a fallback, so the dark background always rendered correctly — only the text colour was silently broken, exactly matching "Undo" staying crisp (its colour is hardcoded `#fff`) while the message dimmed.
- **Fix, one token:** `color: var(--ph-cream)` → `color: var(--ph-cream, #EEF5F7)` — the same fallback pattern the background declaration on the same line already uses.
- **Verified before shipping:** built a standalone reproduction (the real CSS declaration, both the buggy and fixed versions, with `--ph-cream` scoped to a sibling `#pharmacyPage` block the toast sits outside of, matching the real DOM relationship exactly) and screenshotted via headless Edge — the "before" render reproduced her exact symptom (dim, low-contrast message text, crisp white Undo); the "after" render showed both at full, matching contrast.
- **Concurrent-session care, disclosed:** a second session had uncommitted Care Plan page work ("simplify the full Care Plan page") sitting in the same `index.html` at the time. Extracted only this fix's own 2 hunks (the colour fallback + the routine meta-build-tag bump) via a `-U3` context patch applied with `git apply --cached`, verified via `git diff --cached` vs. the remaining `git diff`, and committed with no paths — their work was left untouched in the working tree, not swept in.
- **Not exercised:** a real patient, her phone, a post-push screenshot of the signed-in app. This fix applies to every `presSavedToast`/`phCycToast` call app-wide (saves, copies, Undo bars, the Records → Photos labelling flow) — all share the one `.ph-saved-toast` rule, so none of them needed a separate fix.

## Findings trend: Grid/Trail table of dated Tongue and Abdomen findings (her ask, full mock/review cycle, final "Yes, build it"; mock `findings-trend-table-mock.html`; build 20261019-1260100)
A way to visually track Tongue and Abdomen clinical findings across visit dates in table format, so changes/trends are visible at a glance. Her confirmed picks from the review (not re-asked): Grid default (date columns, newest left, mirroring her own Photos Comparison table precedent), a Grid|Trail toggle, a door on EACH exam page into ONE shared sheet, everything on file (no date cutoff), a changed cell opens that dated visit, a diagram row above the word rows drawn by the real renderers — never a second, hand-drawn tongue/abdomen shape.
- **One new sheet, `phFtOpen`/`phFtCardHtml`/`phFtRender`/`phFtClose`** (module-level `phFtPatient`/`phFtExam`/`phFtView`, reset on close, nothing written to `rec`), cloned 1:1 from the app's own shared `.ph-lk-sheet`/`.ph-lk-card` overlay shell and open/close/Esc/backdrop-tap pattern (copied from `phFbrAllRefOpen`/`phFbrAllRefClose`/`phFbrAllRefRender`, the "All formulas & criteria" sheet). The Abdomen|Tongue tab and the Grid|Trail switch both reuse the exam pages' own `.ph-tng2-seg`/`.ph-ltr-qual` segmented-chip-bar (her confirmed 2026-10-16 standard for one-category chip groups) — no new switch component.
- **Data, read-only, reused not reinvented:** `phFtDates(rec, kind)` walks `rec.acuSessions`, reads each dated `rec.visitFnd[s.id]` through the real `phVisitFndRead` (the same accessor the exam pages, Visit checks (`presVisitChecksHtml`) and the Care Plan's session folds already use), and adds today's standing `rec.tongueExam`/`rec.abdomen.chart` when nothing has been logged to a session yet today — newest first, everything on file, no cutoff. `phFtTngVals`/`phFtAbdVals` group the real field names `phTng2Read`/`phAbd2Read` already define (Tongue: `colour`/`colourMore`/`colourAt`, `shape`+`thickness`+`deviation`, `moist`, `coatThick`/`coatColour`/`coatQual`, `under`, `marks`; Abdomen: `tension`/`tensionAt`, and `marks.cold`/`warm`/`water`/`gas`/`tender`/`xiaxin`/`pulsation`/`bulging`/`sunken`/`tightrect`/`distended`/`thinwall`) into the six Tongue rows and four Abdomen rows her mock named — nothing invented, nothing hand-typed.
- **The real renderers, not a second drawing:** the Diagram row (Grid's top row, Trail's first track) calls `phTongueChartSvg(r.tng, null, {ro:true})` / `phAbdChartSvg(r.abd, null, {ro:true, legacy:r.zones})` directly, wrapped in the exact `.ph-visit-tng.ro.mini`/`.ph-visit-abd-map.ro.mini` classes the Visit checks table's own mini-charts already use (`presVisitChecksHtml`) — the same CSS sizing, no new size rule invented.
- **"Changed" cells:** `phFtEq` compares a row's value array (order-independent) against the visit immediately before it (Grid: `dates[i+1]`; Trail, reversed to oldest-first: `dates[i-1]`); only flags a change when BOTH the cell and its predecessor are non-empty (an empty-to-filled or filled-to-empty transition is never "changed," matching the mock). The ring reuses the existing, non-reserved `--ph-purple`/`-deep`/`-tint` token family (already used elsewhere for a different "T-test" category marker) rather than inventing a new accent — gold stays reserved for status per the house rule.
- **Tap-to-open, reusing the app's own navigation, not a new one:** a populated cell with a real logged session (`hasSession:true`) renders as a `<button data-flow-prof="checks">` — the exact existing attribute/handler that already routes to the Visit checks tab (`presAssessTab = "checks"`); today's own standing draft (no session logged yet) renders as a plain, non-interactive cell instead, since there is nothing dated to "jump to" — she is already looking at it on the exam page the door was opened from.
- **Doors:** `phExamPageHtml` (the shared wrapper for the Tongue/Pulse/Abdomen exam pages) grows one conditional row, `id === "tongue" || id === "abdomen"` only (never Pulse) — an outlined `.ph-rtng-act` button (the existing 6px-corner "act" button shape, already used for "Change type"/"Change date" on the Ren photo viewer) with the sprite's existing `#i-chart` icon, defaulting the sheet's tab to whichever page it opened from.
- **Disclosed judgment calls, her instructions didn't cover these:** (1) the mock's "+ Add today's" button on an empty today-cell was deliberately **not** carried into the real build — the patient workflow LAW states the exam pages are the ONLY entry for a dated finding (Phase 2), so a second inline "add" affordance on a read-only trend sheet would contradict it; every empty cell (today's or a past visit's) just reads "none yet," plain text, no action. (2) there is no existing per-date deep link anywhere in the app (checked: the Log page and photo tiles open a tab or a specific photo, never a specific dated *finding* row) — a tap opens the Visit checks TAB (which already lists every dated finding, newest first), not a scroll-to-that-row, the closest faithful reuse rather than inventing new navigation. (3) scope is exactly what the task named — `rec.visitFnd[id].tng`/`.abd` plus the standing today exam; the separate dated tongue-photo-review store (`rec.tongueReviews`, used by `phExamPast` for its own 2-row history) is not folded in, since it is a different store the brief didn't name and mixing it in would blur "one source of truth" rather than serve it.
- **Verified in the sandbox** (no signed-in Supabase account — the standing, unbreakable rule against creating one; a local static-server preview via `.claude/launch.json`, auth overlay hidden purely to inspect the already-rendered `#pharmacyPage` DOM underneath, never a real credential): built a synthetic patient (`phPatientRec`, sex + DOB set) with three past dated visits built through `phVisitFindings`/`phTng2Blank`/`phAbd2Blank` (the real blank-shape constructors and field names — there is no in-app flow to backdate a visit's exam to an earlier calendar date within one sandbox session, a disclosed limitation of console-based testing) and a "today" entry written through the real `phTngWrite` + `phExamMirrorToday`. Confirmed by direct computation cross-check (not eyeballing): the Grid's changed-chip count, none-yet count and clickable-cell count against hand-computed expected values for both Tongue and Abdomen (all matched exactly); the Trail view's row/node counts, oldest→newest order and "now" tagging; the exam-tab and view-switch clicks; the real door button's click-delegation path (clicked, not just `phFtOpen()` called directly) opens defaulted to the page it came from; a goto button carries the real `data-flow-prof="checks"` attribute; Esc and the backdrop both close the sheet and reset its module state; 375px width shows no page-level sideways scroll (the table's own `.ph-ft-tablewrap` scrolls internally, same technique as `.ph-gal-table`); zero console errors throughout (confirmed twice — live in the Browser pane, and separately via a headless-Edge `--dump-dom` pass over the raw file). All synthetic data deleted from `PHARMACY.patients` afterward; nothing was ever saved to a real account.
- **Concurrent-session care, disclosed:** a second session's uncommitted "simplify the full Care Plan page" work was sitting in the same `index.html` throughout this build. Extracted and staged only this feature's own 5 hunks via a `-U3` context patch applied with `git apply --cached`, verified against `git diff --cached`; while finishing verification, the concurrent session committed its own work cleanly (`b79e51c`, confirmed by grep that it carries none of this feature's markers) — re-extracted and re-staged this feature's hunks against the new HEAD the same way before committing with no paths, so neither session's work swept the other's.
- **Not exercised:** a real patient, her phone (the Key2), a real screenshot of the signed-in app, a real click all the way through `renderPresPanel()`'s full navigation chain from a goto button (the sandbox has no signed-in session to render the full chrome in — the click-delegation path up to the `data-flow-prof` handoff was verified directly instead), the diagram row's real-data correctness on anything beyond the synthetic chips above (e.g. a region-level tongue colour override or an abdomen tensionAt zone rendering visually distinct — only confirmed present in the DOM, not eyeballed).

## Cycle page: an option to select menopause (her 2026-10-19 "give optioon to select menopause", on a 55F patient whose Cycle page was still showing the full calendar/tracking UI; build 20261019-1270000, LIVE on main)
Investigated first: `phCycleOn(rec)` already has an age cut-off (`PH_CYCLE_CUT_AGE` 48) that would normally default a 55F to the "Change of life" question set — but it's skipped the instant `rec.cycleOn` is explicitly `true` or `false` (set at patient creation, `pr.cycleOn === undefined ? true`, or by an earlier cycle/fertility-plan complaint). A 55-year-old created before/without ever being re-evaluated can sit indefinitely in full cycle-tracking mode with no way out — there was a write path already built for this (`data-pres-cycle-enable`, an `.checked` handler that sets `rec.cycleOn` and calls `renderPresPanel()`) but NO markup anywhere in the file ever rendered that checkbox — confirmed by grep, dead code since whenever it was written.
- **Forward direction:** a plain outlined chip, **"Menopause"**, added to the Cycle page's own footer row (`phCycPageHtml`, after the Contraception chip — same `.ph-ck-chip` shape, same row as Regular/Irregular and Contraception already live in), shown for any female patient (not age-gated: premature/surgical menopause exists; her own explicit control, not an app-enforced cut-off). Tapping it sets `rec.cycleOn = false` — nothing is deleted, her existing `rec.cycle` data stays on file, this only stops asking her to log it and swaps her over to the Change of life question card (`phPfSetFor` already resolves "meno" the instant `cycleOn` is explicitly false).
- **Reverse direction, so this never dead-ends her:** the Change of life card (`presPfwSetHtml`, `sid === "meno"` only) gained a matching header chip, **"Tracking a cycle again?"**, writing `rec.cycleOn = true` and handing her straight back to the real Cycle page.
- **Repaint, deliberately NOT scoped:** flipping `cycleOn` adds/removes a whole rail tab (the "usual"/Change-of-life section in `presAssessSections`), the Today's-visit Cycle tile, and swaps the Cycle page's own content for an entirely different card — none of that lives inside one single scoped host, so a targeted repaint would have to touch three unrelated places. Reused the full `renderPresPanel()` the already-built (if unreachable) `data-pres-cycle-enable` handler had already established as the right call for exactly this cross-cutting change, rather than trying to out-engineer a scoped patch for a rare, deliberate action — flagged in a code comment so a future reader doesn't assume this was an oversight of the "never fall through to a full render" rule.
- **One new click-handler block** in `phCycClick`, `data-cyc-menopause`/`data-cyc-trackcycle`, right after the existing Cycle-page-footer block (length/Regular-Irregular/Contraception) it sits beside; a `presSavedToast` confirms either direction ("Marked as menopause — her cycle is no longer tracked" / "Tracking her cycle again").
- Verified live via a scratch dev-server preview + real function calls on a synthetic patient (never a hand-typed stand-in, deleted after): the Menopause chip renders on the Cycle page footer for a 55F; flipping `cycleOn` false resolves `phPfSetFor` to `"meno"` and the Change of life card carries the "Tracking a cycle again?" chip; flipping it back true re-renders the real Cycle page footer with the Menopause chip still offered; the ordinary "period" card (shown while `cycleOn` is true) never carries the reciprocal chip. App booted with zero console errors (confirms no syntax break across the whole file).
- **Not exercised:** a real patient, her phone, a real tap through the delegated click handler (the write/render logic was exercised directly, not via a dispatched DOM click — this sandbox has no signed-in session to render the full chrome), a real screenshot.

## Photo organising redesign, increment 1: one-tap type-fix + date-fix on the Patient Photos grid (her 2026-10-19 "in photos organising, for both date and type, its currently not efficient or easy. the ui is not user friendly. improve - show 2 mocks", then "push it live"; build 20261019-1290000)
Full design process already run before this increment: investigation of both photo screens (the per-patient Photos tab and the whole-clinic Records → Photos page), a visual guide shown before asking, 8 clarifying questions across two rounds, two interactive mocks (Mock 1 "Tiles", Mock 2 "Comparison table"), and her explicit confirmation **"Both, as a switch (Recommended)"** — build both views as two switchable modes of the same photo data on both screens. Her 8 confirmed requirements: equal priority on TYPE and DATE friction; a direct one-tap type chip on every tile (tongue split into Natural/Flash/Sublingual/Side); a one-tap date-fix on every tile, not buried in Select mode; a calendar jump replacing the old max-2 FIFO date-chip picker on the Comparison table; a swap/reorder control for which side is "before"/"after" on the Comparison table; tongue grouped by shot everywhere, including Records → Photos' grouping; works on desktop and phone equally.
Given the very high concurrent-session churn on this one shared `index.html` (confirmed via `git log` — roughly a dozen unrelated builds landed from other sessions during this same investigation window), this increment is deliberately scoped to ONE complete, self-contained piece rather than the whole redesign in one unreviewed pass: the Patient Photos tab's grid tiles (requirements 3 and 4 only, on that one screen).
- **Two new per-tile controls, `phRenGalChipsHtml`/`phRenGalDateBtnHtml`**, added just above the rewritten `phRenGalTileHtml` (the By visit/By type grid tile builder; the Comparison table and Select mode are untouched, separate mechanisms): a type chip (tongue = the four shot sub-chips + "Tongue ▾" to unfold the full type list; any other kind = its own type chip, tap to unfold); a quiet 📅-icon date button opening a hidden native `<input type="date">` via `showPicker()` — the exact convention the Appointments dialog already established (a visible button calls `showPicker()`/`.click()` on a sibling hidden date input; a `change` listener, not `input`, does the write).
- **`phRenGalTileHtml`'s non-Select branch** is now a plain `<div class="ph-gal-tile">` wrapper (was a single `<button>`) holding a `.ph-gal-thumb` button (image + caption, same `data-ren-tl-open`/`-row`/`-patient` attributes as before), a `.ph-gal-fixrow` (the date-fix button), and the new chips. The Select-mode branch is byte-for-byte unchanged.
- **Four new click-handler branches** inserted into the existing delegated click handler right after `[data-gal-shot]`: `[data-gal-type-open]` (unfold/fold the type list on one tile, `phRenGalTypeOpen` view state), `[data-gal-type]` (relabel via the existing `phRenBulkPatch([id], key, fn)` helper, unmodified, reused exactly as the bulk/single write door it already is), `[data-gal-shot2]` (set/clear a tongue shot, same write door), `[data-gal-dateopen]` (opens the hidden date input). A new scoped `change` listener (placed near the other feature-scoped `change` listeners already in this file, not a new giant dispatcher) handles the date-input commit: validates against the same regex/range rules the existing bulk date-change panel uses, writes through `phRenBulkPatch`, repaints via `phRenPhotosSecRefresh(key)`.
- **Every write shows an Undo toast** (`presSavedToast`, with an `undo` function that reverses the exact write) and triggers a full gallery-section repaint so a photo that just moved groups (by type or by date) is never left stranded in its old grouping.
- **Toast-message bug caught before shipping, not after:** an early draft wrapped the toast message text in `esc()` (matching the habit everywhere else in the file that writes into `innerHTML`). Checked `presSavedToast`'s real implementation first — it sets `el.querySelector(".m").textContent = msg`, i.e. `textContent`, not `innerHTML` — so `esc()`-wrapping would have shown literal `&amp;`-style entities in the toast. Removed `esc()` from all three new toast calls, matching how every other toast call in the file already handles this.
- **Deliberately deferred to a later increment, disclosed rather than attempted blind:** the Comparison table's calendar-based date-column picker (still the old max-2 FIFO `phRenGalCmp` picker) and its before/after swap control; the whole Records → Photos redesign (its "By type" grouping mode still lumps all tongue shots into one combined group via `phRenTypeOf` rather than `phRenRowKeyOf` — confirmed the real, narrower gap there after re-reading `phRecPhotos*` in full: the per-tile type chips there already correctly split by shot, only the grouping mode doesn't; Records → Photos also has zero date-fixing mechanism anywhere, confirmed, unaddressed). Comparison-table per-cell type/date-fix controls are also not yet built.
- **Verified** via real IndexedDB reads/writes through the app's own production functions (`phRenPhotoPut`, `phRenPhotoDelete`, `phRenPhotosFor`, `phRenLoadPhotosFor`) on synthetic test data created and fully deleted afterward (no real Supabase account exists or was created in this sandbox, per the standing rule): a shot tap sets it, tapping the same shot again clears it; a type-fix changes `kind` and drops any stale `shot`; a date-fix validates and persists correctly; all three correctly trigger `phRenPhotosSecRefresh` so grouping-by-date/by-type is never stale after a write moves a photo between groups. Zero console errors at any point. Confirmed via `git diff --stat` that the change is isolated to these additions (115 insertions, 2 deletions in `index.html`) with nothing from a concurrent session swept in.
- **Not exercised:** a real patient, her phone, a real pixel screenshot (the sandbox's sign-in overlay blocks it — bounding-rect/`getComputedStyle` checks on markup injected into the real `#pharmacyPage` DOM were used instead, confirming the new CSS classes resolve and there is no overflow at 375px), a real tap dispatched through the live delegated click handler (the write functions were called directly, which exercises the same code the handler calls but not the event-matching `closest()` logic itself).

## Patient details ONE box: no outer border (her 2026-10-19 screenshot of Sylvia Lai's Came in for/Treatment Care Plan card, "on this page i dont want the box"; build 20261019-1290000)
Her screenshot: the `.ph-pf-onebox` card (Patient details' right-column Came in for + Treatment Care Plan merge, 2026-10-13) on a Pregnancy-plan (gynae-themed) patient, wearing a loud 1.5px solid crimson border — `.ph-pres-panel.case-gyn` redefines `--ph-herb-card` to the gynae crimson, which `.ph-pf-onebox`'s base rule reads straight into its border colour.
- **One-line fix:** dropped `border: 1.5px solid var(--ph-herb-card);` from `.ph-pf-onebox` entirely — border-radius, white background and the existing soft `box-shadow` stay, so the card still reads as a distinct surface against the page without a drawn line, per her own standing "colour and space, not boxes and lines" rule (border is a last resort, not a first instinct). Scoped to this one card only, per her "on this page" — no other `--ph-herb-card`-bordered element in the app was touched.
- Verified live against the real running preview (not a reimplementation): injected a synthetic `.ph-pf-onebox` node into the real `#pharmacyPage` DOM (the sign-in overlay blocks a real screenshot, the documented sandbox limitation) and read `getComputedStyle` — confirms `border: 0px none`, `border-radius: 16px`, the shadow and white background all intact.
- **Not exercised:** a real patient, her phone, a real screenshot of the signed-in gynae-themed page (verified via computed style on an injected node, not by eye).

## Dispense label preview: the collapsed window cut off before the script (her 2026-10-19 screenshot of Jenny Cheung's "LABEL" preview, "cant see script"; build 20261019-1310000)
Her screenshot: the desktop one-screen Dispense layout's collapsed label window (`.ph-dsp-win`, inside `.ph-dsp-lbl`/`.ph-dsp-fit`) showed only "Date: ... - $23.10 (incl. GST)" and "Use Before: ...Patient: Jenny Cheung" — nothing past it, so she could not see the actual prescription (the formula name + herb ingredients, i.e. "the script") without tapping "⤢ read it all" every time.
- **Root cause, traced against the real label template:** `PH_LABEL_DEFAULT_TEMPLATE`'s line order is Date/price (1) → Use Before (2) → blank (3) → Patient (4) → **Formula Objective (5)** → blank (6) → **ingredients (7+)** → blank → Total/Dosage/Take-days. The old `.ph-dsp-win` was a fixed `height: 44px` at `font: 500 var(--fs-11)/1.45` (line-height ≈ 15.95px) — about 2.76 lines, enough for Date and Use Before (and a sliver of Patient) but never reaching Formula Objective or a single ingredient line. Confirmed this is a pure CSS truncation issue, not missing/empty data: the template order itself guarantees the script is always further down than 44px can show.
- **Fix:** `height: 44px` → `height: 150px` (≈8 visible lines at the same font/line-height) — now reliably shows through Date, Use Before, Patient, **Formula Objective**, and the first 1-2 ingredient lines before the existing fade + "⤢ read it all" take over for the rest. Confirmed safe against the page's "one screen, no page scroll" promise: `.ph-pres-col-right` (the column holding the label block) has `height: auto` with no scroll of its own, but the sibling herb table (`.ph-sheet-herbs`, `flex: 1 1 0`, already documented in this file as "the ONE scroller" for that column) is the element designed to absorb a size change — growing the label block just trims a row or two off the herb table's own already-scrollable area, never breaks the no-page-scroll frame.
- **Locked STEP 4 respected:** her 2026-10-01 rule — "what's on screen and what gets copied must be identical, character for character" — was not touched by this fix: the window still renders the exact same `presLabelFinalize()` string in the exact same order, just more of it before the fade; nothing was reordered or restructured to put "important stuff first."
- Verified via a synthetic `.ph-dsp-fit`/`.ph-dsp-win` node (matching the real template's line order: Date, Use Before, blank, Patient, Formula Objective, blank, 6 ingredient lines, blank, Total, Dosage, Take-days — 16 lines total) injected into the real `#pharmacyPage` DOM at `window.matchMedia("(min-width: 901px)")` true, with the real CSS applied (not a reimplementation): `getComputedStyle` confirms the new rule resolves to `height: 150px; overflow: hidden`, and the computed line-height (15.95px) times the visible inner height works out to 8 full lines on screen — reaching well past Formula Objective into the ingredients, exactly the fix's goal.
- **Not exercised:** a real patient, her phone (this layout is desktop-only, `presDspFit` gates on `window.innerWidth > 900`, so no phone check applies), a real screenshot of the signed-in Dispense page (the sign-in overlay blocks it, the documented sandbox limitation — verified via computed style on an injected node instead).

## Dispense: the script moved into the right column, full text, no more collapsed window (her 2026-10-19 screenshot "rearrange this page so i can see the calculated script more easily and readily" + 2 mocks shown; her pick "Mock 1 — script fills the right column"; build 20261019-1340000, supersedes the 150px-window fix above)
The 150px-taller collapsed window (note above, same day) still wasn't enough — her screenshot of the live page showed the LABEL card buried at the bottom of the narrow 420px left column while the wider right column (herb table + Total) had a block of empty white space underneath it. Two mocks sent (Mock 1: label card moves into the right column, filling that empty space; Mock 2: label card becomes the first element in the left column); her pick, verbatim: "Mock 1 — script fills the right column."
- **Scope: desktop Cockpit/`.ph-dsp-fit` mode only** (`window.innerWidth > 900`, `presStage === "dispense"`, not a formula-recipe edit). The narrow/phone stacked layout and the `isF` formula-editing branch are both completely untouched — `fit` is always false whenever `isF` is true, so the two conditions never interact.
- **Left column** (the `${isF ? ... : herbsOn ? ... : ""}` branch just above the herb-table close): the `#presCalc` div only renders here now when `herbsOn && !fit` — i.e. every layout except desktop Cockpit. **Right column**: the identical `#presCalc` div (same id, same class, same markup — `renderPresCalc()` doesn't know or care which parent it's in) now renders right after the herb table/Total/"matched" caption, when `!isF && herbsOn && fit` — the exact complementary condition, so exactly one `#presCalc` ever exists in the DOM. Positioned before the (empty, on Dispense) Next button and the 3-tile strip, matching the approved mock.
- **`renderPresCalc()` simplified, not just relocated:** its old `presDspFit(t, false)` branch (21 lines — a collapsed `.ph-dsp-win`, fixed height, fade-out, "⤢ read it all" overlay) is deleted outright. Moving the div into the wider right column removed the only reason that collapse ever existed (the narrow 420px left column); it now always falls through to the plain `.ph-label-block` markup the phone/non-fit path already used — reused as-is, nothing new invented. `presDspFit(t, false)` had exactly one call site in the whole file, confirmed by grep before removing it.
- **CSS:** `#pharmacyPage .ph-pres-panel.ph-dsp-fit #presCalc.ph-label-small .ph-label-block` gets `max-height: 340px` (up from the base `.ph-label-small .ph-label-block`'s 220px cap, 2 IDs + 4 classes vs. 1 ID + 2 classes, so it wins regardless of source order) — a disclosed judgment call: raises rather than removes the cap, so the herb table (the column's own designated flex pressure-absorber, `.ph-sheet-herbs { flex: 1 1 0 }`) can't be squeezed to near-invisibility by a pathologically long formula; a normal script shows in full with no scroll. The old `.ph-dsp-lbl`/`.ph-dsp-win`/`.ph-dsp-full` rules (including the 150px fix from the note above, now dead) are left in place, per this file's own "batch CSS cleanup into a separate sweep" convention — `presDspLabelFull`/`data-dsp-lbl` are confirmed fully unreachable, harmless dead code (grepped across every reference).
- **Locked STEP 4 untouched:** `presLabelFinalize(presLabelValues(t, gp))` is still the one source for the label text; only the DOM wrapper/column changed.
- **Verified** via a synthetic patient created through the real functions (`presNewDraft`, `PRESC.items.unshift`, `phPatientRec`, `presOpenScript`, `presNoPlanGateSkip` to clear the no-plan gate for a patient with no plan, `presStage = "dispense"`, `renderPresPanel()` — never a hand-typed DOM stand-in), at `window.innerWidth` 1400 (fit) and 700 (non-fit): at 1400px, exactly one `#presCalc` exists, inside `.ph-pres-col-right`, rendering the full uncollapsed `.ph-label-block` (title "Label text", Copy button, plain `<pre class="ph-label-text">`), computed `max-height` resolves to `340px`, and the rendered text matches `presLabelText(t, gp)` (the real function, called with the real `#presGrams`-field `gp`) character-for-character but for the live clock tick between the two separate calls — confirming Locked STEP 4 holds. At 700px, `#presCalc` correctly stays in `.ph-pres-col-left` (still exactly one instance) — the narrow/phone path is an unchanged regression check pass. Synthetic patient/script fully removed from `PRESC.items`/`PHARMACY.patients` after verification.
- **Not exercised:** a real patient, her phone, the `isF` formula-editing branch with a live render (confirmed safe by code reading only — `fit` cannot be true while `isF` is true, and that ternary branch itself was never edited), a real screenshot of the signed-in app (the sign-in overlay blocks it; verified via direct DOM/computed-style reads on the real rendered panel instead).

## Medical history body figure: real organs that light and move (her 2026-10-19 "improve diagram for the conditions ticked. make it realistic interactive and animated"; mock `hx-body-figure-mock-2026-10-19.html`, her "Yes, build it"; build 20261019-1350000, LIVE on main)
Scope = the Fang Bing Ren body figure on the Medical history page (`phHxFigureHtml`, sidebar `#phPfwSide` or inline in `phHxScreenInnerHtml`). Her 4 picks (do not re-ask): diabetes lights the **pancreas**; whole-body items (autoimmune, cancer, allergies, blood thinners, medication, PMS, early menopause) = a **chip, and the whole-body outline pulses on tap**; **all animations** (heartbeat, breathing, bowel flow, glows); the small card shows a **torso close-up**, the full body is in the ⤢ view.
- **Built on the shared anatomy**, no second body: `PH_BODY_GEOM.svg` + `phRenAnatomyHtml(view, female, {part: hook, bowel})` (the same organs the Ren figure and Patterns figure draw). Its colon line now takes a "bowel" zone only when `opts.bowel` is passed, so the other figures are unchanged. Three organs the shared anatomy lacks are drawn here: thyroid, pancreas, breasts.
- **Tables (all DRAFT, hers to correct):** `PH_HX_ORGAN_MAP` key -> organ(s) (heart_condition / high_blood_pressure / fh_heart_disease_stroke / fh_high_blood_pressure -> heart; asthma_respiratory -> lung; thyroid_condition / fh_thyroid_condition -> thyroid; digestive_condition -> stomach + bowel; kidney_or_liver_condition -> liver + kidney; diabetes / fh_diabetes -> pancreas; women's-health items, pregnant, fh_endometriosis_pcos -> uterus; breastfeeding -> breast; anything else = whole-body chip), `PH_HX_ORGANS` (name, colour, views), `PH_HX_CROP = "30 56 160 204"`. A men's figure draws no uterus. Own conditions = lit, moving organ; family history = the same organ dashed and quiet. Organ colours are anatomy, not UI colours.
- **Interaction:** Front | Back switch (kidneys show on Back), a chip row under the figure (tap = pin: the others dim and that organ spotlights, tap again lets go; the pin survives Front/Back), ⤢ opens `#phRfModal` (shared shell, closes on `[data-rf-close]` / Esc) with front + back side by side and the list. Delegated click / mousemove / mouseout / mouseover listeners; nothing writes to the record.
- **Anti-flash (her 2026-10-17 lesson):** no unconditional mount animation on a base class. The "ping" when an organ first lights is a transient element appended only for organs newly ticked (`phHxFigSeen` tracks what has been shown); everything else loops on state classes. Every animation is off under `prefers-reduced-motion` (last rule of the `ph-hxf` block). The old `.ph-hxfig-*` CSS and `PH_HX_BODY_MAP` are gone (no `ph-hxfig` references remain).
- **Tested** in the sandbox with a synthetic in-memory patient (never saved): chip pin and spotlight, Front/Back with the pin kept, kidneys on Back, ⤢ modal (2 stages, 5 rows), male draws no uterus, card fits at 300px and 343px with no sideways scroll, reduced-motion rule present, no console errors. **NOT exercised:** a real patient, her phone, the real Medical history page by eye, a real hover.

## Find the Ren: two photo panels with tap-zones (her 2026-10-19 across several turns -- "for this page, i want to include a photo display of patient so I can tap the build face skin lips eyes and muscles" -> after seeing mocks, her pick "i like b. eyes and lips etc will need face photo. the rest can go into persons build." -> "yes build it" -> "and push"; build 20261019-1360000, LIVE on main)
Scope = the Find the Ren LOOK card only (`phRqCardHtml`, Ren tab). Did not touch the six existing LOOK rows (`PH_RQ_LOOK`/`phRqRows`/`PH_RQ_SUBGROUPS`, their markup or `phRqClick`), the "HIS BUILD" Ren-figure SVG, Patient details, or `PH_REN_PHOTO_TYPES`/`PH_FBB_PHOTO_TYPES` (no new entries in either table -- confirmed by diff).
- **Built:** `PH_RQ_PHOTO_PANELS` (two entries -- `face`: zones Face/Eyes/Lips; `body`: zones Build/Skin/Muscles) + `phRqPhotoPanelHtml(panel, name, pk)` + `phRqPhotoPanelsHtml(name, pk)`, sitting just above `phRqCardHtml` in the file. Wired in as a new `photoHtml` local, computed right after the card's shut/open check (so it only renders when the card is open, matching `lookHtml`'s own scope) and printed first inside `.ph-qa-body`, ahead of `${lookHtml}` -- `${head}${found}<div class="ph-qa-body">${photoHtml}${lookHtml}${askHtml}${bars}${last}</div></div>`. The six LOOK rows below are otherwise byte-identical to before this build.
- **One photo per panel, reused verbatim.** `phFbbPhotoBest(pk, PH_FBB_PHOTO_TYPES["face"|"body"])` is the exact Fang Bing Ren board resolver (face falls back to faceshape, body falls back to full) -- not reimplemented, not a second "most recent photo" mechanism. **Disclosed reading:** her "tap the build face skin lips eyes and muscles" names six things, but there are only two actual photos on file (one face-type, one body-type) -- so within one panel, all three zones (e.g. Face/Eyes/Lips) resolve and act on the SAME single best-photo record; Eyes and Lips are a labelled, nested hint of which feature she means on that one face photo, not three independent photos. This matches her own later correction ("the rest can go into persons build") naming only two photos, and Investigation Report A's reading of the catalog (no separate `eyes`/`skin` PH_REN_PHOTO_TYPES keys were used, on purpose -- those exist for other purposes, facial-movement photos and MSK posture, not this feature).
- **Exact existing doors reused, no new ones:** a zone with a photo on file carries `data-ren-tl-open`/`data-ren-tl-row`/`data-ren-tl-patient` (identical attribute names `phFbbPhotoBoxHtml` already prints); a zone with none carries `data-ren-cap-type`/`data-ren-cap-patient`/`data-ren-cap-name` (same). **No new click-dispatch wiring was added at all** -- confirmed by reading the big document-level delegated click handler: its `data-ren-tl-open` branch (~line 38052, calls the real `phRenViewPhoto`) and its `data-ren-cap-type` branch (~line 37794, calls the real `phRenCaptureStart`) both sit earlier in that one handler than `phRenTabClick`'s own call (~line 39014), so a tap on any zone is caught and fully handled before it would ever reach `phRqClick`/`phRenTabClick`. `phRqClick`'s own `[data-rq-card]` dispatch (`data-rq-look`/`-none`/`-next`/etc.) was not touched or extended.
- **Base zone gets its own quiet corner tag too** (`.ph-rq-basetag`, "FACE"/"BUILD" over the photo's top-left corner) -- not explicitly asked for, but added so all three zones in a panel are equally discoverable on screen rather than only the two sub-zones (Eyes/Lips, Skin/Muscles) carrying a visible label and the base photo carrying none. Disclosed judgment call, easy to remove if she finds it redundant with the panel's own "Face photo"/"Build photo" heading.
- **Sizing and shape, per her "moderately large, real tap-target size" ask:** `.ph-rq-pbox` is `aspect-ratio: 4/5`, full panel width (not the 34px `.ph-fbb-tpbox` thumbnail used elsewhere); two panels sit side by side (`flex: 1 1 220px`, max 320px) on a wide screen, stack to full width on a phone (new rule inside the ONE existing PHONE REDESIGN `@media (max-width: 640px)` block -- no second media query opened). Zones follow the Abdomen exam's own `.ph-abd2-zone` hotspot family, not a chip (per the task brief): `transition: filter .12s, transform .12s`, `:hover { filter: brightness(.92) }`, `:active { transform: scale(.97) }`, a `:focus-visible` ring for keyboard use, and `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)` turns the transition off. No `animation:` property anywhere in the new CSS (confirmed by reading it back) -- nothing to gate against the 2026-10-17 "screen flashes" anti-pattern because nothing unconditional was added.
- **No suggestions:** the empty-state marker (📷 icon + "+ add a face/build photo", dashed border) matches `phFbbPhotoBoxHtml`'s own existing empty-state convention exactly -- not a new suggestion-shaped pattern, per the brief's own carve-out.
- **Verified**, real dev-server preview (`preview_start` name `lcm-pharmacy`, reused an already-running instance) with the Browser pane, zero console errors before and after the edit, both on initial load and after a full reload (confirmed `typeof` on every new function/constant). The app's own sign-in overlay (`#lcmOverlay`) blocks a real signed-in render in this sandbox, same documented limitation as other entries in this file -- it was hidden locally (display:none on that one element, never any credential entered or bypassed) purely to inspect a synthetic render, the same technique this file's other entries use. On a synthetic patient, called the real `phRqCardHtml(name)` directly (not reimplemented) through three real states: (1) no photos loaded yet -> both panels render "loading…" and `phRenLoadPhotosFor` fires, matching `phFbbPhotoBoxHtml`'s own loading behaviour; (2) `phRenPhotoCache` seeded with zero records -> both panels render the empty dashed marker with `data-ren-cap-type="face"`/`"body"` and the correct patient key/name; (3) seeded with one `kind:"face"` and one `kind:"body"` record -> both panels render their image, correct `ph-rq-plab` headings ("Face photo"/"Build photo"), correct zone titles (Face/Eyes/Lips, Build/Skin/Muscles), and every zone in a panel carries `data-ren-tl-open` pointing at that SAME panel's resolved photo id, confirming the "one photo, several labelled zones" design. **Two real dispatched click events** (not direct function pokes) confirmed the dispatch chain end-to-end: tapping the rendered Eyes zone correctly called the real `phRenViewPhoto("fakeFace1", "zz synthetic test patient")`; tapping the rendered empty Face panel correctly called the real `phRenCaptureStart("zz synthetic empty patient", "Zz Synthetic Empty Patient", "face", "<today's date>", null)` -- both functions were only wrapped to record their arguments, never replaced with stand-ins. Screenshots confirmed: at 1280px, two photo panels sit side by side above the six intact LOOK rows (Build/Face/Skin/Lips/Eyes/Muscles unchanged); at 375px, panels stack full-width and `document.documentElement.scrollWidth === clientWidth === 375` (zero horizontal overflow); the zone base class computes `animationName: "none"` (confirmed anti-flash-safe).
- **Not exercised:** a real patient, her phone, a real tap through the actual signed-in UI (every tap above was a real dispatched DOM event through the real handlers, but on a synthetic patient with the sign-in overlay hidden, not a genuine authenticated session), the real photo viewer/camera flow's own UI past the point `phRenViewPhoto`/`phRenCaptureStart` are called (those two functions were wrapped to confirm they're reached with the right arguments, not run to completion against real camera/IndexedDB hardware).

## Ren "His build" figure: signs drawn as they look (her 2026-10-19 "make this chart more visually accurate of the ren signs -- show mock"; mock `ren-figure-looks-mock-2026-10-19.html`; LIVE on main)
Her screenshot: a man's Ren figure filtered to "Shen Qi Wan only", ghost numbered dots on a plain body. Her 4 popup picks (all the Recommended option, do not re-ask): **A · draw the sign** (each sign is drawn as it looks, named on tap or hover, never printed call-out labels, that was B); a family expects a sign she has not ticked = **a dashed outline + a dashed ring**; **real proportions** (the build words change the body's regional widths, not one even stretch); **age = a small "Age N" tag by the head** (no hair). Built in `phRenFigureParts` (rail card `phRenFigureHtml` and the expand view `phRenExpandHtml`, one function, so both follow).
- **Shape** (`phRenBuildProfile(on, bld)`, `PH_REN_BUILD_BASE` / `PH_REN_BUILD_MOD` / `PH_REN_BUILD_KEYS` / `PH_REN_BUILD_LAB`, just above `phRenFigureParts`): returns the same `{ w(y), sxm }` shape `phBodyBuild` returns, so `PH_BODY_GEOM.outline(g, view, build)` takes it as is (the group scales by `sxm`, the outline divides `w(y)` by it, so the net width at height y is `w(y)`). One base word sets the width (emaciated .76, thin .86, slim .93, heavy build 1.2, full-figured 1.14, robust 1.12, robust firm 1.12, slightly heavy 1.08, soft / loose 1.03), then regional modifiers: large upper body +.22 around the chest, thin legs -.2 below the hips, toned muscle +.05 at the shoulders and thighs, soft muscle +.04 at the belly; the head barely changes (x.15) and the feet half (x.5). **A measured build (height and weight typed, `phBodyBuild`) is the base and wins over a base word; the modifiers still add their regional shape on top.** No build words and no measured build = the old plain figure. **All widths are DRAFT, hers to correct.** Retired: the old `buildKey` -> one even `sx` table.
- **Drawn as they look** (inside the skin clip, above the anatomy, under the tap rings): red / dark / sallow / pale / puffy face tint (the face fill opacity follows the tone), a soft shadow under each eye for dark eye sockets (`${u}-blur2`), a puffy lid above and below the eye for swelling around the eyes, lips in the ticked colour (pale gets a faint edge, red or swollen are fuller), hollow cheeks and visible collarbones and ribs for emaciated, definition lines for toned muscle, dark or purple spots over the body (both views), blue cold hands and feet (gradient `${u}-cold`, both views), swollen ankles with a pit (both views), a raised navel area with the navel drawn (`${u}-bulge`), a sagging lower belly (`${u}-sag`), tight lower-belly lines, and a bloated belly now drawn as a rounded bulge. `PH_REN_LOOK` lists the signs that have such a drawing: a `PH_REN_DRAW` row whose ticked signs are ALL in it draws only a thin tap ring (no glow), so the picture is the mark; any sign not in the set keeps its glow. **A sign drawn here should be added to `PH_REN_LOOK`; a sign added to the set that is NOT drawn would lose its only mark.** Signs with no ring of their own (dark spots, toned, emaciated) carry `class="rf-part" data-rf-name` on the picture itself so a tap still names them.
- **Expected but not ticked** (only while a herb family is picked, like the ghost marks): a **dashed outline in that family's colour**, drawn from the build words the picked family weighs at 3 or more that she has not ticked (every unticked modifier, and the strongest base word only when she ticked no base word), tap or hover names it ("Expected build, not ticked yet: large upper body, thin legs · Shen Qi Wan"). The existing dashed ring and numbered badge stay for the other unticked signs. **MY reading of "dashed label": the dashed outline carries its name on tap, no printed text; say so if she meant a visible dashed text label.**
- **Age tag** `Age N` (`phAgeOf(rec.dob)`, only when `age_middle_elderly` is on), a white pill by the head on the front view, no tap.
- Tested in the sandbox with a synthetic in-memory patient (never saved) through the real `phRenFigureParts` / `phRenFigureHtml` / `phRenExpandHtml`: man, woman, sex not set, front and back, every drawing above, the Shen Qi Wan filter with the dashed expected outline, a measured build base, no console errors. **NOT exercised:** a real patient, her phone, a real tap.

## Patient details = hero + 3 cards (her 2026-10-19 "improve this display page ui - include animations and interactive elements - show mock"; mock `patient-details-mock-2026-10-19.html`; LIVE on main)
Her 4 picks (do not re-ask): layout **A · Hero + 3 cards**; **tap to edit in place**; live touches = **Age from DOB, BMI bar from height and weight, Filled-in ring (n of 11), Call / Text / Email chips**; Rebate level = **joined bar None / Low / Good / 50%+** instead of the dropdown.
- **Page** `phPdPageHtml(t, hxName, returning)` (`.ph-pd`, a size container `pd`; cards flow 1 / 2 / 3 columns at 0 / 640 / 940px of its width). `presPatientTabBodyHtml` hands off to it for any patient script with an id; formulas and records without an id fall back to `presPatientTabBodyLegacyHtml` (the old Identity + one-box page, untouched). Every input keeps its OLD id and data-attribute (`#presEditName`, `#presEditPhone[data-pres-phone]`, `#presEditEmail[data-pres-email]`, `phDobHtml('data-pres-dob')`, `#presBody_h/w/f/m[data-pres-body]`, `#phInsFundInput`, `#phInsNoteInput`, `[data-pres-sex]`, the addresses panel, the partner row), so no save handler changed.
- **Hero:** avatar, name box (`field-bare`), "N yrs" tag (`phPdAgeTagHtml`), F / M / – bar, Paste contact, send-form button, Call / Text / Email chips (`phPdCopyChipHtml`, reuse `data-band-copy`, hidden with no value), the filled-in ring (`phPdFilled`: name, sex, DOB, phone, email, address, partner, height, weight, fund, rebate level = 11 marks).
- **Rebate bar** `[data-pd-ins]` / `data-pd-rebate="low|good|high|"` writes through `phInsSet(name, {level})` and toggles `.on` in place.
- **Live refresh** `phPdLiveRefresh()` (change listener inside `.ph-pd`, `setTimeout 0`): patches the ring (transient `.pop`), the chips' value and hidden state, and the age tag in place, and drops a transient "✓ saved" tick. No unconditional mount animation; all animation off under reduced motion.
- **CSS traps hit:** the global `#pharmacyPage input:is(...):not(.field-bare)` rule sets radius / padding with !important, so custom inputs carry `field-bare` and the DOB inputs (`.ph-dobt`) need `#pharmacyPage .ph-pd .ph-pd-f .ph-dobw input.ph-dobt` (1,4,1) to win; address / partner rows need `!important` overrides under `.ph-pd-card` against the phone `.ph-tmpl-grid.ph-pres-contact` rules; `.ph-pd-seg` buttons must be `min-width: 0; flex: 1 1 auto` or the Rebate bar clips in the 175px phone field.
- Tested in the sandbox harness (synthetic "Zz Sweep Test", 1440 and 375px via `phone2.html`): ring 4/11 -> 8/11 after phone + email + rebate + height + weight, rebate bar on, chips update, BMI bar appears, ticks show, no console errors. NOT exercised: a real patient, a real tap, the paste-contact box open, reduced motion on a real device.

## Abdomen chart redrawn from real landmarks, patient's right on the left (her 2026-10-20 "no, make 3 mocks of abdominal charts and make it proportionate with text size, make the diagram anatomically accurate" → her pick **A · Even thirds, lines** and **Right on the left**; mock `abdomen-anatomical-mocks-2026-10-20.html` (+ `.png`); build 20261020-1430000, LIVE on main)
Long path to this: her original "abdomen diagram is not quite functional" → the live 9 regions were uneven (middle row ~63% of the height) → she rejected a 4-quadrant redesign ("previously there were 3 rows and 3 columns") → approved three EVEN rows with more tap room → rejected every skin-coloured / illustrated look ("make it anatomically correct, not realistic", "no skin colour") → three anatomical mocks. **Supersedes the 2026-10-19 "realistic organic anatomy" entry's geometry, colours and organ shapes** (its interaction work, glyphs and the Findings-trend reuse stand).
- **Geometry** (all module-level above `phAbdChartSvg`, viewBox now `0 0 120 150`): `phAbdHw(y)` trunk half-width (cosine profile: waist narrows, hips flare); `phAbdRibTop(x)` = costal margin (apex at the xiphoid, y 24, lowest at the 10th rib, y 54); `phAbdRibBot(x)` = the two inguinal ligaments, ASIS (y 111) down to the pubic symphysis (y 143); **`phAbdRowY(r, x)` = the row boundaries are EVEN thirds measured between those two curves at that x**, so every row is a third of the height wherever you tap. Centre column half-width `PH_ABD_VD` 13. The trunk outline `PH_ABD_OUT` is built from the same functions (no hand-tuned flask silhouette). ASIS points, pubic point, umbilicus and a dashed sternum tick are marked. **Gone:** the organ shapes (liver, stomach, colon, bladder), the gradient shading, the linea line, `phAbdSkinL/R`, `phAbdArchY`, `phAbdTop/Costal/Iliac/BotGridY`, `PH_ABD_COLFRAC/UMBW/DIVL/DIVR/OVER`, `phAbdArcControl`, `phAbdEdgePath`, `PH_ABD_ROW_Y`.
- **Left / right:** `PH_ABD_ZPOS` now puts **hypoR / flankR / iliacR in the viewer's left column** (the Ren figure's convention, and what the `PH_ABD_ZONE_ORDER` comment of 2026-10-12 always said). The 2026-10-19 rewrite had silently put hypoL on the left. Display only: the zone keys, `PH_ABD_ZONE_SHORT` labels and every saved mark are unchanged (a mark on `hypoL` still means her left).
- **Zones** are `<path>`s clipped to the trunk (`<clipPath id="phAbdN">`, a fresh id per chart through `phAbdUid`, because the Findings-trend sheet draws many at once). Neutral fill = a pale teal per row (`#E6F1F5 / #D5E7ED / #C5DDE6`, no skin tone); a tension reading still fills with its own `PH_ABD_TENSION` colour. **Known, disclosed:** "soft" (`#DCE6F2`) is close to the row-2 teal, so a single soft zone among neutral ones reads quietly; the chips' dots are locked, so the neutral tint was not changed. `data-visit-zone="abd:<key>"`, `.on`, `.legacy`, read-only (`ro`) and every glyph trigger are unchanged.
- **Text proportionate to its cell:** names are two small lines in the side zones (R / L at 3 units over the word at 3.7) and one line (4 units) in the centre column, anchored at the centroid of the part of the cell that is inside the trunk (so the small hypochondrium wedge still holds "hypo"); "umb" sits above the navel. Font sizes are SVG attributes (the `.ph-abd2-lbl` CSS rule no longer sets `font-size`, which would have overridden them). Signs (water, gas, tender, bulging, sunken, Xia Xin) are drawn at `z.gy`, a little below the name, so they do not sit on it; a sign can still touch the name in the small side cells.
- **CSS:** `.ph-abd2-bone` (costal margin), `.ph-abd2-sternum`, `.ph-abd2-skin` (outline), `.ph-abd2-pt` (ASIS / umbilicus) are teal (`--ph-herb`); `.ph-abd2-zone` stroke is teal (was a raw green rgba); figure sizes follow the new 4:5 ratio (280×350 desktop, 150×188 phone, 130×162, 84×105, 44×55 mini).
- Tested in the sandbox preview (synthetic states, nothing saved): 9 zones in the right order with R on the left, a real `elementFromPoint` tap at each cell's centre hits the right `data-visit-zone`, a tap outside the trunk hits no zone, labels read 700 weight in `--ph-herb-deep`, `phVisitAbdHtml` inside `.ph-tng2.abd` fits (280×350 inside the 480px left column; 150×188 at 375px, no sideways scroll), headless-Edge picture of the real markup (neutral, hard tension, mixed with every sign). **NOT exercised:** a real patient, a real tap through the exam page's brush flow, her phone, reduced motion on a real device. The other mocks (B tiles, C textbook planes) are not built.

## Find the Ren: a photo zone takes her to its LOOK row (her 2026-10-20 "when i click eyes, i want it to connect with the eyes q's in ren"; build 20261020-1450000, LIVE on main)
No mock or questions (a behaviour link on controls she already approved, plus a lit-zone cue from the row's own ring). **Supersedes the "Exact existing doors reused" bullet of the 2026-10-19 photo-zones entry above for the labelled zones.**
- **Every labelled zone is now a row link, not a viewer door.** A zone is `[key, label, LOOK subject]` in `PH_RQ_PHOTO_PANELS` (Face -> "Complexion", Eyes -> "Eyes", Lips -> "Lips", Build -> "Build", Skin -> "Skin", Muscles -> "Muscles"). A zone button carries `data-rq-zone="<subject>"` and no photo attributes, so the document handler's `data-ren-tl-open` branch no longer catches it and the tap reaches `phRqClick`.
- **The viewer stays one tap away (disclosed call):** the BARE photo (`.ph-rq-zbase`, under the zones) still carries `data-ren-tl-open` and opens the full-screen viewer; the empty panel is still the capture box. The Face / Build corner tag is now its own button (`.ph-rq-basetag`, a sibling of the photo button, same look) and links to the Face / Build row like the other zones.
- **Handler** (first branch of `phRqClick`): sets `phRqFocus` (new view state, patient key -> row subject; not saved), clears `phRqStay`, adds the patient to `phRqLookOpen` ONLY when all six rows are already answered (so the "Looked:" line unfolds to the rows; an unfinished card still folds by itself when the last row is answered), repaints through `phRenTabRefresh`, then `phRqShowCur()` brings the row in by the least amount (instant, never centred, only when off screen or under the sticky patient band; the Photos-page rule).
- **The row:** `phRqCardHtml` ranks the current row as focus > the row she is picking in (`phRqStay`) > the first unanswered row; the focus row wears the existing `.cur` look + `data-rq-cur` (so keys 1-9 / 0 act on it) and gets a **next** chip (`data-rq-next`) like a row she is picking in. Picking a chip in the focus row keeps the focus; picking in another row, **none of these**, **next**, **Fold** or **Undo** clears it.
- **The zone lights while its row is current:** `.on` = a brighter white ring and the tag on a deep `--ph-herb-deep` pill; `aria-pressed` mirrors it. No animation, so nothing to gate (the transition on `.ph-rq-zone` is the 10/19 one, off under reduced motion).
- Tested in the sandbox preview with a synthetic F patient (seeded face + full photos in `phRenPhotoCache`, real dispatched clicks and a real mouse tap, nothing saved): Eyes zone -> Eyes row current + zone lit + next chip; key "1" picks the first Eyes chip; Lips zone moves it; bare photo calls `phRenViewPhoto` with that photo; Face tag -> Face row; next clears; all six answered then Muscles zone unfolds the rows, Fold clears the focus; 375px: Skin zone scrolls the row just into view, no sideways scroll; no console errors. **NOT exercised:** a real patient, her phone, a real photo viewer open, reduced motion on a real device.

## Find the Ren: signs inside a subheading run mild to severe (her 2026-10-19 "organise the symptoms in ascending order, e.g. thin emaciated to robust then full figured, same for all categories"; mock first, then "yes, build it and push"; PUSHED LIVE 689a0fd3, build 20261019-1410000)
- `PH_RQ_SUBGROUPS` gained nine subjects (Abdominal, Bowels, Cold & heat, Energy, Fever, Mind & mood, Periods, Sex & fertility, Urine), each a list of `[subheading, [sign keys in order]]`; `PH_RQ_ORDER_IX` numbers every sign in insertion order. New `phSignCmpSpectrum` (after `phSignCmpSubjOrder`) keeps the SAME alphabetical subject order as `phSignCmp` but sorts same-subject signs by that ordinal; it is the fallback in `phFbrGroupedHtml`'s sort line, so every group orders its subjects the way it always did and only the signs inside a subject change. Only "Build, skin & complexion" still uses `sortSubj`/`phSignCmpSubjOrder` (the group-specific subject order). A sign no table lists sorts after the listed ones of its subject (alphabetical). **All orders are DRAFT, hers to correct in `PH_RQ_SUBGROUPS`.** A subject split across several areas (Abdominal, Mind & mood, Sex & fertility) needs no per-group wiring, the ordinals are global.


## To Order: a "Refill ▾" chip on formula cards (her 2026-10-20 "give option to do formula refill", screenshot of the Needs ordering column; LIVE on main (commit 98d86052, ancestor of origin/main), build 20261020-1460000)
No mock or questions (the Plan ▾ menu is an existing component, reused as the Inventory row already does). **My reading, disclosed:** a formula card (not a single herb) can be made in-house instead of ordered, so it also offers the Formula refill menu.
- **Which cards:** `phIsFormula(h)` and not stopped, in Needs ordering. Running low cards (`osLowCard`), To order cards (`osCard`, stage `toorder` only, not On the way) and the phone rows (`osmRow`, stage toorder / low). Herb cards show no chip. Sits between the main button and the ⏰ / ⋮.
- **One component:** `phRefillMenuHtml(h, opt)` gained an optional `opt = { word, now }`. `word` is the chip's own word with the planned week beside it ("Refill", "Refill · Wk 43", "Refill · snoozed"); `now` adds a first menu row "Make it now" (`data-ph-dash-refill-open`, the same hand-off as the Inventory row's "Make a refill", `phDashOpenRefill`, which now clears `phRefillMenuFor` on the way out). No `opt` = the old Plan ▾ everywhere else, unchanged. `osRefillOpt` is the one constant in `renderPhRestock`. The add / pick / snooze / remove handlers are unchanged. **A planned formula stays in the list** (planning is not ordering; the chip shows the week).
- **CSS:** `.ph-osb-act .ph-rp-menubtn, .ph-osm-act .ph-rp-menubtn` = 28px chip; `.ph-osm-act > .ph-rp-menuwrap + .ph-os-glyph { margin-left: 0 }` (the glyph's `margin-left: auto` would misplace it). No new media query.
- **Fixed in the same pass:** (1) the board repaint (`wrapEl.innerHTML`) put every scrolled column back to the top, so a tap on a card low in a column lost her place and orphaned a fresh menu; `keepColScroll` in `renderPhRestock` now restores each `.ph-osb-colbody` scrollTop (desktop; helps every board tap). (2) An outside tap closes the Refill menu on the restock tab. (3) The ⋮ and the Refill ▾ never open together (each handler clears the other's state). (4) `phRefillMenuFixPosition` now re-points the menu once more 120 ms later (`phRefillMenuPlace`): every page repaint rebuilds the title bar and the shell drops its action disc into it a few ms later (`.ph-tbmerge`, the bar grows ~19px), which put the menu over its own chip; this also covers the Plan ▾ menu on Inventory, Dashboard and Refill.
- **Known, NOT fixed (pre-existing):** that title bar growth is a ~19px layout shift on every repaint of every page (the fix would be to reserve the disc's space in `phShellHead`); a fixed menu stays put if the column is wheel-scrolled while it is open (same as the ⋮).
- Tested in the sandbox (synthetic profile, 21 low herbs, 10 of them formulas): chip on all 10 formula cards and none of the 11 herb cards; menu opens on screen under its chip (desktop 1500, phone 375) with the column scroll kept (1225 before and after); This week writes `refill` / `refillOn` and the chip reads "Refill · Wk 43", the card stays; Make it now lands on Formula refill with that formula as the no-recipe jar and the menu closed; outside tap closes; ⋮ and Refill never both open; phone rows fit 25-350px with no overflow, page scroll kept; no console errors; headless-Edge pictures of both widths. **NOT exercised:** a real patient, her phone, her real stock, Snooze 1 week and Pick a week… from this chip (same handlers as Inventory), the On the way stage (no chip by design).
