## Pulse chart: every tick dates it, each new day starts blank; Abdomen tension is the whole abdomen, chart larger (her 2026-10-20 "how does this chart get saved and dated?" -> "fix them all"; then "abdominal tension is all over, not just quadrants. make the chart larger."; LIVE on main (commit f65c3a72, pushed in 525ae50f))
**Pulse, how it is saved.** `rec.constitPulse = { pulse, pulseRef, hammer, at, hist }` is the ONE live chart; the dated copy lives in `rec.visitFnd[sessionId].pulse / pulseRef / hammer`, filled by `phExamMirrorToday` (same-day sessions only, and only once `phTodayDonePart(rec, "pulse")`, which reads `at`). Two things were wrong, both fixed:
- **Every write dates the chart.** `phHxConstitPulse(rec)` is the ONLY write door (grid, referred chips, every Shen-Hammer tick, the rate box); it stamps `p.at = Date.now()`. Before, only a grid tap stamped it, so a referred / Hammer / rate tick saved but was never "examined today" and never filed into the visit (the rate handler now also calls `phExamMirrorToday`).
- **A new day starts blank, last time dashed.** A chart last written on an EARLIER day is "stale" (`phHxConstitPulseStale`: `at` before today and it has marks, referred or Hammer content). The page then shows it BLANK with the last earlier-day chart dashed in its cells and a key line "Dashed · 19 Oct" (`phPulsePrior`: a visit's dated copy, an archived chart, or the stale live chart; read side, nothing written). The first write of the new day moves the stale chart into `p.hist` (kept, never deleted) and starts a blank one, so yesterday's marks are never filed as today's finding. **Render functions never mutate**: `phHxConstitPulseRead` is the read door.
- **Readers that follow `hist` (and the stale live chart, so a reader answers the same before and after today's first tap):** `phExamPast` (the history line, days with no visit row), `phVisitPrevPulse`, `phFbrChartWords` (Fang Bing Ren pulse words when no filed visit is newer), `phTsExamHist` (the Treatment table's pulse history), `phTodaySumPart` (a stale chart is "Nothing recorded", not today's finding). `phPulseHistLatest(rec)` returns the live chart while stale, else the newest archived one. A new reader of "the last pulse chart" must go through these, never `rec.constitPulse` alone.
- **My reading, disclosed:** "blank each day" was built because she said "fix them all" after I had offered to mock it first; she has not seen it. If she wanted last time to carry over as a starting point instead of dashed, change `phHxConstitPulseHtml`'s `p` line and `phHxConstitPulse`'s archive step together.
**Abdomen exam page.** (1) The Tension row is ALWAYS the whole abdomen (`data-visit-abd2="tension:<v>"`, tap again clears; it fills every zone). Tapping a zone adds a second row **Zone** (`tensionAt:<v>`, only that zone), and the region line's "whole abdomen" link became a **Done** chip. The old rule "a second different tension arms a brush" is gone (a zone is set on the Zone row); a brush for marks (water, gas ...) is unchanged. **My reading of "tension is all over", disclosed, open to her correction.** (2) The chart is bigger: viewBox is now `20 0 80 150` (the trunk is only x 27-93, so the old 120-wide box was half empty), the exam chart is 250 x 470 desktop / 170 x 319 phone (was 280 x 350 / 150 x 188), names read 9-12px. Every OTHER place keeps its old height and loses the dead width (86 x 162, 56 x 105, mini 29 x 55). The neutral zone fills are paler so a "soft" reading shows against them (`ROWFILL`). (3) Temp / Sounds / Feel bars were clipping their labels when the right column narrowed: they are now `.ph-tng2-seg.wrap` (one joined box that wraps, dividers = each chip's left and bottom edge, the wrapper's -1px margins hide the box edge doubling).
- Tested in the sandbox (preview + synthetic patients, nothing saved): pulse stale -> blank + dashed + untouched record, grid tap / referred tap / rate tick each archive then stamp today, rate filed into today's visit, yesterday's visit copy untouched, readers agree before and after, no console errors; Abdomen at 1280 and 375 px (250x470 / 170x319, no sideways scroll, bars wrap with nothing outside), real clicks on the Tension and Zone chips. NOT exercised: a real patient, her phone, reduced motion on a real device. The Tongue and Abdomen standing charts probably carry over day to day the same way the pulse chart did (`rec.tongueExam`, `rec.abdomen.chart`): unchecked, unchanged.


## Session grid on a cycle plan: plan a phase by cycle days or a number of visits (her 2026-10-20 "this format is not working for me. i cant change that the treatment is once a week for menses", screenshot of the Care Plan Session grid with Menstruation open; LIVE on main, build 20261020-1520000)
Her screenshot: a cycle plan's open phase (Menstruation, CD 1-5) offered only 1x/wk, 2x/wk, Fortnightly, Monthly, so "once a week" could not be changed to a day or a visit count a 5-day phase can use. Four questions, all her picks (do not re-ask): Menstruation gets BOTH "specific cycle days" (tap CD chips, squares dated from her real period) AND "a number of visits" (a - N + stepper, no weekly rhythm); applies to EVERY phase of a cycle plan (follicular, ovulation, two-week wait too); a non-cycle plan keeps the four weekly choices unchanged; the pills wrap to two rows (all choices visible, one tap), not a dropdown.
- **The cadence string stays the one truth.** A cycle-day plan is `phase.cadence = "CD 3, 5 acupuncture · herbs"` (`phTpCadenceCompose(0, "cd", forWhat, null, cds)`) and `phase.sessions = cds.length` (`phTpCadenceCdList`, `phTpSessionsOf`). The Treatment tab and Communications already read "CD ..." lists; nothing new is stored. `phTpRhKeyOf` is null for a CD list, so the rhythm pills show none picked.
- **Cycle days exist only where `phTpCdCtx(name, plan)` gives a window** (Natural fertility, PCOS, Endometriosis, Dysmenorrhea with a tracked cycle and a logged period; the same gate as the CD chips). `phTpPlanCdAvail(cdx, ph)` = the window's days; `gone` = today's CD is inside the window and the day is already past (dimmed, disabled, "Already passed this cycle"). `phTpCdDates(name, cds, from, after, count)` dates a CD list from `rec.cycle.lmp + c*cycleLen + cd - 1`, this cycle or the next (a window entirely past reads the next cycle: tested, CD 1 on 23 Oct with the LMP 28 Sep is Mon 26 Oct). `phTpCdSpread(days, n)` = n visits spread evenly down the days (`days[floor(i*L/n)]`, n clamped 1..L, so a one-day window gives one visit however many squares are tapped).
- **Pills** (`phTpSessionLadderHtml`): one joined wrapping box (`.ph-tng2-seg.wrap`, chips get their left + bottom divider) using the whole phase cell; **Cycle days** is the fifth pill and shows only when the phase has a window. Two rows from a ~165px cell up, three rows below that (a 375px phone or a narrow Course column), never clipped. A phase of a week or less with no rhythm of its own previews **cycle days** by default; a phase with its own rhythm (a follicular template's 1x/wk) keeps it and she taps Cycle days.
- **Open phase, Cycle days:** a panel row under it (`tr.dd.cdp`, `phTpPlanCdOpenHtml`): "Cycle day" chips (`data-tp-plan-cd`, picked = solid, draft in `phTpPlanUi.cd`, view state), a "Visits" stepper (`data-tp-plan-cdn`, spreads that many down the open days) and the page's one solid "Plan" button (`data-tp-plan-cdgo`, `phTpPlanCommit(..., "cd", n, cds)`). Tapping a dashed slot commits the draft's first N days, else N spread. "Same for all phases" is hidden in this mode (cycle days belong to one phase). **Planned phase:** the dropdown's "Other..." panel has the same Cycle days segment; chips and stepper write at once (`phTpPlanWrite({cd})`), the planned pill reads `CD 3, 5` (up to four days) or `CD a-b · n`. Undo (the one toast) restores cadence and sessions.
- **Dating:** `phTpSessionCells` dates a CD cadence's next squares from `phTpCdDates` (after the last real visit, strictly increasing), not by stepping 7 days; `phTpPlanSlotDates` does the same for the preview. A "1 visits" label now reads "1 visit".
- **CSS** by the `.ph-tpg-rhs` rules: `.ph-tpg-rhs { display: block }`, `.ph-tng2-seg.wrap .ph-tpg-p3.rh` (padding 2px 5px, nowrap), `.seg.cdd button`, `.gone`. No new animation, no new media query.
- **Disclosed:** the default to Cycle days applies only to a phase of 7 days or less with no rhythm of its own; "Same for all phases" does not take cycle days; `phTpProjectedVisitsForCds` (Treatment tab projections) was left as it was and still skips days up to today.
- Tested in the sandbox (preview + a synthetic Natural fertility patient, 1280 / 700 / 560 / 480 / 375 px, deleted after): pills wrap two rows at 620px and up and never clip; chip toggles, stepper spread (3 -> CD 6, 8, 10; 4 -> CD 6, 7, 9, 11), Plan commit, planned squares and dates (~23 / ~24 / ~26 / ~28 Oct), toast + Undo, slot-tap commit, the planned phase's Other... panel writing at once, a next-cycle window, a Neck & shoulder plan unchanged (four pills, no Cycle days, Same for all + Undo), no console errors. NOT exercised: a real patient, her phone, the Treatment tab's own copy of the grid, reduced motion on a real device.

## To Order: the desktop board is 3 columns, animated (her 2026-10-20 "improve this design ui. it has the features i need but its not easy to read, not spaced out well, and needs interactive animated elements to improve workflow. make mock"; mock `order-board-mocks-2026-10-20.html`; commit e49cdda6 on `session-a`, LIVE on main (its tree reached origin/main via its pre-amend sibling 0dbef951, pushed by a concurrent session; the CLAUDE.md note followed on her "push live" 2026-10-20), build 20261020-1530000)
Scope = the desktop To Order board only (`renderPhRestock`, `isDesk = window.innerWidth > 900`). The phone layout (<=900px: `osmRow` / `osmBand` / `osmSupGroups`, the phone `shellHtml` with its stat strip) is BYTE-FOR-BYTE untouched, her explicit pick. Mock approved, **her 8 picks (all Recommended + the drag option):** 1 Layout **A - Rows**; 2 **remove the stat strip** on desktop (the counts move into the column heads); 3 **fill the window** (the three columns stretch to the viewport, each scrolls inside its own `--ph-osb-h` height, the page itself barely scrolls); 4 **+ Add to order in the Needs-ordering head** (`phOsAddPick`, a search box that slides the herb into the list); 5 motion = **Arrived flies across** (the row lifts, a ghost clone slides to the top of Arrived and lands with a gold wash, the head counts + money roll; "All arrived" staggers 140 ms apart) + **jar and bar fill in on landing** (0.7 s, the % counts up) + **Add slides into the list** + **drag a row between columns** (mouse; the buttons still work); 6 **drop the supplier dot**; 7 **two lines, as the mock**; 8 **one toast with Undo** (`presSavedToast`).
- **Three columns:** **Needs ordering** (To order grouped by supplier + Running low merged in, same action, + the Snoozed strip, the gold cover-% bar), **On the way** (grouped by supplier, Copy for email / All arrived / per-card Arrived, the bottle-count fact), **Arrived** (last 14 days, "+N older, not yet settled" reveal, never drops anything). Rows `.ph-osb-row.{low|toorder|ordered|arrived|snz}[data-ph-os-row=id][data-ph-os-drag="id:stage"]`; colbodies `.ph-osb-colbody[data-ph-os-drop=toorder|ordered|arrived]`; heads `[data-osb-n]` / `[data-osb-money]`.
- **Every existing interaction reuses its own `data-ph-*` attribute**, moved from a `<table>` row into a `<div>` card -- no handler change for mark ordered / arrived, the Refill / status menus, snooze, supplier picker, copy-for-email, bulk mark/arrived, arrival edit/undo. New engine: `phOsbMove(ids, to, srcRows)` (write FIRST via `phOsbWrite`, then the fly animation, then a scoped `renderPhRestock` + scroll the landed row into its colbody), `phOsbFillIn` (the jar `.ph-os-jar .fill` scaleY from pWas/pNow + `.ph-stockbar-fill` width + `.ph-level-pct` count-up), `phOsbRolls` / `phOsbRoll` (head counts + money), `phOsbFit` (the `--ph-osb-h` column height from the board top and the footer) / `phOsbTween`, `phOsAddPick`.
- **Motion engine, guarded:** `phOsbTween` short-circuits in a hidden tab and carries a `setTimeout(ms + 80)` guard so the counts always settle even when rAF barely fires; everything is off under `phTpReduceMotion()` (the reduced-motion CSS covers `.ph-osb-row.glow`, `.ph-osb-rw.gone`, `.ph-osb-fly`, `.ph-osb-exp`, `.ph-osb-colbody`, `.ph-osb-lvl .ph-stockbar-fill`); the data write is `phOsbWrite` / `phArrive(list, {noRender:true})` (the new `opts.noRender`) and never waits on an animation. Flies append to `#pharmacyPage`, not `<body>`.
- **Disclosed judgment calls:** (1) "All arrived" on a supplier group moves only that group's ORDERED rows -- `herbNeedsOrder` ignores `h.ordered`, so the shared handler would otherwise sweep the supplier's still-to-order herbs into Arrived; the board path filters `h.ordered` (the phone path is unchanged). (2) "Back to To order" keeps the herb's existing order date (`orderOn` is only stamped when it newly joins the list). (3) the To-order row buttons are outlined per the mock; the "Order now" red-lined button (`.red`) shows only on a zero jar (zero = `--ph-zero`, the status red, never gold). (4) columns 2 and 3 get a plain static accent strip, not a live percentage bar (there is no honest "on the way" / "arrived" percentage; only Needs ordering has the real cover %).
- **Tested** in the sandbox (preview at 1280 and 1600 px, synthetic stamps on `PHARMACY.herbs`, restored from a full `zz-osb-snap` snapshot after, viewport reset): the board height fills to the footer with no page scroll, each column scrolls inside; a single Arrived flies and lands in Arrived with the glow, the jar fills to 100% (verified end to end: inline `transform: scaleY(1)` with the 0.7 s cubic transition, pWas 20% -> pNow 100% count-up, "+200g" toast, row class `arrived glow`) and the On-the-way bottle-count fact reads right; "All arrived" staggers; "Mark all ordered"; "Order now" on a low row; drag (dropok on the allowed columns, dropno otherwise); "+ Add to order"; reduced motion (immediate repaint, no flies); Undo on every path restores the data and the log; no new console errors. The earlier "jar fill-in did not animate" note was a stale / concurrent-render-disturbed reading -- a clean run animates correctly (the `.ph-os-jar .fill` selector matches; the `scaleY(0.2)` a getComputedStyle reports is an SVG `transform-box: view-box` quirk, the inline `scaleY(1)` is what paints and the fill rect renders full `height=16`).
- **NOT exercised:** a real patient, her real stock, her phone (desktop only), a real mouse drag, reduced motion on a real device, a 3-column board at the widest width with several real suppliers in one On-the-way group.

## Musculoskeletal page: "B · Picture row" (her 2026-10-20 "when i add muscles for knee, diagram too small, the overall layout takes up too much space, tesselate it better so its a cohesive workflow ... make it so information is not redundant. make a mock before building"; mock sheet `msk-mock-sheet.html` in the session scratchpad (Today / A / B); her 12 picks then "build and push"; build 20261020-1560000)
**Her 12 picks (do not re-ask):** B picture row (she first typed "make 2 columns", then "i meant to pick b"); tap a muscle belly on the diagram = add / remove it from the plan, side from the leg tapped; ONE add door (the Add pain box gets an **Areas ▾** button, the "1 pain · + Pick areas" row is gone); "+ Likely cause" is a **+ Cause** chip in the muscle footer beside "+ Muscle for knee", the Caused-by row shows only once a cause is linked; the **All pains** row is hidden for a single pain; ★ folded into the Pain cell AND the header's second line dropped; the view controls (Front + back / Turn / Side / 3D, Labels, Enlarge, ⓘ) stack vertically beside the diagram; the colour caption, the "⤢ Diagram" button and "usually treated by hand" are gone ("Deep" alone); diagram 290px tall; the Palpation / Examination card untouched; motion = new muscle line eases in + belly fades to maroon + diagram cross-fades on a region change, all off under reduced motion, never on load.
- **Root cause of "too small":** the old `PH_MSK_REGION_YRANGE.knee = [268, 320]` cropped a 220 × 52 strip (108 × 29px on screen). Now **`PH_MSK_REGION_CROP`** = a real x/y viewBox per region (knee `50 248 120 147`), drawn 290px tall (`.mk-dgsvg { height: 290px }`, the svg `height: 100%; width: auto`, so a crop is 237px wide).
- **Picture row** (`phPainMapHtml`'s mskCtx branch, `.ph-pm.mk .mk-picrow`): `.mk-picfig` (the pain stage, 290px) | `.mk-picdg[data-mk-dg=<region>]` ("KNEE · MUSCLES" + `phMskDiagramPanesHtml(rk, rec, plan0, true)` = front + back + the Deep line) | `.mk-picctl` (128px column: the layout segment, Labels, Enlarge, ⓘ). The table is full width under it. **Widths:** 290 + 12 + (237 + 10 + 237) + 12 + 128 = 926px, so it fits the 930px `.ph-pm` of a 1280 window WITH a 15px scrollbar (the first cut used 14px gaps = 934 and the back pane wrapped under the front one; measured, fixed). `@container (max-width: 925px)` wraps the row (controls become a horizontal row), `(max-width: 560px)` draws the crops 220px tall. **3D** is unchanged: it still returns its own full view (no picture row, no muscle diagram) — the diagram lives in the three flat views.
- **Which region the diagram shows** = `phMskDgReg` (view state): `phMskDgRegFor(list)` normalises it on EVERY `phPainMapHtml` render (the first pain's region unless the current one is still on the map, so a removed pain can never leave a stale region). A tap on a pain row (`tr.ph-pm-tr[data-mk-reg]`, read by `phMskDgFollow` from the pain-map click handler when the box sits in `[data-msk-pain]`) swaps the diagram in place (`phMskDgRefresh(true)`: only `[data-mk-dg]` is repainted, `.swap` = `mkDgSwap` .3s, added only on a change). `phMskDgSync` recolours the bellies (`.on`, `aria-pressed`) in the page AND an open Enlarge clone after every plan write.
- **Belly tap** = `data-msk="dg-tap" data-v="<muscleId>:<R|L>"` on every `.mk-dgm` path (role button, `<title>` "Quadriceps · Right · on the plan"); `phMskClick`'s `dg-tap` branch toggles it through `phMskPlanAdd` / `phMskPlanRemove` (technique copied from the other side when that side is on the plan, else "mp"), sets `phMskMuFresh` for a NEW muscle so its line eases in (`.mk-mu.in`, `mkMuIn`), then `phMskSyncDraft` + `phMskDgSync` + `phPmTblRefresh`. One line per muscle with R / L toggles (the 10/14 row), so a second side lights the toggle on the existing line.
- **Enlarge** (`figbig`) clones the whole `.mk-picrow` (minus `.mk-picctl`) into `#phRfModal` as `.ph-hx-card.ph-rfx.ph-mskx` (`.mk-dgsvg` 400px there, `.ph-pm-spot` pointer-events none); the bellies in the clone are live (same `data-msk="dg-tap"`, `phMskDgSync` recolours both).
- **Table** (`phPmTableHtml`): thead `Pain · Side · Detail · Diagnosis · How long (· Now · Best · Worst)` — the Main column is gone, the ★ (`.mcp`) sits first inside `.ph-pm-nm` **on every pain table that uses this function** (the Care Plan tile and Patient details too, for consistency — disclosed); sub rows `<td></td><td colspan>`; main rows carry `data-mk-reg`; the cause row is pushed only when `causes.length || causeOpen`; `phMskPainMusclesHtml(..., extra)` prints the + Cause chip in its footer (a pain with no muscle block gets a footer-only `tr.mu`); the All pains row + its strips only when `list.length > 1`; `phMskHeadHtml` prints the h2 alone (no `.mk-sub`). Removed: `phMskDiagramHtml`, `phMskDiagramOpen`, the `diagram` act, `.mk-dgbtn`, `.mk-mudiagram`, `.mk-dgsub`, `PH_MSK_REGION_YRANGE`.
- **Known / disclosed:** at phone width the page keeps its pre-existing shape (the 290px stage overflows the 246px card by 44px beside the rail, the table scrolls sideways inside its card — desktop-first page; the document itself has no sideways scroll at 375); the Patient details page is now the hero + 3 cards page (`.ph-pd`) and draws no pain map, so the ★ fold there was checked only on the Care Plan tile; a computed `fill` read on a just-tapped belly in the hidden Browser pane reports the pre-transition grey (the throttled-transition trap already in Traps) — with the transition off it reads maroon.
- Sandbox-tested at 1280 (synthetic "Zz Msk Mock", deleted after): the row fits with no wrap, every crop renders with taps, a real belly tap adds / removes (plan, line, aria, fill), a new muscle's line eases in, Enlarge opens the row with live bellies and closes, Turn / Side / 3D / back still work, a second pain (hip, via Areas) shows the All pains row and a row tap cross-fades the diagram, removing it normalises the region and hides the row, no console errors beyond the pre-existing offline 404; 375: no document overflow, the controls row wraps, 220px crops. **NOT exercised:** a real patient, her phone, reduced motion on a real device, the Care Plan tile's ★ with real data.

## Profile search: a box in the patient header band (her 2026-10-20 "include a search box so i can quickly search inside profile e.g. surgery pain etc"; mock `profile-search-mock-2026-10-20.html`, her "make mock" then 8 popup picks; LIVE on main)
**Her 8 picks (do not re-ask):** (1) both: typing drops a results list under the header grouped by page; tapping a row opens its page, marks every match and rings that line, and a find strip (`‹ n of N ›`, page chips, ✕) steps through the rest; (2) it searches everything: Came in for, pain table, surgeries, history and notes, visits / re-checks, plan text, Fang Bing Ren signs, supplements, bloods, photos; (3) related words count (surgery finds arthroscopy / repair; pain finds aching / sharp) and the related word is the one marked; (4) desktop: the field is always in the band beside the camera and grows on focus (230 to 360px); (5) phone: a magnifier disc opens a full-width box in place of the name; (6) the rail shows a match count per page while searching, gone when cleared; (7) keyboard: `/` jumps to the box, up / down walk results, Enter opens, Esc closes the panel then clears, Enter steps to the next match once a page is open (Shift+Enter back); (8) result groups run in rail order (File, Exam, Plan, Log).
- **Where:** `#presPanel` only (a patient script, not a formula). Field = `phPsFieldHtml()` in the band template of `renderPresPanelCore`, results `#phPsRes`, find strip `#phPsFind`, both children of the band; `phPsAfterRender()` runs at the end of that render. The block sits just above `renderPresPanel`: `PH_PS_ALIAS` (related words), `PH_PS_GROUPS`, `phPsBuild` (one `add(pages, group, text, meta, alias, ringText)` per indexed fact), `phPsIndex` / `phPsMatches` / `phPsRenderRes` / `phPsRenderFind` / `phPsBadges` / `phPsMark` / `phPsRingApply` / `phPsNavigate`, and delegated input / focus / click / capture-phase keydown listeners. **Nothing is ever saved**; state is module vars (`phPsS` keyed by the open script) because the band is rebuilt on every panel repaint.
- **Pages** come from the rail itself (`phPsPages`, keys `p:<id>` profile, `g:<id>` plan page, `s:<stage>`). Plan-dependent pages (Musculoskeletal, Tongue, Pulse, Abdomen, Ask her, Cycle, Timeline) only exist once a plan is open, so each item carries a candidate list `pg` ending in an always-present fallback (the pain table is `["g:msk","g:plan"]`), `it.page` = the first one in the rail now, and the id is hashed from `pg[0]` so a result keeps its name when the rail gains pages.
- **Marks and ring:** `mark.ph-ps-mark` on every text match (a MutationObserver re-applies them after any repaint, debounced 70 ms, `takeRecords()` after its own writes), `.ph-ps-ring` (2px `--ph-herb`) on the line the tapped result came from, found by its own text with the commas / spaces stripped (a page that splits points into chips has no contiguous text), climbing out to the whole line; the pain table rings its row's first cell (`reg` = the pain's region, `tr.ph-pm-tr[data-mk-reg]`); a plan phase that is not open shows only its name, so the ring lands on the phase name (`it.alt`). Scroll is the least amount, below the band.
- **Pinned while a search is on:** the panel's `overflow: hidden` made it the band's scroll container, so the sticky band never pinned. While the query has words (`.ph-ps-pin`) the panel uses `overflow: clip; display: flow-root` (scoped with `:has(.ph-pres-band.ph-ps-pin)`, not on the one-screen Dispense), so the box, results and find strip stay in view. Clearing the search (✕) puts the band back to its normal, non-sticky self.
- **Phone:** inside the one 640px block: the field is a 28px gold-ringed disc; the find strip is count + stepper on the first row and the page chips scroll sideways on the second (a single-page search hides the chip bar), so the pinned strip is about 84px and the band about 134px.
- **Disclosed:** `rec.medicalHistoryNotes` (the free-text notes) is NOT searched: no box shows it since the dead-code sweep, so a hit would land on nothing. The first Pain-table tap before a plan is open lands on the Care Plan figure (that table only has its own page once a plan is open). A phase's text that is not on screen is reached by its name. Related words are a small draft list (`PH_PS_ALIAS`), hers to correct.
- **Trap met:** the CSS block was swept into another session's commit before this one (HEAD briefly held the styles with no script); this commit carries the script, the band markup and the last CSS edits.
- Tested in the sandbox preview (synthetic patient "Zz Search Test", deleted after) at 1440 and 375: results grouped and counted, rail badges, a tap opens its page and rings one line, the stepper walks every match, retyping while scrolled keeps the band, Esc order, no console errors; a sweep of every result for four queries (18 + 11 + 3 + 7 hits) leaves exactly one ringed line each. **NOT exercised:** a real patient, a real phone, reduced motion on a real device, photo and blood results (the synthetic patient has none).

## Care Plan = the findings beside the plan (her 2026-10-20 synth22 item 1, merged mock D; "ok done. build and push"; PUSHED LIVE 1516c21f, build 20261021-1580000)
Her words: "right now i hardly touch it. i focus more on the assessments and findings before i get to the treatment plan ... is there a way to integrate the plan to show all the findings so i can use that to develop/design the treatment plan cohesively? i want a full redesign". Her picks across the mock rounds ended in merged mock D ("Yes, this is the design"); do not re-ask. **Supersedes the Case column and band gauges of "Care Plan page 'Case | Course'" above.**
- **Layout** (`presTpPlanTabBodyHtml`, P12 of the patch): band = title + stage pill + ⋯ only (`phTpHeroHtml` is no longer called there; `phTlSideHtml` no longer drawn: both left as dead code, the Timeline page is still on the rail). Under it `.ph-tp-cc-cols.ph-tp-fxcols[data-tp-fxcols=<planId>][data-tp-fx-name]` = `svg.ph-tp-fxlk` (the lines) + `.ph-tp-cc-fx` (Findings, 270-320px) + `.ph-tp-cc-plan` (Diagnosis card, Session grid, then scans + pain map / Also-treating tiles as `.ph-tp-cc-more`). Side by side from 1280px; below that they stack (findings first) and the lines stay off.
- **Findings engine** (block just above `phTpLiveCtxRowHtml`): `phTpFxCtx(name, plan)` → `phTpFxRows` builds Came in for, Ren, Tongue, Pulse, Abdomen (`phTpFxExamRow`), Cycle (only when `phCycleTracked`), Ask her, Blood tests, History, Musculoskeletal (pain patients). **Each line is its own try/catch**: one odd record drops only its own line, never the column. Every row reads the app's own renderer (`phTongueChartSvg`, `phAbdChartSvg`, the pulse sprite, `phCcRibItems`, `phBldList`, ...), never a second drawing. Purple dot = changed since last visit; a finding not done today shows last visit's value, dated and greyed. Render never writes; the saved selection and the formula % paint after it draws (`setTimeout(phTpFxAfter)`).
- **Tap a row** (`data-tp-fx-f="planId|rowId"`, `phTpFxClick`): its drawer opens (today vs last visit, bigger chart, **Open <page>** = `data-flow-prof`, **+ Watch / + Aim**), the others dim, and lines + dots draw to the diagnosis chips and the Fang Bing Ren row it feeds (`phTpFxDraw`, paths measured from the DOM; bails when stacked). **+ Watch / + Aim** append "; <Finding>: <text>" to the live phase's Watch / Aim (`phTpLivePhase`), a chip flies, the button reads "✓ Watch", toast with Undo (`presSavedToast`); tap again takes it off. Tapping a diagnosis chip (`data-tp-fx-dx`) lights the findings behind that pattern; tapping the formula row lights the findings that feed it. Tap again clears. The clue -> pattern and sign -> finding mappings that draw the lines, and which Session-grid phase a finding tints (`tr.fxlit`, `data-tp-phid`), are **MY DRAFT**.
- **Diagnosis card** (P2, `phTpFocusGoalHtml` non-modal): heading "Diagnosis"; `.ph-tp-dxgrp` "Likely" / "Possible" joined bars (`.ph-tp-dxseg`, ★ on the main pattern when there are several, ✕ = Not this patient); "Taken off · N ▸" holds the ghosts (`phTpCcUi.dxMore`); Edit / Open Patterns / Open Fang Bing Ren; then `phTpFxFmRowHtml` (the leading formula: colour dot, name, %, track, the Bing/Ren agree line); then Goal. The full-screen plan window still draws the older form.
- **Editing on the plan:** Aim / Points / Watch as before, and Formula is now a `data-tp-edit=":formulaName"` cell (P6-P8, P10): a typed name is hers (`formulaId` cleared, a `formulaHistory` entry `{typed:true}`, rolled back on a failed save), "→ Fill" still fills from Fang Bing Ren without opening the editor. "Edit on Treatment" is gone. An Aim / Watch commit calls `phTpFxRefreshAll()` (P9, anchored right after `el.classList.remove("editing")`, before any early return), as do `phTpFgRefresh` and `phTpPlanRepaint`.
- **Motion** (CSS block after the `.ph-tp-hero.ph-tp-slim` media rule): lines draw, dots appear, the drawer opens, the fly; all transient classes, none on a base class, and all off under reduced motion.
- **Concurrent trap met:** while this was being committed another session was rewriting the same Aim / Watch cell editor (Aim / Watch "write themselves from the record", `phTpFieldCellHtml` / `phTpFieldCommit`). The patch anchors were moved so they apply to both versions. **Open, for whoever lands that work:** + Watch / + Aim write `live.watch` / `live.aim` directly; once Aim / Watch can be auto text, those taps should go through `phTpFieldCommit` (or the auto text first), or a tap on an auto Watch makes it "hers" with only the appended line.
- Tested in the sandbox (synthetic "Zz Fx Test", Natural fertility plan, deleted after): 9 rows, drawer + lines, dx and formula lighting, + Watch / Undo / take off, ✕ and the ghost, → Fill, typed formula, Edit diagnosis, Open Tongue, 1440 / 1280 / 1100 (stacked, lines off) / 375 (no document overflow), no console errors. **NOT exercised:** a real patient, her phone, reduced motion on a real device.


## Watch and Aim write themselves from the patient (her 2026-10-20 "i want the templates watch to be from chief complaints and secondary complaints and findings e.g. cycle etc, aim needs to link as well... aim - i can preset"; guide `watch-aim-link-guide-2026-10-20.html`; commit fdcf1dc0, PUSHED LIVE 2026-10-21 in build 20261021-1600000)
**Her 8 picks (do not re-ask):** Watch sources = chief complaint, secondary complaints, cycle, other findings; the template's own Watch text is **"Red flags"**, always first, fixed per template phase; wording lives in ONE Settings sheet "Watch wording" beside Goal wording; chips carry a source tag (Red flag, Main, Also, Cycle, finding name) and the same chips tick on the Treatment tab; **Aim = her preset per template phase with tokens**; tokens are tap chips (no curly brackets typed); typing her own Watch / Aim on a plan makes it hers with a "back to auto" chip; live on every plan, derived on read.
- **Block** `Watch + Aim written from the patient`, just above `phTpFocusGoalHtml`: `var` tables (`PH_WATCH_MAIN/WORDS/CYCLE/CYCLE_KEYS/HX/TAG`, `PH_AIM_NEUTRAL`, `PH_AIM_TOKENS`) so a boot-time read never hits the TDZ, then `phWatchItems(name, plan)` (main, each secondary, cycle, findings; 300 ms memo `phWatchMemo`, NOT busted by `phTpAutoBust`: reset `phWatchMemo.m` in a test), `phTpAimResolve` / `phTpAimNeutral`, `phTpFieldView(name, plan, ph, f)` -> `{hers, auto, text, flags, items}` (the ONE read), `phTpFieldCommit`, `phTpFieldCellHtml` (the cell), `phTpFieldBackToAuto`, `phWatchChipsHtml`, `phWatchWordSettingsHtml` (`PHARMACY.watchWording = { main, cycle, rules, find }`, her lines first).
- **Aim tokens** `{main}` `{sec}` `{cyc}` are stored in the TEMPLATE only; the editor shows `[main complaint]` etc. (`phAimTokToDisplay` / `phAimTokFromDisplay`), the Aim row has three chips (`data-tp-mgr-aimtok="id:idx:tok"`, insert at the caret, write at once) and the Watch row is labelled "Red flags". A token with nothing behind it drops out with its joining word. **A new plan stores a TOKEN-FREE Aim** (`phTpNewPlan` -> `phTpAimNeutral`), so nothing that reads raw `ph.aim` ever shows a bracket.
- **Hers vs auto** (`phTpFieldHersText`): hers when `ph[f+"Hers"] === true`, else when the stored text differs from the template phase's text, its neutral form AND the shipped built-in phase text (`phTpBuiltinPhaseOf`); a plan with no template phase whose Aim still carries tokens writes itself. Consequence, disclosed: an OLD plan whose stored text differs from both the current and the shipped template text (e.g. made after a template override changed) reads as hers and frozen, with a "back to auto" chip.
- **Cycle chips** only for a woman who tracks a cycle (`phCycleTracked`), never a man, a pregnant patient, or an IVF / Cycle & IVF / Pregnancy plan (`phWatchCycleOk`); a late period reads "Period N days late".
- **Routed through** the live strip (`phTpLiveCtxRowHtml`, Watch spans the row), both Planned cells, the hero line, Treatment tab chips (`phAcuWatchItems`; a tick stores the chip text in `presAcuWatchChecked` / `observedSigns`), the plan-to-ask box, print doc, spine row, patient letter (`phTpWatchFixedText`: red flags only, never today's chips), profile search, the template band, the plan's Apply-the-usual. **Deliberately NOT routed:** "Also treating" shadow-phase tile rows, the appointment brief `phBit`, save-as-template copies, library-sourced detour copies.
- **CSS** `.ph-wch*` (tag chips; red flag uses `--ph-zero-*`, cycle purple, Also blue, findings paper), `.ph-wch-src` (the tag inside a Treatment-tab chip), `.ph-ww-h`, `.ph-tp-mgr-aimcell/aimtoks/aimtok`; no animation added.
- **Known / disclosed:** all wording is Claude-drafted, DRAFT, hers to correct on the "Watch wording" sheet; `PHARMACY.watchWording` has no cloud-merge protection (same as `goalWording`); finding wording is only overridable through the "Finding words" box; the template editor still shows Aim / Red flags only in Detailed mode (Build 2 removes the switch); the Treatment-tab chip splitter (`phAcuWatchChips`) breaks a long red-flag sentence at dashes and semicolons, as before.
- Tested in the sandbox preview (synthetic "Zz Wa Test", deleted after): chips and tags, empty-token drop, hers -> back to auto, a man / IVF / Pregnancy plan gets no cycle chip, a Natural fertility plan does, editor token chip writes `{cyc}`, typed brackets convert back to tokens, print doc and letter carry no braces, 691px and 375px with no overflow, no console errors. NOT exercised: a real patient, her phone, the Treatment tab's own render with a tick, reduced motion on a real device.

## Old acupuncture points cleared from her saved template copies (her 2026-10-21 "yes clear old acu points"; commit be4b5b5b, PUSHED LIVE with Build 4 on her "push live", build 20261021-1600000)
Follows [the built-in Points emptying of 2026-10-20, `4ae54304`]: her own edits of the built-in templates (`tpTemplateOverrides` copies whole `phases`) and her own saved templates (`tpTemplates`) still carried the old points, and she answered yes to clearing them.
- **`phMigrateTemplatePointsClear`** (boot IIFE beside `phMigrateLabelObjectiveLine`): runs once. Empties `points` on every phase of an override whose edit stamp (`tpTemplateOverridesAt[id]`) is older than the cut-off (a stamp of NOW is written, so the cloud merge's newer-edit-wins keeps the cleared copy) and of a saved template whose id's embedded base-36 timestamp (`uid()`) is older than the cut-off. **Nothing she types after the build ships is touched**: a newer stamp or a newer id is skipped. `pressPoints`, Aim, Watch and every other field stay; plans already made keep their points; the built-ins were already empty.
- **Flag and backup in one:** `PHARMACY.tpPointsCleared = { at, templates, phases, was: { "<templateId or user:<id>>": [[phase label, removed text], ...] } }`. Set only when the device holds at least one override or saved template (an empty new device stays unflagged so a later cloud pull of old copies is still cleared). `mergeTemplateOverrides` or-merges it like `tpTemplateOverridesMigrated`. One toast on the first boot says how many were cleared ("Old points cleared · 2 templates, 2 phases"); nothing shows when there was nothing to clear.
- **Disclosed:** a stale device that is not updated yet and wins a whole-blob conflict can bring old copies back with their old stamps; the flag then blocks a second clear on that device (restore from a backup file re-runs it, as the restored data has no flag). `PHARMACY.tpPointsCleared` carries the removed text, so a mistake is recoverable by hand from the data until she deletes it.
- Tested in the preview with seeded made-up data: an old override cleared (pressPoints and other fields kept, stamp refreshed), a newer-edit override left alone, an old saved template cleared, a new-id saved template left alone, flag + backup written, toast shown, no console errors.

## To Order column 1: C · Switch (her 2026-10-20 "this screen shakes too much when i select something ... when i select add to order list, it moves things to the top ... i want it more organised and fixed areas for order and low level", then "when i click add to order, dont move the screen, stay where i am"; mock `order-col1-mocks-2026-10-20.html` tab C; "build and then push"; PUSHED LIVE 9bbc8345, build 20261021-1610000)
The shake (the title bar rebuilt on every desktop repaint) was fixed first, e3dbbdcd. **Her 11 picks are in memory `project_pharmacy_order_col1_switch_2026_10_20`; do not re-ask.** Desktop board only (`renderPhRestock`, `isDesk`); phone To Order untouched.
- **Switch:** Needs ordering holds `.ph-osb-segrow > .ph-osb-seg[role=tablist]` (To order `data-ph-osb-side="toorder" data-osb-tabdrop`, Running low `data-ph-osb-side="low"`, counts `data-osb-n="list"` / `"low"`) and ONE `.ph-osb-colbody[data-osb-side]` showing that side. View state `phOsbSide` (null = pick again: To order unless it is empty), `phOsbSideScroll` (each side's own scroll, restored on every repaint). Both reset when she arrives on the page from another one (`phRestockOnTab` in `renderPharmacy`'s dispatcher), never on a board action.
- **A to Z, fixed spots** (`phByName`). **Nothing scrolls a column** on any board move (the old `phOsbMove` scrolled to the landed row). `phOsbMove(ids, to, srcRows)`: write first (`phOsbWrite`), then the row lifts (`.out`, or `.lift` when it flies to the tab), the gap closes, it lands in its A-Z spot with the gold wash (`.ph-osb-rw.grow`); a landed row out of view is not chased. Add / drag from Running low to To order flies into the TAB (`.ph-osb-fly` shrinks onto it), the tab lights gold for ~2 s from landing (`phOsbTabLit`, `.lit`), pings once (`phOsbTabPing`, `.ping`) and the count rolls (`phOsbRolls`). `phOsAddPick` (+ Add to order) lights the tab the same way.
- **Running low row:** one button (Add to order, or red Order now on an empty jar) + ⋮. The fact line reads supplier · `empty` · `refill Wk 44` (`osRefillFact`). The ⋮ menu (`osCardDots`, heading = code + name): formulas get Make a refill now and **Plan the refill · Wk N ▸** (`data-ph-os-plan`, toggles `phOsMenuPlan`, unfolds This week / Next week / Pick a week… / Take off the refill plan in place), then Snooze 1 week, Open in the edit form (To order rows: Take off the order list; On the way: Back to To order first). Pick a week anchors under the row's ⋮ (`phRefDatePopPlace` falls back to `[data-ph-os-menu]`).
- **Snoozed:** a `Snoozed N ▾` chip (`data-ph-osb-snz`, `phOsSnzOpen`) on the "Not on the list" line of the Running low view (as the mock: it scrolls with the list); its menu lists each with its date and Unsnooze. **A Snooze tapped on the board now gives the one toast with Undo** ("X snoozed until 28 Oct", in the shared `data-ph-snooze` handler, only when `phOsbBoard()` exists, so phone and Inventory behave as before).
- **Pop-out details:** a row tap (`data-ph-os-card-toggle`, the `osCardFold` branch) sets `phOsPop`; the render prints `.ph-osb-pop` (Supplier, Size × qty stepper, Bottle $, Low at, Total once the price is confirmed) and the row wears `.popped`. `phOsbPopPlace` puts it beside the row (right, else left), re-runs on scroll / resize, adds `.off` while the row is scrolled out of its list and removes it (and `phOsPop`) when the row is gone. Closes on a second tap, an outside tap or Esc (pop first, then ⋮ / Snoozed). Its entrance `.in` is added only by the tap, never on a repaint.
- **Drag** (`phOsbDropAt`): a row can drop on the To order tab, the other columns, never its own list (`.dropno`).
- **Motion** all transient classes, none on a base class; the reduced-motion block turns off the ping, wash, pop entrance, flies and the switch's transitions, and `phOsbMove` writes and repaints at once.
- Tested in the sandbox (preview, 1280 / 1600 / 375 px, real handlers and drag events, data snapshot restored after): default side incl. re-entry, switch both ways with each side's scroll kept, Add from Running low at a scrolled list (no scroll, fly, lit + ping + count, gold gone after ~2 s), pop placement / follow-scroll / hide / close three ways / stepper inside it, Mark ordered, Back to To order, Arrived, Mark all ordered, All arrived, drag to the tab and refusal on its own list, Plan the refill unfold + Pick a week anchor + "refill Wk 44", Snooze toast + Undo, Snoozed chip + Unsnooze, reduced motion, phone unchanged, no console errors. **NOT exercised:** her real stock, her phone, a real mouse drag, reduced motion on a real device.

## Guide Families: study + edit (her 2026-10-21 "correspond the guide page of formulas and body type with the fang bing ren design, colours, chips, etc. make it study friendly so i can study and edit e.g. add more symptoms for the formula, rank them in importance etc.")
Mock `guide-study-edit-mock-2026-10-21.html`; **her 12 picks are in memory `project_pharmacy_guide_study_edit_2026_10_21`; do not re-ask.** Built `58505db8`, PUSHED LIVE on her "push live" 2026-10-21 (main `d7593be9`, build `20261021-1630000`; the Care Plan-shape templates and Watch / Aim write-door commits on the branch rode along).

The old Body types / Formulas / Formula notes tabs and their renderers are gone; their DATA (`bodyTypes`, `formulaPres`, `formulaNotes`) is untouched and shown in the card's "From your notes" block (matched by name: figures, eyes, build line, signs, quotes, Edit / Delete into the existing `phGdOpenBt/Fp` modals; "Add notes" opens the modal with the name and Chinese name filled in).
- **Engine layer** (before `phFbrSignFam`): `phFbrPristine` (copy of each formula's ren/bing/core/or at load), `phFbrEditsApply` (rebuilds the tables IN PLACE, because many readers hold `.ren` / `.bing`; an `or` line survives only while both halves are on the formula), `phFbrEditsSync` (called first in `renderPharmacy`; re-applies only when `JSON.stringify(PHARMACY.fbrEdits)` changed; the signature is set only AFTER a successful apply so a boot-time TDZ is retried), `phFbrEditsBust` (`phTriOrderCache`, `phTpAutoBust`, `phWatchMemo`, `phTpFxCtxCache`). Store `fbrEdits[id] = { r:{key:w|null}, b:{…}, c:"pearl" }`, stamp `fbrEditsAt[id]`.
- **Guide code** (`phGdf*`, before `phGdBodyHtml`): state is view-only; `phGdfRows` (non-zero keys; `ed` / `add` flags), `phGdfSections` (Key 5 / Strong 4 / Supports 3 / Weak 1–2 / Against, A–Z), `phGdfUnits` (joined `or` pairs), `phGdfDone` (the one write path, rollback if `savePharmacy()` fails), Undo stack per card (`phGdfUndoSt`), `phGdfShow(id)` (jump from the Fang Bing Ren ⋯ "All formulas & criteria", via `phFbrAllRefOpen`; the old read-only sheet code is left in place but unreachable). Hooks: `phGdfClick / Input / Change / Key` are called first in the four delegated handlers. `phSignLabel` now resolves an `a_` key to its learned label.
- **New symptom** = `triLearn.added` `{kind, label:"Subject: text", pts:0, agreed:true, fam:"general"}` + weight under `a_<id>`; undoing the create drops it when no formula still uses it. **Notes-only**: tiles `n:<id>`, rows / pearls in `gdNoteEdits[id]`, own notes can be added, hidden and deleted from the card; memory aid, distinction and conditions of the seeded three stay read-only. **Learned** = `gdLearned[id] = {on, at}`.

- **Disclosed calls:** the figure and eyes drawings of an old body type sit small inside "From your notes" (not in the mock); a weight of 0 can stay in a table (every reader tests `> 0` / truthy, the Guide filters it); a conflict on `pharmacy_core` that takes the other whole side can still lose an unstamped learned sign (only symptoms an edit uses are rescued); old CSS for the retired Body types / Formulas / Formula notes layouts (`.ph-gd-layout`, `.ph-gd-cards`, `.ph-gd-card*`, `.ph-gd-detail-*` except `-lbl`, `.ph-gd-formulakb*`, `.ph-fkb-*`) and the unreachable All-formulas sheet code are left for a later sweep.

NOT tested: a real second device through the cloud, a real patient's Fang Bing Ren card after an edit (only the rank function), the Chrome-on-phone touch feel.

## + Watch / + Aim go through the Watch / Aim write door (the 2026-10-21 docs audit's "Known gap", first flagged in "Care Plan = the findings beside the plan"; commit 74f57e02, LIVE on main via d7593be9, build 20261021-1630000)
Before: `phTpFxAddToggle` appended "<Finding>: <phrase>" to the raw stored `ph.watch` / `ph.aim`. Since "Watch and Aim write themselves from the patient" the stored text of an auto field is only the template's, so one tap made the field read as hers (frozen) with the red flags and the new line only, and the auto chips were gone.
- **Now** (`phTpFxAddToggle`, block "+ Watch / + Aim: adds (or takes off)"): starts from what she sees, `phTpFieldText(name, plan, ph, which)` (= `phTpFieldView(...).text`), adds or removes the segment, and writes through `phTpFieldCommit`. A failed save and Undo put back the exact stored text AND the `watchHers` / `aimHers` mark (present or absent, `snap` / `put`); Undo finds the phase by its id (it used to take whichever phase was live at Undo time) and rolls itself back if its own save fails. Toast, fly (`phTpFxFly`) and repaint (`phTpPlanRepaint`) as before.
- **The button's ✓** (`phTpFxRowHtml`) reads `phTpFieldText` for the live phase, not raw `ph.watch` / `ph.aim`. **"↺ back to auto"** (`phTpFieldBackToAuto`) now also calls `phTpFxRefreshAll()`, so the drawer's ✓ goes back to + with the field.
- **(Superseded the same day by "+ Watch / + Aim: her own lines on top of the auto field", below.)** **Decision, disclosed: a + Watch / + Aim makes the field hers** (she chose it). The auto text she saw at that moment (red flags and every chip) is kept as her text with the finding added, and the cell shows "↺ back to auto". Consequences: (1) while hers the field no longer follows the patient (a "Day 12, follicular" chip stays as written, a new complaint adds no chip); back to auto brings the live chips back and drops her added lines; (2) the Treatment tab shows her text as plain chips split at commas (`phAcuWatchChips`), without the Red flag / Main / Also / Cycle tags, the same as any Watch she types; (3) taking her last pick off returns the field to auto only while the rest still matches the auto text (`phTpFieldCommit`'s rule); if the auto text has moved on (a new cycle day), it stays hers and back to auto is the way out. A field she typed herself stays hers through add, take off and Undo. Keeping the field auto with her picks as a separate list on top would need a new stored field and a change to the read; hers to decide.
- **Left alone on purpose:** `phTpFxColInner` and `phTpFxPhaseTint` (a concurrent session reworked both on 2026-10-21; the tint now follows the template's "Looks at" and no longer reads the Aim / Watch text).
- **Pages checked (★★★):** Care Plan (Watch and Aim cells, the findings drawer's ✓), Treatment tab Watch chips (`phAcuWatchItems`), the print doc (`phTpPlanDocHtml`), the spine row / letter text (`phTpWatchFixedText`); Today's visit shows no Watch (unchanged).
- Tested in the sandbox preview (synthetic "Zz Wa Fix", Natural fertility plan on Follicular week 1, main + secondary complaint, cycle day 12; deleted after, sandbox left empty): auto Watch (red flags + Main + Also + Cycle) → + Watch keeps all four and adds "Came in for: Trying to conceive", ✓ Watch, back-to-auto chip, toast with Undo; take off → auto (template text stored, no `watchHers`); Undo of the take-off → hers exactly; Undo of an add → auto exactly; back to auto → + Watch; a typed Watch stays hers through add / take off / Undo; + Aim on an auto Aim → hers, take off → auto; a failed save (`savePharmacy` stubbed false) puts text and mark back with the fail toast; the fly element and the field's landing flash seen; 375px no sideways scroll; no console errors. **NOT exercised:** a real patient, her phone, reduced motion (`phTpFxFly` unchanged), a cloud sync between a tap and its Undo.

## Templates in the Care Plan shape (her 2026-10-21 "integrate this treatment care plan format with the saved templates too - make suitable integrations accordingly - show mock"; mock `template-care-plan-mocks-2026-10-21.html` "A · Full page"; "Yes, build and push"; commits 1c120e5a + cc7bb289; LIVE 2026-10-21 in the Guide Families push d7593be9, build 20261021-1630000, with her OK for all three builds to go together)
**Her 12 picks are in memory `project_pharmacy_template_care_plan_shape_2026_10_21`; do not re-ask.** Settings → Prescriptions → Treatment Care Plan templates.
- **Two views** (`phTpTemplatesSectionHtml`, state `phTpxS()`: `open`, `lit`, `prev`, `menu`, `rh`, `ther`, `recipe`, `json`, `drag`…). Closed = the list (`.ph-tpx-list`, the rail's category groups across the page via `phTpMgrRailHtml(selId, true)` → `.ph-tpx-lcol`, + New template). Open = the full-page editor `phTpxEditHtml(id)` (root `.ph-tpx[data-tpx]`, a `tpx` size container); the Settings left nav hides and the shell goes full width while it is open (`.ph-settings-shell2:has(.ph-tpx)`). The old `phTpMgrEditHtml` and its band / tiles are left in place as dead code for a later sweep.
- **Band** (`phTpxBandHtml`): ‹ Templates, title ▾ switcher, category pill, Case select, Preview patient select (`data-tpx-prev`), Used by N ▾ (`phTpxUsedList`, Open = `phTpxOpenPlan`), ↩ (the manager's undo, `phTpMgrUndoLast(quiet)`), ⋯ (Save a copy, Reset to built-in, JSON, Delete). Popovers live inside the band; the outside-click close clears state at once and removes the DOM a tick later, so the main click handler still sees the tapped item.
- **Findings column** (`phTpxFxColHtml`): rows cc, ren, tongue, pulse, abd, cycle, msk, ask, bld, hx (`phTpxRowDefs`), drag (+ Alt+↑↓, `phTpxMoveRow`, FLIP slide `phTpxFlip`) and an eye per row; hidden rows sit under HIDDEN. Store: template `fx = { order, hidden }` via `phTpMgrSetField(id, "fx", …)` ("" when equal to the category draft). Read `phTpxFx(tpl)`; draft `phTpxFxDraft(cat)` = her pick 9 (MSK cc, msk, tongue, pulse, abd, ask, ren, hx, bld, cycle hidden; Gynae cc, cycle, tongue, pulse, abd, ask, bld, ren, hx; others cc, ren, tongue, pulse, abd, cycle, msk, ask, bld, hx). A finding tap lights its phases and draws the lines (`phTpxPaintLit`, `phTpxLines`, svg `.ph-tp-fxlk`).
- **Right column:** Diagnosis card (`phTpxDxCardHtml`: likely / possible, the Fang Bing Ren row, Goal) + the Session grid as ONE table (`phTpxTableHtml`; a phase opens in place, `phTpxPhaseHtml`): name + ↑ ↓ ×, Aim (contenteditable with `.ph-tpx-tok` tap-tokens, `phTpxAimHtml` / `phTpxAimRead`, `{main}` `{sec}` `{cycle}`), Points, **Formula** seg Blank | Fang Bing Ren | Set formula (phase `fmode`, Set name = `defaultFormulaName`), Objective + Recipe, Watch red flags + chips, **Looks at** joined tick bar (phase `looks`, `data-tpx-look`), Therapies (`phTpMgrTherapiesHtml(id, i, {lean:true})`), Message. Rhythm popover per phase (`phTpxRhPopHtml`), + Add phase, Standard course (squares pop).
- **Looks at draft** (`phTpxLooksDraft`): Low back pain's three phases seeded from her pick 10 (Acute cc, msk, pulse, ask; Subacute msk, cc, tongue; Maintenance ren, tongue, pulse, hx); every other phase reads its Aim + Red flags through a keyword table (max 4, else cc). Derived on read until she ticks.

## Watch chips: "Ask her" follows the patient (the follow-up left in "Templates in the Care Plan shape"; her standing rule "pronouns follow the patient"; commit 482266b3, LIVE on main 2026-10-21 in build 20261021-164100 (344796b3), her "push live")

The `ask` key is gone from `PH_WATCH_TAG`, so nothing can read a hand-typed "her" again. No other wording changed.

## Diagnosis signs name the condition only (her 2026-10-21 screenshot of Wu Ling San's Key list in the Guide + "audit all the diagnosis and split the ones that arent related e.g. fatty liver is not related to diarrhoea")
Audited every "Diagnosis:" sign in both pools (46). Seven Wu Ling San Bing rows bundled a condition with symptoms that are not part of it; the rest name one condition, or keep words that describe the condition itself. New standing rule in CLAUDE.md "Locked conventions".
- **Relabelled, id kept** (her ticks and Guide edits are stored by id): `fatty_liver_edema` "Diagnosis: fatty liver" (was "..., swelling or diarrhea"), `gout_fluid` "Diagnosis: gout" (was "with thirst, sweat, swelling"), `diabetes_thirst_vomit` "Diagnosis: diabetes" (was "diabetes, thirst, vomits fluid"), `hyperlipidemia_thirst` "Diagnosis: hyperlipidemia" (was "high lipids, heavy, thirsty, sweaty"), `glaucoma_urine` "Diagnosis: glaucoma" (was "with scant urine").
- **Merged into the plain row the pool already had:** `hepatitis_edema` ("chronic hepatitis, swelling or diarrhea") -> `hepatitis`, `cirrhosis_edema` ("cirrhosis, swelling or diarrhea") -> `cirrhosis`; Wu Ling San now weights `hepatitis: 4`, `cirrhosis: 4` (Xiao Chai Hu / Xue Fu Zhu Yu already used those rows). `var PH_FBR_KEY_MOVED` (just above `phFbrEditsApply`) maps old -> new and is read in three places: `phTriToday` moves a saved tap (today's, the carried-forward one, its start date, a link's No) to the plain row, never over an answer it already has (the 2026-10-13 pulse-split pattern); `phFbrEditsApply` applies a Guide edit on the old key to the plain row unless she edited that row too; `phSignLabel` reads an old key as the plain row (old audit lines).
- **Where the split-off symptoms went (Wu Ling San Bing):** swelling (`edema_surf` 5), loose stools (`diarrhea` 5), thirst (`thirst_intense` 5) and vomits clear fluid (`vomit_fluid` 5) were already on the formula, unchanged. **Added, MY call at the old row's weight:** `sweat_profuse` 4 and `body_heavy` 4 (the sweat and heaviness from gout / hyperlipidemia), `urine_reduced` 3 (glaucoma's scant urine). Hers to change in the Guide.
- **Integration:** two new twins (one shared row) with the Ren diagnoses of the same words: `diabetes_thirst_vomit` <-> `dx_diabetes`, `hyperlipidemia_thirst` <-> `hyperlipidemia_tend` (both in the Energy & general area). The Bing hyperlipidemia now reads cholesterol / LDL / triglycerides above range (`PH_TRI_BLOOD`), same as its Ren twin; diabetes still is not read from bloods (the HbA1c reason above `PH_TRI_BLOOD` stands), and Medical history pre-fill stays Ren-only. Fatty liver moved to the "Liver, gall & gut disease" group (`PH_BING_GROUPS`); the merged keys left `PH_BING_GROUPS`, `PH_FBR_DX_AREA` and the search words (`hepatitis` gained "chronic", hyperlipidemia "high lipids triglycerides").
- **Kept, judged related (listed for her):** cardiac insufficiency / chronic nephritis "with swelling" (Fu Zi), asthma "with copious clear sputum", gastritis "vomits fluid", the Xiao Qing Long cold-type / watery-discharge rows, acute nephritis "fluid, cold signs", epilepsy "episodic", and the "X or Y" rows that name one condition two ways. **Not in scope (not "Diagnosis:"), same problem:** "Head: headache with swelling, scant urine", "Head: migraine with swelling or scant urine", "Eyes: light-sensitive, blurred sight, swelling", "Sweat: heavy, with swelling", "Urine: scant, with swelling and thirst" — offered to her as a yes / no.
- **Consequence, disclosed:** a bare diagnosis now scores the formula on its own (Diagnosis: diabetes alone gives Wu Ling San 4; before, that row meant diabetes WITH thirst and fluid vomiting). A patient with all the parts scores the same as before.
- Tested in the sandbox preview (synthetic "Zz Dx Split", deleted after, sandbox left with 0 patients and no Guide edits): labels and weights, no duplicate ids or labels in the Bing pool, every group key exists; old taps moved (d, prevD, since, auto) and a plain-row answer kept; a Guide edit on an old key (weight and removal) and on both keys; both twins render as one shared row; the blood pre-fill ticks the Bing hyperlipidemia; the Fang Bing Ren card renders with no old wording and no raw keys; the Guide's Wu Ling San Key / Strong / Supports lists. **NOT exercised:** a real patient, her phone, a cloud sync carrying an old key from a device that is not updated (the read-time move and `phSignLabel` cover it when it arrives).

## + Watch / + Aim: her own lines on top of the auto field (her 2026-10-21 "yes i prefer that": keep Watch / Aim automatic, with the findings she adds kept on top, instead of a tap making the field hers; commit 437960c0, LIVE on main via 5117232b, build 20261021-164300)
Supersedes the "Decision" bullet of "+ Watch / + Aim go through the Watch / Aim write door" above.
- **Store:** `ph.watchPicks` / `ph.aimPicks` = her picks, an array of "<Finding>: <phrase>" lines on the plan phase, written only through `phTpFieldPick(ph, f, seg, on)` (the second half of the Watch / Aim door, beside `phTpFieldCommit`) and read through `phTpFieldPicks`. A pick never sets `watchHers` / `aimHers`, so an auto field keeps following the patient.
- **Read** (`phTpFieldView`): now also returns `base` (the field without her picks: her typed text, or the auto text) and `picks` (minus any already in the base); `text` = base + picks, which every text reader gets (print doc, spine, profile search, plan to ask, the cell editor's seed). `phTpWatchFixedText` (dated record, letter) = her typed text, else the red flags + her picks.
- **Cell** (`phTpFieldCellHtml`): her picks are whole chips after the auto chips, `.ph-wch.t-pick` (paper, `--ph-herb-deep` text, `--ph-herb-card` inset line, so it follows a case colour), no source tag (the line names its finding), hover "Added from Findings"; shown on a closed phase too. "↺ back to auto" compares her typed text without the picks.
- **Treatment tab** (`phAcuWatchItems`): her picks come last, each one whole (never split at a comma), untagged like her typed lines; a tick is stored by the line.
- **Typing** (`phTpFieldCommit`): the editor's text carries her picks; a pick still in it stays a pick, one she deleted is taken off, and only the rest is the field (hers, or back to auto by the old rule). **Back to auto** (`phTpFieldBackToAuto`) drops her typing and keeps her picks.
- **The tap** (`phTpFxAddToggle`): a pick goes on or off through `phTpFieldPick`; a line an OLDER tap wrote into the field's own text (builds 1580000 to 1630000) comes off through `phTpFieldCommit`, which returns the field to auto when the rest matches it. A failed save and Undo restore the text, the hers mark and the pick list exactly.
- **Not converted, disclosed:** a field an older tap already made hers stays hers until she taps ✓ to take that line off (or back to auto) and adds it again; no stored data was rewritten.
- **Pages checked (★★★):** Care Plan Watch and Aim cells, the findings drawer's ✓, Treatment tab Watch chips, print doc, spine / letter text, a closed phase's cell.
- Tested in the sandbox preview (synthetic "Zz Wa Pick", Natural fertility plan on Follicular week 1; deleted after, sandbox left empty): + Watch keeps the field auto with the pick chip after Main / Also / Cycle, no back-to-auto chip, ✓ Watch; a new secondary complaint (Headache) added its own auto chip while the pick stayed; take off and both Undos exact; + Aim the same; editor text unchanged keeps auto + pick, deleting the pick line takes it off, typing her own keeps the pick beside her text; back to auto keeps the pick; an older-tap line comes off and the field returns to auto; a failed save keeps the list exactly; 375px no sideways scroll; no console errors. Picture: `watch-picks-build-2026-10-21.jpg`. **NOT exercised:** a real patient, her phone, reduced motion, a cloud merge where two devices changed the same phase's picks (the new field rides with the phase, like `watchHers`).

## 2026-10-21 · synth22: Today's visit tidy, skip a check, Quick assess 8 in 2 columns, board subject headings, search adds a complaint
Her seven asks (synth22 batch): Today's visit "more space saving, cohesive"; "option to exclude/avoid assessment categories"; Quick assess "2 columns 8 questions"; "audit chip shape size" on the Today's Fang Bing Ren / Care Plan cards; "space saving with this too" (the Fang Bing Ren page's Quick assess); "i like the ren display category ... do this for fang bing ren page too"; the complaint search "can also act as a secondary complaint addition". Her four picks (all the recommended option): skip = today with an "always for this patient" option; 8 questions in 2 columns; twin headings from the Bing subject; search adds her typed words. Mock `synth22-today-fbr-mock-2026-10-21.html`, her "yes build it".
- **Checks as rows** (`phTodayTilesHtml` / `phTodayTilesRefresh`): tick · name + today's finding (last visit's after it, one line, full text on hover) · Open · Skip ▾, two across (one on a phone, where Open goes and the row itself opens the page). Cycle keeps its Day 1 chip and finder, full width.
- **Skip** (`phTodaySkipped`, `phTodayPartOk`, `phTodaySkipWrite`): Skip ▾ → Not today (`rec.todaySkip = { day, ids }`) or Always for him / her (`rec.todaySkipAlways`). Skipped checks fold into "Skipped: Tongue ↺ · Pulse · always ↺"; ↺ brings one back. A skipped check counts as done everywhere the visit is counted (n of m, the rail ticks, the Up-next walk, the Dispense note, the rail's "n of m"); `phTodayDonePart` stays "examined today" so the findings mirror never copies a skipped exam.
- **Compass:** every 0–10 (complaints) and 1–10 (Energy) row is one joined bar (CSS only, `.ph-cx-row .pills`, `.ph-en-line .chips`); the "n of m done" line moved into the compass header (`phCxCardHtml` prints `phTodayProgHtml`; on its own only when the full compass is not drawn).
- **Close-ups · Surgeries · Pain intake:** one joined bar (`.ph-tng2-seg.mk-xseg`), each part opens its own section (`phMskXOpen` is now plan id → "z" / "s" / "i", `data-msk="xseg"`, `phMskXShow`); a pain name still opens Close-ups. Outside the Musculoskeletal page the old line had no style at all, which is why it looked like a native dropdown. Close-ups are smaller on Today's visit (86px drawings).
- **Buttons:** Today's visit actions are the 6px / 28px button (`.ph-today-btn`); Log today's session is the one solid; Open full Fang Bing Ren / Open full Care Plan / Dispense Open are outlined. "Fang Bing Ren's current lead" is its own quiet line under Today's formula (`.ph-tpg-fbrlead` was only a block inside the Session grid strip).
- **Quick assess (both pages):** `PH_QA_AT_ONCE` 4 → 8, rows in 2 columns (`.ph-qa-rows`), the family bars in a row under them. Picking 8 together uses `phNqOutcomes` + `phNqHw` (each outcome split once per candidate) instead of `phNqJointH`; same number to 6 places on the test patient; a full 8-row fill = ~210k small steps, a refill about a quarter of that. Two signs worded the same (a Bing and a Ren sign that are not twins) are never on screen together (it showed "Pulse: faint?" twice).
- **Board subject headings** (`phFbrBoardHtml`: `bySubj`, `runs`, `subHd`, `twinCells`, `sideCells`): one quiet heading per run of same-subject rows with its count, and the "Subject:" word drops from the rows. A twin pair's heading is its Bing subject; the Ren cell keeps its own subject word only when it differs ("Chest & breasts: distention before periods"). Only while rows are subject-sorted (no family colour), the Ren tab's rule.
- **Search adds a complaint** (`phCsAddHtml`, `phCsIsComplaint`): "+ Add as complaint" beside the box whenever she has typed (also when no sign matches) → `phCcSecAdd` (Came in for's own add, `phCcWrite`), then the ribbon, the plan cache and the Fang Bing Ren card repaint; "✓ Added" while the words already are a complaint (read, never stored). No sign is ticked.
- **Tested** (headless Chrome, the synthetic "Zz Sweep Test" patient, in memory only): 6 rows + 1 Skipped line; Skip → menu → Not today moved Tongue to Skipped and the count went 0 → 1 of 5; Always stored `todaySkipAlways: ["pulse"]`; ↺ cleared `todaySkip`; 10 buttons, 1 solid; 8 questions in 2 × 534px columns at 1440; joined bars (999px bar, square cells); Close-ups bar opens Surgeries, then Pain intake, then closes; search "energy low" → 9 matching signs, Add → 3 secondaries, "✓ Added"; "zzqx" → "No matching sign yet." with the chip still there; no page errors. Pictures at 1440 and 375 (Today's visit, Fang Bing Ren). **Not exercised:** a real MSK patient's close-up drawings at the new size, timing on her Key2 (the headless clock is frozen; the step count is above), a real tap on the board's headings (structure only).

## 2026-10-21 · synth22: the "Looking at" line, Quick assess follows it, body map close-ups with organ plates
Her three asks (synth22 batch): Quick assess should change its questions with the formula / herb tile she taps; the complaint filter should come from her chief and secondary complaints; the body map should zoom to a place when she points at it, with anatomy pictures. Her 12 popup picks (memory `project_pharmacy_synth22_focus_bodymap_2026_10_21`), mock `synth22-focus-bodymap-mock-2026-10-21.html`, her "Yes, build it". Sandbox first; pushed live on her "push live" in build 20261021-165200.
- **One "Looking at" line** (`phFbrLookHtml`, Fang Bing Ren page and Today's visit): Everything · her complaints (chief first, then secondaries, read through `phCcRibItems` / `phCcOf`) · the tile she tapped (✕ clears it). It replaced the nine complaint-kind pills, the "Filtering" pill, the "For this complaint" bar and its sheet (`phFbrForBarHtml`, `phFbrForOf`, `phFbrSheetHtml`, `.ph-fbr-for-line`, `.ph-fbr-filtpill`, `details.ph-fbr-rest` are gone). Starts on Everything; view state only (`phFbrFocus`, `phFbrFocusOf`), never saved. Today's visit shows complaints only (`ctx.today`).
- **A complaint lifts, nothing hides** (`phFbrCcSigns`, `phFbrCcList`, `phFbrCcLift`): its own signs (the sign she added it from, else the best word match, rarer words weigh more, plus same-subject matches and twins) and the key signs (top weight, 5 at most) of every formula that weighs one of them rise to the top of each area; the rest fade. The matching rule is MY draft, hers to correct.
- **Quick assess follows the line** (`phQaFocus`, `phQaSub`, `phQaOwnRows`, `phQaPickOwn`, `phQaCcRow`): Everything = every formula (as before); a complaint = the formulas that count it, its own question first; a herb tile = the herb and its formulas; a formula tile (or a complaint only one formula counts) = that formula's own signs, strongest first. Same family engine (`phNqState`) over fewer families. Narrow columns wrap the answers under the question (`.ph-qa-rows .ph-qr` flex-wrap).
- **Body map close-ups** (her "A · Zoom + plates"): pointing at a place in the big chart's list (~0.1 s pause, `phFbbZT`) zooms the figure column to that place (`phFbbZoomShow`, `phFbbZoomSvg`, `phFbbZoomCrop`, `phFbbZoomTween`; the shared `PH_BODY_GEOM` figure with its anatomy, only this place's glow) and its plates come up under it (`phFbbpCardHtml`). Moving off the list returns to the whole body (450 ms); a header click (or Enter / Space) pins the place (`data-fbb-pin`, "Pinned" tag, `phFbbPinMarks`); "← Whole body" unpins. The close-up sits over the whole body's own room (`.ph-fbb-close`, absolute), so nothing below moves. A tick inside repaints in place with no motion (`phFbbPaint` → `phFbbZoomShow(…, true)`). Energy, fever, comes-and-goes and other whole-person groups keep the whole body.
- **Plates for every place** (`PH_FBBP`, `PH_FBBP_PLACE`, `phFbbpFor`, `phFbbpLit`, `phFbbpSvg`): 26 drawn plates over 22 places plus pelvis (liver & gallbladder, pleura & diaphragm, airways, heart, stomach, pancreas & spleen, bowel, kidneys to bladder, kidney cut open, womb / prostate (by sex; unknown sex gets both), hip, lower back, brain, inner ear, face, mouth & throat, thyroid, neck bones, breast, flank, wrist pulse, joint, lower-leg veins, sweat gland, skin & hair) plus the app's own drawings as plates: her latest Tongue chart, her Abdomen chart, the Musculoskeletal muscle diagrams, the hand / foot / knee close-ups (`PH_FBBP_APP`, `phFbbpAppSvg`). ## 2026-10-21 · Concept pictures: one moving picture per pattern
Her ask: "include conceptual images/animations/diagrams e.g. blood stasis - clotted blood flow - show mock". Mock `concept-pictures-mock-2026-10-21.html` (9 pictures) plus a second inline mock of the 4 later drawings; her 8 popup picks (memory `project_pharmacy_concept_pictures_2026_10_21`) and "Yes, build it". Sandbox first.
- **Her picks:** in all four places (the body map close-up, the Care Plan Diagnosis, the Patterns page cards, the Guide study card); the body map shows the pattern HER signs at that place point to; style "flow + the pot"; a picture opens on the pattern, Balanced is a tap away; it moves gently, always (still under reduced motion); short labels, on the pattern side only; her own matching signs under it; **all 13 patterns, each its own picture** (not the reuse I offered).
- **The pictures** (`PH_PP`, one IIFE before `phPatCompute`, `PH_PP.SC[pattern id]`): two families so they read alike. FLOW: Blood stasis (cells back up and darken behind a clot, a stabbing spark; starts already backed up, `S.warm`), Blood deficiency (few pale cells, the tissue pales), Qi stagnation (qi knots and swells, stop-start), Qi deficiency (a weak push, qi sags), Damp / phlegm (slow heavy fluid, sludge, sticky globs, fog), Cold-damp (the pipe contracts, frost, damp pools, cramping). THE POT over the fire: Yang deficiency (fire low, water cold), Yin deficiency (water low, empty heat rises), Heat (fire high, boils over). Their own drawings: Kidney yang (the root fire between the kidneys, cold kidneys, clear urine often), Middle deficient cold (the stomach over a low middle fire, food sits, dull ache), Cold in the channels (cold tightens the vessel, blood can't reach the cold fingers), Plum-pit qi (qi circles a lump in the throat). Labels use the organ plates' own label style.
- **One runner:** a picture is `<svg data-pp="id" data-pp-key="key">` anywhere in `#pharmacyPage`; a MutationObserver mounts it (`phPpScan`), one requestAnimationFrame clock (`phPpTick`, about 30 frames a second) moves the ones on screen (IntersectionObserver), nothing runs under reduced motion (a still picture), and a hidden tab lands a flip with a timeout.
