- **Heat (`SC.heat`) = pot + body:** `pot(svg, w, ox, oy, sc, rich)` now draws into a group; the rich pot (Heat only) has a rattling lid, foam spilling down both sides, a red glow, five flames, shimmer, a dropping water level. `body(svg, ox, oy, sc, m)` is the person: Heat = flush, sweat beads, cracked lips, heat waves.
- **The person on three more:** Yang deficiency (pale, shivers, frost, pot fire low), Yin deficiency (hot cheeks, night sweats, cracked lips, pot water low), Qi deficiency (heavy lids, head and shoulders slump, the channel drawn small beside; `chan(S, svg, m, noLab)` can now hide its own labels). **No mock was shown for these three** (she picked "Yes, add it to three more"); hers to correct.
- **Real anatomy:** stomach (oesophagus, fundus, body with folds, pylorus, the duodenum's loop; food pools low) for Middle cold; throat from the front (hyoid, thyroid cartilage, cricoid, windpipe rings, gullet behind) for Plum-pit; kidneys with pyramids, pelvis, adrenals, discs between the vertebrae and a bladder for Kidney yang; Blood stasis / Blood deficiency / Cold in the channels get the vessel wall's three coats, flat lining cells, platelets, a white cell and dimpled red cells (`rbc`, `coats`, `lining`, `platelets`, `whiteCell`).
- **Contract unchanged:** every scene is still `SC.id = (S, svg) => { ...; S.step = (dt, t) => {...} }` in `PH_PP`; `phPpHtml` / `phPpSvg` / the runner / the Balanced | pattern bar, all placements (Care Plan Diagnosis thumbnails, body map, Patterns cards, Guide) and `S.k` Balanced-to-pattern are untouched, so every place that shows a pattern picture shows the new one. Labels are on the pattern side only.
- **Tested** (headless Edge, `_pp-all.html` / `_pp-sheet.html`, both removed): all 13 on both sides, 1x and 2x; every scene stepped at k 0 / .3 / .5 / .8 / 1 with and without reduced motion: no exceptions, no NaN / undefined in any SVG; the Care Plan thumbnail row at 1280px and 390px. Two label fixes found on the way (the throat's right-hand label cut off; the Qi deficiency label sat on the head). **Not tested:** a real patient's Care Plan (no sign-in in the sandbox); the big sheet over a real plan (same SVG at a larger size, seen on the board only).

## Abdomen exam redesign: side view, Seen row, °C, neutral colour (her 2026-10-22 "improve the abdominal analysis page ... realistic animation / tension covers whole abdomen / temp in celsius / water mainly umbilicus / gas at umbilicus, epi, lower and flank / distension is seen, not felt" + "include a side view" + "not blue, more neutral"; mock `abdomen-exam-mock-2026-10-22.html` + `abdomen-colours-mock-2026-10-22.html`; 15 picks)
Her picks: Q1 muscle lines under the zones; Q2 standing side view beside the front; Q3 the side view shows ALL (belly line from Seen, wall weight from Tension, sounds + pulsation inside, °C strip); Q4 tension whole abdomen only; Q5 °C at **epi + lower only** (her "i dont take temp at umbilicus"); Q6 keep the cold / warm chips; Q7 water lands on umb; Q8 gas at five spots (umb, epi, lower, R flank, L flank); Q9 Seen row = full / distended, bulging, sunken + a NEW visible pulsation; Q10 Seen sits by ROW (whole, epi, umb, lower); Q11 row order Seen, Temp, Sounds, Tension, Feel; Q12 Fang Bing Ren in labelled Today (Bing) / Usual (Ren) groups; Q14 soft tension dot pale grey; Q15 cold wash / water ink / °C cool end grey; Q13 her words "colors can correspond with patient details and symptoms" = **the drawing is plain white, every colour is a marked finding** (confirmed).
- **Data:** `phAbd2Read` adds `tempC{}` (numbers, junk dropped); `phAbd2Empty` counts `tempC` (without it a temp-only chart is nulled on save); `phAbd2Summary` writes "epi 33.6 °C, lower 32.4 °C, lower 1.2 °C cooler" (`phAbdTempText`); new `PH_ABD_SEEN`, `PH_ABD_SPOTS`, `PH_ABD_ROWSPOTS`, `PH_ABD_TEMPC`, `phAbdRowOf` (zone -> side-view row). Keys are unchanged (`distended`, `bulging`, `sunken` keep their saved marks and the 11 Fang Bing Ren `abd:distended` specs); `vispulse` is new and feeds nothing yet. `PH_FT_ABD_ROWS` and `phFtAbdVals` changed together (Seen added, order Seen / Temp / Sounds / Tension / Feel).
- **Handlers (`data-visit-abd2`):** water on = `["umb"]` and no brush; gas and every Seen sign start `[]` with a spot bar; gas is refused outside its five spots (toast); `tensionAt` can no longer be added (`tensionAtClear` stays so old zone tension can be removed); `tempc:<spot>:+1|-1` steps 0.1; a typed value goes through the new `[data-abd-temp]` change listener (commits on change, clamps 28-40, empty clears).
- **Drawing:** `phAbdChartSvg` is white-based with faint muscle lines (not on `.ro` minis); signs are CSS-animated and scoped `.ph-abd2-svg:not(.ro)`, all off under reduced motion; the distension dome is a dashed ring on the row. New `phAbdSideSvg` (viewBox 100 x 150, rows = the front's even thirds, SMIL breathing, off under reduced motion).
- **Fang Bing Ren strip:** `phTriInlineHtml` labels the two kinds Today / Usual when a strip has both (all strips, not only Abdomen); never merged by label (they save to different places).
- **Tested** (harness, seeded patient): every control tapped, typed 99 clamps to 40, clear empties, Findings trend shows the Seen row and °C, 360px phone frame (charts 112 + 140 wide, rows stacked). **Not exercised:** a real patient, real taps on her phone, the animations by eye.

## Today's visit Compass: Fang Bing Ren signs as monitored rows (2026-10-22, BUILT on session-a, NOT pushed)
Her ask: "help improve todays visit so it integrates with chief complaints, complaints, signs and symptoms from ren fang bing etc are accessory symptoms to monitor". Mock `today-signs-mock-2026-10-22.html` (v2 is the contract), her 18 picks (memory `project_pharmacy_today_signs_compass_2026_10_22`), then "Yes, build it".
- **One table.** Complaint (its Goal under the name, tap to edit, ↺ auto kept) | Since | Today | Change | Note. Today = a slider with a number badge ("last N" until rated; a % box for any number on % better). Notes are kept for every visit: today's box, last visit's note faint under it, "+N" opens all (`phCxNoteCellHtml`, `phCxNotesBefore`). Gold "now asking" row + glance line ("Asked n of m · better · worse · gone"); keys 0-9 (1 then 0 = 10, % = 100), G B S W on a sign, ↓ skips (`phCxKeys` listener, `phCxAskSkip`). A tap on a complaint's name lights its chart line and points Quick assess at it (`phCxLitKey`/`phCxFocusFbr`, stored in `phFbrFocus`, so the Fang Bing Ren page's own bar agrees). The legend chips and Today's "Looking at" bar are gone.
- **Signs.** `phCxSignList(name, rec)` = Fang Bing Ren signs she ANSWERED: her own taps (Fang Bing Ren, Ask her, Quick assess), last visit's carried Bing ticks, intake form, usual answers, Medical history, cycle PMS log, signs rated before or pinned with "+ add". Never one the app only works out (Patterns clues, implied / linked / compound, charts, pain map, bloods, build / age / sex). A Bing / Ren twin pair is one row keyed by its Bing key. A sign sits under the complaint of the same subject, else under Other signs (my rule, hers to correct). Cap 2 per complaint / 6 other, "+ N more"; rated or pinned always show; Gone at two visits in a row leaves; Not watching (▾) puts it in a "Not watching" line with ↺. "Rest the same" fills unrated sign rows with Same. First visit: "rated from next visit".
- **Storage.** `rec.ccTrack.days[day].sx[signKey] = {v:"g|b|s|w", n, t}`, `ccTrack.sxOff[signKey] = day`, `ccTrack.sxPin[signKey] = {cc, at}`. Only `phCxWrite` writes them (its tidy now keeps `sx`; before it deleted unknown buckets).
- **Gone → Fang Bing Ren.** `phCxGoneToday` (a Gone rated after her last Yes tap on that sign, read from `rec.tri.audit`) turns today's Bing tick off in `phTriEffective` / `phTriAutoWhy` / `phNqState` (Quick assess counts it answered). A Yes tap after the Gone puts it back. Ren is never touched. `phTriRefresh` repaints the Compass once (guard `phCxTriBusy`); a rating repaints Fang Bing Ren through `phCxTriSync`.
- **Energy.** A complaint about energy or fatigue (`phCxIsEnergy`: words fatigue / energy / tired / exhaust, or the "Weight & energy" family label) is scored by the am · pm · ev bars under its row (`phEnergyLineHtml(name, {inRow:true})`), 10 = best, no chart line; the standalone Energy line stays only when there is no such complaint. `fatigue_now` / `fatigue_tend` get no sign row then. `phCxEnergyMain` / `phCxEnergyChg` / `phCxMainChange` keep "n of m done", the Treatment Response and the Re-check mirror working for an energy MAIN complaint.
- **Order.** Drag ⋮⋮ (desktop) or ▾ Move up / down writes `rec.complaint.secondary` (`phCxReorder`), so Came in for, the Care Plan Goal and `phase.symptoms` follow; MAIN stays first.
- **Treatment tab.** The Subjective table is replaced by one read-only "Today's ratings" line (`phTsRatingsLineHtml`; the her-words box stays); Watch = the template's Red flags only (`phAcuWatchItems`); "Apply the usual" and its handler are gone; the Log saves today's sign ratings as `session.subj` (Gone = `o:"better", gone:true`); the Visit record's Ask lists them. Old "s:" Subjective drafts are dropped on restore. Dead code left in place on purpose: `phTsSubjHtml`, `phTsSubjRows`, `phTsCycleRowsHtml`, `phTsSinceSet` and the `phTsClick` branches that fed them.
- **Goal and letter.** `phCcSecAll` returns her typed complaints only (the auto Fang Bing Ren "complaints" are gone from the Goal, `phase.symptoms` and the card). `phCcSeedSymptoms` seeds complaints + the Compass's visible sign rows (`ccKey "x:<signKey>"`), in her order; an untouched seeded sign row (or old `fbr:` row) leaves when the Compass no longer shows it, a row she wrote on stays. `phTpSymSummary` prints complaints, then " — and with them <signs>"; the letter's "Watching:" line carries it. **That wording is DRAFT, to show her before it ships.**
- **Pages checked for the changed facts:** Today's visit (Compass, Quick assess, "n of m done"), Fang Bing Ren (ticks, Looking at), Treatment tab (Today's ratings, Watch, Log), Care Plan (Goal, Spine "Symptoms:", letter line), Came in for order. Not opened: Patterns, Dispense, Messages, print.
- **Tested** (sandbox harness, seeded patient, real functions and real clicks through the click router): render at 1000px, 549px and 360px; first visit and return visit; Better / Gone / same-cell-again; Gone vs a later tap (both orders); slider, % by tens and the % box; keys 0-9, G B S W, ↓, `1` then `0`; reorder by ▾ and by `phCxReorder`; "+ add" search with arrows + Enter; Make it a complaint; Not watching and back; note per day; seed of `phase.symptoms`, the summary and the Goal; the Treatment ratings line. **Not exercised:** a real patient, a real drag with the mouse, real touch on her phone, the Treatment tab and the Log screens on screen, the letter PDF.
- **Disclosed calls (mine, hers to correct):** a sign sits under the complaint with the same subject; an exam / lab / build / age / sex sign never gets a row; a tapped sign still gets one; the "Weight & energy" label counts as an energy complaint; the Treatment "Cycle - reported, not examined" rows left with the Subjective table (the Cycle page is their home); the letter's sign names read "Subject: wording".

## 2026-10-22 · Formula pictures (Ban Xia Hou Po Tang, Huang Lian Jie Du Tang)
- **Her ask:** "design diagrams and animations of how certain formulas work e.g. ban xia hou po tang treats qi stagnation in throat, huang lian jie du tang clears heat everywhere ... showing it in the body, how it affects the body e.g. tongue". Her 11 picks and the mock are in memory `project_pharmacy_formula_pictures_2026_10_22` (mock `formula-pictures-mock-2026-10-22.html`).
- **What:** one moving picture per FORMULA, `PH_FP` (right after the `PH_PP` IIFE's helpers, before the runner): the body head to hips with its three burners, the trouble where it lives, the herbs at work, a tongue + pulse inset with two words under it. Three stops on one bar: Trouble (k 0) → Formula at work (k 1) → Settled (k 2); opens on Trouble (`phFpStop`, view state only). Herb chips: all light on the Formula stop, a tapped one keeps only its own work (`S.herb`), cleared on leaving the Formula stop.
- **Runner:** shares the pattern pictures' one clock. `svg[data-fp]` is mounted by `phPpMount` (`fp` branch, opens at `phFpStop`), `phPpTick` calls `phFpLight` and wraps each picture's `step` in try/catch so one failing picture cannot stop the clock; clicks (`data-fp-stop`, `data-fp-herb`, `data-fp-big`) are handled beside the `data-pp-*` ones.
- **Where:** the Guide card (`phGdfCardHtml`: the formula picture REPLACES the pattern picture when `PH_FP.SC[id]`, full width, bars under it), Dispense (`presDispenseFpHtml`, the END of the right column so Next and the tiles stay on screen), the Care Plan formula cell (`phFpTpCellHtml`, a small thumbnail that opens `phFpSheetOpen`). `phFpIdFor(name)` matches the prescription / phase formula name, and also a modified name that starts with it ("... + Gui Zhi").
- **Adding a formula:** one `PH_FP.SC[id]` scene (body + tongue inset helpers are shared), its herbs in `PH_FP.HERBS[id]`, its pattern in `PH_FP.PAT[id]` (colour). Until it has one, the Guide card keeps its pattern picture.
- **Review:** 5 lenses × adversarial verify (63 agents) found the bars wrapping into broken pills, the Dispense sheet pushing Next off the screen, herb chips rendering solid, chips unlit on mount under reduced motion, a held herb surviving a stop change; all fixed. Real-page renders (Guide 1280 and 360, Dispense, Care Plan) checked in headless Edge from a copy of the app.
- **Not tested:** her real patient data and her phone. Known: labels are about 6.5px on the narrowest phone (9px rule in the 640px block); Dispense and Care Plan show no picture for a formula whose name does not start with the base name.

### Abdomen: seen pulsation counts as felt (her 2026-10-22 pick, committed ed34577e)
`phFbrChartWords` adds `abd:pulsation` (and `@zone`) whenever `marks.vispulse` is set, so seeing the abdomen pulse ticks the same two Fang Bing Ren chips as feeling it (`pulsate` Bing, `pulsate_upsurge_tend` Ren). Tested: vispulse at epi gives `abd:vispulse`, `abd:pulsation`, `abd:pulsation@epi`. Live-build click-test of the whole Abdomen page also re-run and passed (build 20261022-051100).

## 2026-10-22 · Concept pictures round 2, build 2 (batch 1): the nine women's health plan conditions
Same ask as build 1 ("design more animations for health conditions"); her picks in memory `project_pharmacy_concept_pictures_round2_2026_10_22` (mock = artifact `women-mock.html`, **mock = build**; every answer was "as mocked"). Batch 2 onward (pain / MSK, face / skin, stress / sleep / weight / immune / men's: 20 more) is not built; each batch is mocked first.
- **Pictures:** `PH_PP.SC["cd_" + template id]`, drawn in their own scope at the end of the `PH_PP` IIFE: `cd_pcos`, `cd_endo`, `cd_dysmen`, `cd_amen`, `cd_perimenopause`, `cd_natural`, `cd_cycle_ivf`, `cd_pregnancy`, `cd_breech_correction`. Same contract as the pattern pictures (`S.k` 0 = Balanced .. 1 = the condition, labels on the condition side only, `RM()` = still frame). Shared drawing helpers inside that scope: `pelvis` (womb, tubes, ovaries; the lining is the cavity's own stroke width), `route` / `nearest` (walk a path by distance), `cluster` (1-2-4-8 cell embryo), `sperm`, `baby`, `womb`. Long loops run on the clock `t` (Natural fertility 14 s, IVF 16 s, others 5-8 s).
- **Behaviour:** PCOS = a ring of small follicles, thick capsule, none ripens; Endometriosis = chocolate cyst, tissue outside the womb that bleeds with each period, adhesions, pain; Dysmenorrhea = the womb squeezes, vessels tighten, dark clots; Amenorrhea = the signal fades before the ovary, a thin lining, no period; Perimenopause = fewer eggs, a swinging hormone line, a flushed sweating person; Natural fertility = egg released, sperm swim up, the embryo travels and settles (condition: weak egg, few slow sperm, thin lining); IVF = many follicles, needle collection, a dish, catheter transfer (Balanced: one follicle); Pregnancy = Balanced the womb at rest, condition a baby with heartbeat, placenta and cord; Breech = head up (Balanced head down; the baby turns when flipped).
- **Wiring:** `PH_PP_COND` holds a pseudo pattern def (`name`, `fam: "pink"`) per picture so `phPpDef` / `phPpCol` / `phPpBarHtml` / `phPpSheetOpen` (the one renderer, bar and sheet) treat it like a pattern; `PH_PP_SHORT` has the bar's short names. `phPpDxRowHtml(name, pats, plan)` now puts the plan's own condition picture FIRST in the Care Plan Diagnosis thumbnail row (the plan's template via `phTpxTplOfPlan(plan)`, so a plan on a template she renamed or copied under another name shows none). Her pick: the main plan's picture only (no pictures for "Also treating").
- **Same limits as the pattern pictures:** the thumbnail row sits in the Diagnosis card's auto branch only, so a Diagnosis she typed herself, or one that came from a booking, shows no thumbnails (patterns included); the plan modal has none.
- **Tested** (headless Edge harness, since removed): all nine on both sides at 1x and 1.5x; every scene stepped at k 0 / .3 / .5 / .8 / 1, with and without reduced motion: no exceptions, no NaN / undefined in any SVG; `phTpFocusGoalHtml` for a synthetic Endometriosis plan renders one condition thumbnail, `phPpSheetOpen("cd_endo")` opens with "Balanced | Endometriosis" and mounts; the thumb row for all nine template names at 1280px and 390px. **Not tested:** a real patient's Care Plan (no sign-in in the sandbox); the loops' later frames by eye (only the stills and a time strip of Natural fertility and IVF).
- **Wording** on the pictures (e.g. "Hard to turn late on", "Few, slow sperm") is DRAFT until she has read it.
- **How it went live:** the code was swept into another session's whole-file commit (081e7f55) and published with build 20261022-053200 before this note was written.

Same contract (`S.k` 0 = Balanced .. 1 = the condition, labels on the condition side only, `RM()` = still frame). The block has its own small helpers (`lab3` = a label whose leader joins the text on the side that faces the dot, `sm`, `G` gaussian, `ecg`, `vir`, `wbc`, `along`, plus the `star` / `dots` copies and the skin / nerve colours).
- **Behaviour:** Anxiety = a figure with the brain's alarm glowing and ringing, nerve signals down to a racing heart (a monitor scrolls the trace and shows the bpm, 62 Balanced to 118) and quick shallow lungs; Insomnia = a head on a pillow with a thought spiral and bubbles, a clock, and the night as a graph (Balanced falls into deep sleep and rises once; the condition falls late, is woken again and again, never reaches deep sleep); Burnout = adrenals on the kidneys with hormone drops, a five-segment battery that drains by day and refills by night (only partly, in the condition) beside a sun / moon; General stress = three taps into a bucket that overflows, a slow drain with a valve wheel, a pressure gauge whose needle climbs into the red and trembles; Weight loss = a side view of the belly (oesophagus, stomach, gut, a digestive flame that burns high or low, food that turns to sparks or to damp droplets that pool) beside a bathroom-scale dial; Immune = a figure inside a shield that wears thin, virus particles drifting to the airway, white cells rushing in, heat shimmer and a fever thermometer; Men's fertility = a microscope circle (many fast sperm Balanced; fewer, slower, odd-shaped and dead ones in the condition) beside a testis with its tubules, epididymis, blood vessel and heat haze.
- **Wiring:** `PH_PP_COND` gains the seven (`fam: "purple"` for the four stress plans, `"green"` weight, `"amber"` immune, `"blue"` men's; names equal the templates' own, the Immune one with its `—`); `PH_PP_SHORT` has the bar's short names (Anxiety, Insomnia, Burnout, Stress, Weight, Immune, Men's). `phPpDxRowHtml` needed no change.
- **Same limits as batches 1 to 3:** the thumbnail row sits in the Diagnosis card's auto branch only; the plan modal has none.
- **Tested** (headless Edge harness inside the real app, since removed): all seven mount (7 of 7) and step at k 1 / .75 / .5 / .25 / 0 / .6 with no exception and no NaN / undefined in any SVG; both sides rendered (screenshot checked: pictures, bar, family dots); each template name resolves to its def and colour; `phPpDxRowHtml` for a plan on each of the seven returns that template's one picture; `phPpSheetOpen("cd_metab_weightloss")` opens and mounts. **Not exercised:** a real signed-in plan on the Care Plan (no account in the sandbox, never create one), the phone width, dark mode.
- **Wording** on the pictures ("Alarm stuck on", "Mind will not switch off", "Adrenals run dry", "Drain too slow", "Fire too low", "Shield worn thin", "Fewer sperm", ...) is DRAFT until she has read it.

Her synth22 (screenshots of the Compass and the Pain card): "a well integrated way to establish baseline of chief complaint and symptoms of patients, and to assess the deviation from the baseline overtime. some symptoms are grouped e.g. digestive complaints, bowels, energy, bloating, gas etc", "i want the design to be more interactive", "integrate the pain into the complaints table instead of keeping it separate". Mock `baseline-compass-mock-2026-10-22.html` (artifact HHKMtDyAhLFEp1Gfg7RqVT), **mock = build**; her 8 picks, every one "as mocked" (memory `project_pharmacy_baseline_compass_2026_10_22`): a baseline = the FIRST score (derived, with its date; "Move baseline to today" is the one stored override); b signs = running total from baseline (Better +1, Worse −1, Same 0, Gone counts better); c groups = the nine Fang Bing Ren body areas, headings always shown, ↑ → ↓ counts + average bar + fold + "Same as last time"; d Energy is its own group, normalised so up = better; e pain rows in the table, the separate Pain card goes; f the pain map is the source (one row per pain, ★ = Main, no duplicate typed row); g interactive: live bars and chart while dragging, tap a name = history drawer, chart switch From baseline | Score + group chips + hover tips, fold + "Same as last time", header switch From baseline | From last visit; h the chart opens on From baseline, one line per group (zero = baseline, up = better).

**Code (index.html, Compass block `BASELINE COMPASS` after `phCxPlanOf`):** `phCxGroups` (lazy, the nine areas + Energy / Other; `PH_CX_GRP_STYLE`, `PH_CX_FAM_AREA`), `phCxModel(name, plan, {ov})` (the one model: rows, groups, baselines, deviations, glance, ask row; `ov` = a value mid-drag), pain helpers (`phCxPainState / phCxPainItems / phCxPainScoreWrite / phCxPainGoal / phCxPainGoalWrite / phCxPainGoalHers`), chart (`phCxChartSvg` From baseline | Score, `phCxRowChartSvg` in the drawer), row + group + drawer + pain foot HTML, `phCxRebase`, `phCxRestSame`, live paint (`phCxLiveQueue / phCxLiveRun`, 40 ms, slow reads kept in `phCxCache`). Wiring: `phCxHist` / `phCxTodayMain` read a pain-main's score from the MSK roll-up (`nprs.now`), the Re-check mirror follows `phPmScoreSet`, `phPmRefresh` / `phPmTblRefresh` repaint the Compass (the Compass root carries `data-pm-box` + `data-pm-name`, so the pain map's own handlers work inside it), `phPmWrite` whitelists `goal`, `phTpAutoGoal` adds each pain's Goal, `phTsRatingsLineHtml` shows pains in 0-10, Today's visit leaves out `phPfPainBlockHtml` when the Compass holds the pains. Stored: `rec.ccTrack.base[rowKey] = { day }` (kept by `phCxWrite`'s tidy) and `painMap[i].goal`. Nothing else is copied; view state is per patient and not stored. CSS: after the "quiet progress line" comment; a mid tier `@container (min-width: 641px) and (max-width: 900px)` drops the Trend column; the phone tier is now `max-width: 640px` (was 620; the three JS "narrow" tests moved with it).

**Pages checked (★★★):** Today's visit (the Compass itself), Musculoskeletal (same pain scores: NOW / "last 4" read the same store), Treatment ("Today's ratings" line, pains included), Care Plan Goal (`phTpAutoGoal` names each pain), `phCcSeedSymptoms` (plan symptom rows unchanged), the Re-check mirror (`rec.recheck`), Energy (group + polarity), the pain map's add / side / star / remove / pick areas from inside the Compass.

**Tested** (real app in the Browser pane, seeded patient, real handlers, no sign-in): return visit, first visit, % scale, pain-as-main; slider live paint then change write (rows, group counts, chart, glance), sign pills, group fold, "Same as last time" on a complaint group and the pain group, drawer (complaint, sign, pain), Move baseline to today and back, chart From baseline | Score + group filter, From last visit switch, number keys (folded group, pain row, 1 then 0 = 10, ↓ skip, G B S W), + add, ▾ Move up, Not watching and back, pain add / side / star / remove / pick areas, pain Goal and note, Energy deviation; 1280, 1024 (mid tier) and 360 px, no horizontal scroll, no console errors. **Not tested:** her real data and phone, real touch, a real mouse drag on the ⋮⋮ handle, reduced-motion by eye, the letter PDF.
**Found while testing and fixed:** the Goal box on a pain row did not save (`phCxTypeSave` only knew typed complaints); the slider column collapsed at 1024 px (new mid tier); group names overlapped the counts on a phone (short names, ellipsis); the pain foot's joined bar overlapped on a phone (it wraps).

**Judgment calls (disclosed):** a complaint nothing places goes to an "Other" group; weight sits under Skin, hair & build; a typed complaint that the pain map already shows is hidden, not drawn twice; pain scores are 0-10 only (on % better a pain keeps its 0-10 slider); a % complaint's distance shows in 0-10 steps; Gone counts as better; a sign's baseline date is the visit before its first rating; on a phone the group's "Same as last time" chip stays and the "→ n" count goes; the Trend column is hidden between 641 and 900 px; the chart's end labels follow the group filter. **Known, not fixed:** changing a pain's Side (on the Compass drawer or the Musculoskeletal page) starts a new history for that side, because the pain store keys scores by region and side (changing back brings it back). **Wording is DRAFT** until she has read it: "From baseline", "From last visit", "Same as last time", "Baseline set n of m", "Move baseline to today", group names.

## Baseline Compass: score an earlier visit (2026-10-23, LIVE since build 20261022-232900)

Her words, on screenshots of a neck and shoulder patient whose Compass said "first visit · today sets the baseline" while the Care Plan's appointment table listed 4 done bookings: "ive treated this person multiple sessions. how do i set baseline from earlier appointments?"

**Why it said first visit:** Today's visit strip, `phTodayParts` and the Compass counted only visits logged in LCM (`rec.acuSessions`) and scored Compass days, never bookings; the Up next card already counted bookings. There was no way to score a past date in the Compass.

**Her 8 picks** (mock `earlier-visit-baseline-mock-2026-10-23.html`, all as recommended): her notes have a score for each visit; option A, a "Scoring" bar of visit chips; done bookings count as visits; dates = done bookings only; an earlier visit shows complaints + pains only (signs and Energy sit out); a gold line + the Today column takes the date; a quiet "N earlier visits · none scored" hint; computer only.

**Built:**
- `phSeenDays(who, day)`: the days a patient was seen before a day (logged sessions + bookings, not Break), oldest first; takes a name or the record. Read by `phTodayParts` (Re-check joins a return visit), `phTodayStripHtml` ("Last visit 10 Oct"; the phase's "visit N", else the visit count the way the Up next card counts) and `phCxModel` (`back`).
- `phPastBookDays(name)`: the bookings alone, newest first, for the Scoring bar.
- Compass: `phCxAt = { pk, day }` view state (never stored; cleared on a phone width or another patient's card) and `phCxDayOf(name)`. `phCxModel` scores `today` = that day and keeps `now` for the real today; earlier-visit mode drops Energy rows and sign rows, hides "Same as last time" and the drawer's "Move baseline to today". Writes follow the day: `phCxScoreWrite`, the note box (`data-cx-day`, `phCxTypeSave`), the ↓ skip, and pains through `phPmScoreSet(..., atDay)` (that day's pain store; today's severity is untouched, as before). Bar: Today + up to 5 bookings as chips, the rest under an "Older ▾" select (more than 6). Gold line "Scoring an earlier visit: Mon 7 Sep · Back to today"; the header reads "7 Sep" and "Note · 7 Sep".
- Meta line: "4 earlier visits · none scored" until any earlier day has a score; then "N visits · baseline 7 Sep" as before.

**Tested** in the real app (Browser pane, seeded patient with 4 then 8 past bookings, real handlers): bar and hint shown; 7 Sep scored by slider, number key and note, all stored on 7 Sep (Compass + pain store); baseline moved to 7 Sep; 12 Sep showed "1 better", today "3 better" with the chart and Re-check mirror; drawer has no rebase button on an earlier visit; Older ▾ picks 2 Jul; 375 px: no bar, scores today; no new console errors. Not tested on her real data or her phone.

**Judgment calls (disclosed):** more than 6 past bookings = the 5 latest as chips + Older ▾ (keeps the bar one line); when a phase has no planned visit number, the strip shows the overall visit count; the Re-check check now joins Today's visit for a patient with earlier bookings (the return-visit rule).

## Baseline Compass: pain rows on "% better" (2026-10-23, LIVE since build 20261023-010200)

Her words, on a screenshot of the Compass on "% better" with only pain rows (Neck, Shoulder both sides, Left shoulder): "i cant see the % option". Cause: pain rows always scored 0-10, so the switch changed nothing on a pain-only table.

**Her picks** (mock `pain-pct-better-mock-2026-10-23.html`): "Both A and B" (A: a pain takes % like every row, her % kept and the 0-10 score worked out from the baseline; B: a 0-10 score reads as % in From baseline) and "Ask for 0-10 first" when a pain has no baseline yet.

**Built:** `phCxPctBase(hist, moved, day)` = the baseline a % counts from (a score before the day being scored, above 0; a baseline moved to that day or later = none). The model marks such a pain `pctOk`: its slider is 0-100% with the % box, number keys score tens (`data-cx-askk="pnp"`), live drag converts. `phCxPainScoreWrite` turns a % into `now = baseline × (1 − %)` (one decimal) and passes the % to `phPmScoreSet(..., atDay, pct)`, which keeps it as `vis.pains[r|s].pct`; any other "now" write for that day (0-10 on the Compass, the Musculoskeletal page) drops it. `phCxPctOf` reads a row's distance from baseline in % on "% better" + From baseline: her own % for the day, a complaint's own % entry, else (baseline − now) / baseline; a pain entered as % adds "= 3.5 / 10". The group average reads in % too. The 0-10 bubble shows one decimal (3.5).

**Tested** in the real app (seeded patient: Neck 7 and Shoulder 6 on 7 Sep, Left shoulder new): Neck and Shoulder took %, Left shoulder stayed 0-10; key "5" = 50% → stored now 3.5 + pct 50, "50% better = 3.5 / 10"; % box 35 → 3.9; a 0-10 write of 3 dropped the % and read "50% better"; 0-10 switch shows 3.5 / 3 / 4 and step distances; From last visit unchanged; the Musculoskeletal page shows 3.5 / 3 / 4; phone layout fine; no console errors. Not tested on her real data.

**Judgment calls (disclosed):** the % reading on "% better" applies to typed complaints too (one switch, every row), not only pains; the group's average line reads in % on "% better"; a pain's % is stored next to its score in the pain store (one entry), not in the Compass's own days.

## 2026-10-23 · Calendar paste lost the mobiles (Alexandria day list)
Her paste ("1:00 PM - 1:20 PM ANNI CHIEN Pending" then address lines then "Mb: 0431 321 572") added the bookings but no phone reached the profile. Cause: the line-splitter in `phApptParse` that peels a trailing "Available / Break" off a time line also split these one-line "time + name + status" rows, so the Alexandria branch never recognised the paste; the generic path then left the phone in the service text, put "Pending" in the name and mangled Kitti's slot. Fix: the splitter leaves a line alone when it is an Alexandria row (spelled-out AM/PM time range plus a status word); "No appointment" rows are dropped like Lunch / Unavailable. Tested: her exact paste now gives 6 patients with phones (0431 321 572 ...), clean names, no "No appointment" row. NOT tested: a full import click-through into profiles (the import code that writes the phone, `phApptImport`, was not changed); the Alexandria branch still sets no duration from the time range.

## IVF history, animated and drawn (2026-10-23, BUILT 7651e57a, LIVE since build 20261023-015200)

Her ask: "improve this page design - use animations and workflow to illustrate it. use enhanced medical accurate diagrams and animations where relevant", then "show mock before build". Mock `ivf-history-animated-mock-2026-10-23.html`; her 12 picks were all the recommended option, then "Yes, build it".

1. Workflow rail for her current round (`phIvfCurrentRound`): Stimulation → Egg collection → Fertilised → Blastocysts → PGT & frozen → Transfer → Result (an egg freeze: Stimulation → Egg freeze → Mature → Eggs frozen). A step is done once it or anything after it is typed; the gold ring pulses on the next. Plus "One egg, through the lab": GV, MII, 2PN, day 2, day 3, morula, blastocyst, a stage every 2 s (CSS loop from `--k`; a day chip restarts it there).
2. A round reads first: `phIvfStripHtml` draws each stage (`ivc-*` symbols), its count, the % from the nearest earlier typed stage ("% of eggs" when one is skipped; blasts against fertilised), and one dot per egg/embryo (hollow = didn't make it), filling once per opening (`phIvfFillSeen`). Tap a number: − / + in place around the same `data-ivf-field` input (`data-ivf-num-step`; a blank count's − does nothing, + starts at 1). Desktop focuses the number to type over.
3. Untyped stages: faded drawing + grey "–".
4. ✎ on a round (`phIvfHdEdit`): date picker, clinic, egg freeze, delete, and "typed <date>" (or "✓ Log this collection"). The "✓ logged" stamp is off the card.
5. Frozen bank: straws per embryo in a −196 °C cane per round (cap in the round's colour, dashed = used, max 10 + "+N"); the next straw "+ Transfer" would take lifts. Cane tap opens the round; Use ▸ unchanged.
6. Transfer reads first (`phIvfTransferViewHtml`): uterus from the front, catheter through the cervix, embryo ~1–2 cm below the fundus, lining from her last scan on that round up to the transfer; facts chips; Transfer → Blood hCG (her test date + IU/L, else ~9–11 days after a day-5 transfer, 11–13 after day 3) → 6–7 wk scan (outcome word, else 2–3 weeks after a positive test); result pill (ongoing shows weeks from her test, the same ~4w0d convention). Fields behind its ✎ (`phIvfTEdit`), open by themselves while it has no date or a result has just landed.
7. Stim room: scans drawn as both ovaries (her right on the viewer's left), one follicle per counted one (max 12 a side), growing scan to scan by order; a combined count reads "N follicles, both sides"; the last scan 1–3 days before a dated collection is marked "trigger".
8. Motion as the mock (rail fill, gold pulse, egg loop, straw lift, catheter + embryo, follicles); dots fill once. Reduced motion: still pictures (the egg holds on the blastocyst or her tapped day).
9.–10. "+ transfer from these eggs" removed (handler too); "Use a frozen embryo ▸" sits in the round's footer beside the room button.
11. A round with no month asks quietly: "add month & clinic ✎" (opens the round with its ✎ row).
12. Phone: stages and rail wrap 4 + 3, dots and the footer note hidden.

Removed as dead: `phIvfFunnelCellsHtml`, `phIvfFrozenTileHtml`, `phIvfConvHtml`, the `data-ivf-frozen-step` and `data-ivf-add-transfer` handlers, and their CSS. The closed round keeps its one-line funnel; the closed transfer line drops "fresh/frozen" (now in its title).

Judgment calls (disclosed): a "Current round · date · clinic" caption above the rail; the bank straw lifts on a loop like the mock; the transfer gets the same read-first ✎ as a round; blastocyst % is per fertilised egg; "trigger" is inferred from timing. Tested with a seeded patient at desktop and 375 px; not on her real data or phone.

### Preparing the lining: the third tab of the fertility pictures (her 2026-10-23 ask "a medical animation to show how Chinese medicine aims to improve the environment of the uterus to support embryo implantation and growth, based on TCM principles and research results"; mock `womb-lining-animation-mock-2026-10-23.html`)
Her picks: lives on the IVF picture as a third tab (Balanced | IVF | Lining), on the IVF, Natural fertility and Pregnancy plan pictures (`PH_PP_LINING`); story = before, treating (needles, moxa, herbs), after (as mocked, looping, 26 s); treatments always the three; evidence tags under the picture AND on the sheet; honest note ("large human trials ... not shown more live births") on the sheet only; wording = TCM terms + plain words; no Liver step; chips + slider on the sheet; zoom circle kept at right. Her three changes to the mock: a more lifelike womb, slower (26 s), and the Before follows her findings.
- **Code:** `PH_PP.SC.cd_lining` (own block just before "the one runner"), `phPpLiningFnd(name)`, `phPpLiningHtml(key, big, shown)`, `PH_PP_LIN_STEPS`; `phPpSvg(id, key, name)` swaps `data-pp` to `cd_lining` while `phPpFlip` = 2 and keeps `data-pp-base` + `data-pp-pt` (the patient); `phPpBarHtml` adds the Lining chip; `phPpFlipTo` swaps the picture inside the same frame (unmounts the old one, `phPpScan` mounts the new). Chips (`data-pp-stage`) and the slider (`data-pp-scrub`) pause the story on a moment (`S.hold`, `S.go(t)`); Play resumes. Reduced motion holds on After and the chips still step through it.
- **Findings (the Before):** thin = latest IVF scan lining under 8 mm (no scan = not thin); cold = a cold mark at the lower belly (or whole) on the Abdomen chart (today's, else the latest visit's), or epi minus lower of 0.5 degrees C or more; poor flow = a Blood stasis or Blood deficiency pattern that is Likely / Possible / agreed and not dismissed. No patient (a teaching card) = all three. With none found the labels read "Lining before care" / "Blood flow" / "Embryo may not settle" / "Surface not ready".
- **Evidence wording** (from the 2026-10-23 search, low certainty throughout): pooled small trials, lower uterine artery PI / RI after acupuncture (2019 meta-analysis, very low / low certainty; a 2025 overview rated the underlying reviews critically low quality); herbs plus usual care, thicker lining and more clinical pregnancies in pooled trials, quality mixed; sticky tips (pinopodes), integrin alpha-v beta-3, LIF and HOXA10 raised in rat studies only; warming the womb = TCM principle, no direct measurement.
- **Tested** (real app via the harness, reduced motion): sheet opens, Lining chip swaps the picture, Before / Treating / After chips, slider, Play, back to IVF and Balanced, the Natural fertility card, a pattern picture keeps its two-chip bar, findings from a seeded scan and Abdomen chart, 360 px phone frame. **Not exercised:** a real patient with a real scan, the moving loop by eye, the Pregnancy plan's picture on her phone, the flow finding from a real Patterns page.
- **Trap found:** `phPpMount` swallows a throw inside `make` / the warm frame and leaves `svg.__pp` unset, and the document MutationObserver then re-mounts the picture forever (the page hangs). A scene that throws on its first frame hangs the page rather than showing nothing.

Those were saved before `phApptCleanName` learned the status words, so they pointed at a "… Confirmed" patient, and re-pasting the day would have added a second booking beside each (the dupe check matches by name). `loadPharmacy` now drops the trailing status word (`PH_APPT_STATUS_TAIL`, shared with `phApptCleanName`) from every booking on load and on every cloud pull. Tested locally: saved "… Confirmed" / "… Pending" bookings load clean; a re-paste of the day matches them (no doubles) and fills the phones. Not touched: the "… Confirmed" patient accounts and blank draft prescriptions the old paste auto-made (hers to delete).

- **Remove a complaint:** a secondary complaint row's ▾ menu has Remove (`phCxRemoveSec`, through `phCcWrite`, toast with Undo that puts it back in place). The main complaint and pain rows have no ▾ (as before). Signs already had ▾ > Not watching.

- Tested in the real app with a seeded patient: remove + Undo, add Watch (chip, stored pick, shows in the phase's Watch text), the menu list. NOT tested: phone width, removing a Watch chip by tap, the Care Plan page repaint while open.

## 2026-10-23 · Photo batch: every picked photo saves (the "Saved 0 of 3 — image couldn't be read" error)
Her report (screenshot from her Android phone, 3 tongue photos, "Saved 0 of 3 — 3 didn't save (image couldn't be read ...)"): "i keep
getting this error periodically on my mobile. i need to be able to upload multiple photos at once and have it all save".
**Cause:** a picked gallery photo is a pointer back into the phone's gallery; Save re-opened it later through `<img>` in
`phRenCompressToBlob`. On Android that read can fail (the gallery's hold on the file lapsing after a while, or a big camera photo decoded
while memory is tight), and one failed decode failed the photo outright; all of a batch at once matches the hold lapsing.
**Fix:** (1) `phRenHold(x)` copies each picked photo's bytes into memory straight away (`x.ready`); used by `phRenConfirmOpen`,
`phRenConfirmOpenBatch` and `phRenConfirmAdd` (+ Add more / paste / drop); Save all and the single Save wait for the copy. A copy that
fails keeps the original file. (2) `phRenCompressToBlob` tries three ways before giving up: `<img>`, `createImageBitmap` after 300 ms,
then the bytes copied into a fresh Blob; each canvas is emptied after use. The failure message and the "still here, tap Save again" rows
are unchanged for a truly broken file.
**Tested (sandbox):** forced `<img>` failure → saved through the fallback; a 3-byte fake JPEG still fails with "couldn't open that image";
a 3-photo batch (3000×4000) copies all three at pick time and Save all files 3 of 3, modal closes. **Not tested:** on her phone (the lapse
can't be reproduced in a desktop browser).

  fertilisation line and the printed IVF history. Embryo accounting, the export and the intake merge keep reading the typed values.

blast → mature / fertilised / day 3 = 1; 3 eggs + 1 blast → stays blank; 5 / 3 mature / 3 blasts → fertilised + day 3 = 3; 2 eggs +
2 PGT ok → all 2; typed 0 fertilised kept; egg freeze 4 eggs + 4 frozen → 4 mature; funnel line "1 egg → 1 mature → 1 fert. → 1 blast".

## 2026-10-23 · Appointments paste: several dates in one paste
Her ask (the Appointments dialog's Paste list, opened from a patient): "make it possible to paste multiple appointments on multiple dates
and have it added".
**Cause:** the Acupreg (Zanda) day-view and Alexandria day-list branches of `phApptParse` read only the date line ABOVE THE FIRST
appointment, so every appointment in a several-day paste landed on that one date (the Cliniko day / week branch already switched date
at each date line). A date line between two appointments was also swept into the earlier one's service text.
**Fix:** `dkAt[i]` = the nearest date line above line i (else the picked day); both branches read it per appointment and leave date
lines out of the appointment text (`isDateLine`). `phApptParseDateLine` also takes a weekday before a numeric date ("Tue 28/10").
The dialog already imports across several days ("Import N appointments across D days").
**Tested (sandbox, `phApptParse`):** Alexandria list over Fri 24 Oct / Tue 28 Oct / "4/11" → 3 dates; Zanda over "Mon 27 Oct" /
"Tue 28/10" → 2 dates, service "Acupuncture" only; Cliniko list over Fri 31 Oct / Mon 3 Nov → 2 dates; no date lines → the picked day.
**Not handled:** a date and a time on the SAME line ("Tue 28 Oct 4pm Acupuncture"). **Not tested:** her real paste.

## 2026-10-23 · Appointments paste: a patient's own appointment list (date, time, place, service, status, note)
Her paste for Charmaine Mari-De Leon ("add this to Charmaine Mari-De"): 11 tab rows like
"28/10/2026<TAB>10:00 AM<TAB>Alexandria<TAB>Acupuncture — Follow-up, 50 min<TAB>Pending<TAB>note". `phApptParse` read NOTHING from it:
`phApptParseTable` only knows time RANGES, and the date led each row.
- `phApptParseList(text)` (before `phApptParse`, tried right after the table reader): rows of ≥ 4 tab cells whose first cell is a date
  (`phApptParseDateLine`, dd/mm/yyyy) and second a single clock time, on ≥ 60% of the lines. service = column 4 + "; " + the note;
  "50 min" → `durationMin`; a Cancelled / no-show status (`PH_APPT_HIST_SKIP`) is left out; no name column, so each row is
  `needsName` and takes the patient the dialog was opened from (`historyName` = `forPatient`).
**Tested (sandbox):** her exact paste → 10 appointments (27 Oct cancelled left out), each on its own date; opened from the patient
"Charmaine Mari-De Leon", Paste list → Import → 10 on her record; pasting again → still 10 (same date + time + patient refreshes).
**Not done by me:** adding them to her live record (her sign-in): she pastes it once this is live.

## 2026-10-23 · Her pregnancy is one fact (plan date, Pregnant? test, IVF transfer result)
Her words (screenshot of "Before Nyssa's plan starts" asking her week): "this data is already in her file. can you connect it? make
the pregnant data universal for all relevant input in app".
**Before:** two stores never read each other: the Pregnancy plan's date (`rec.pregnancy.start`, week 0) and the Pregnant? test
(`rec.pregTest.date`, the "4 weeks" anchor); an IVF transfer's positive result counted only for the appointment flag and the cycle pause.
**Now (derived on read, nothing copied):** `phPregStartOf(rec)` = the plan date, else a positive test's date − 28, else the latest
positive IVF transfer's `pregTestDate` − 28 (a negative test with no plan date = not pregnant). `phTpPregFor(rec)` returns it, so every
reader of the plan date follows (the Pregnancy block, timeline items, the plan list's "week N", the Breech / Labour pictures, the
trimester sync). `phPregPositive` / `phPregAnchor` feed the Pregnant? button, the appointment 🌸 week and the cycle pause.
The "Before her plan starts" popup (`phPregQOpen`) no longer asks when her file already has it: the plan starts on that date's trimester
with "Pregnancy plan started at week N · from her file". Her plan date always wins; typing it in the Pregnancy block still writes it.
Left as they are: the Timeline / blood-test items that read a test as a dated EVENT, and the "test logged" banner.
**Tested (sandbox, a copy with only this change; another session's work was mid-way):** test at week 24 → week 24, plan opens on
Second Trimester with no question; plan date 20 weeks ago → button "Pregnant · 20w 0d", appointment "🌸 20w0d"; transfer result only →
11 weeks (button, picture); negative test → not pregnant, the question still shows. **Not tested:** her real patients, her phone.

## 2026-10-23 · Appointments paste: Zanda's own list of one patient's appointments (her "how can you get copy paste to read this"; 7a22a98f, LIVE since build 20261023-065300)
Her paste (Charmaine Mari-De) read nothing: each booking is "28/10/2026 Wed 10:00 AM-10:30 AM", the end time again on its own
line, then the billed items ("Alexandria Acupuncture FOLLOW UP 50 minutes"), a line missing when nothing was billed yet.
New `phApptParseClientList` (after `phApptParseList` in `phApptParse`): one booking per date-led time-range line; the end-time
line and a bare status word are skipped; Cancelled etc. leave the booking out; the rest is the service. Length comes from the
range (30 min), not the "50 minutes" in the service name. No name in the paste, so rows wait for it (needsName), filled when
the sheet is opened from the patient. Steps aside unless most time-range lines in the paste are these date-led ones. The same
rows copied as tab-separated cells read the same.
**Tested (real app on localhost, not signed in):** her exact paste, opened from the patient: 11 rows across 9 days; Import
added all 11 (both 4 Sep and both 8 Aug kept); the tab-separated copy parses identically; an Alexandria day list, the
tab list and a dated day list still go to their own readers.
**Not tested:** at the acupreg location (ACU+CHM pills and pre/post transfer tags show there, not on the test copy).
**Published alone:** 845917a0 (Red flags) sat on session-a above it, held for her word, so publish commit 89e3f633 was built
on 7a22a98f in a private index (GIT_INDEX_FILE) and pushed to main; merge 271acde0 joined it into session-a with session-a's
own tree (no file changes), so the next publish-live.sh still fast-forwards.

One desktop `@media (min-width: 641px)` block after "To Order board chips", covering Dashboard, Communications, Inventory, Formula refill, Stocktake, Stock statistics, Photos, Stock movements, Price review, Suppliers, End of day, All templates, Relocation, Settings and the Prescriptions list.
- 32px 6px: `.ph-rp-menubtn` (Plan ▾, was a 36px pill), `.ph-ck-chip` everywhere (was 22–26px pills), `.ph-cap-tchip` (photo labels), `.add-btn`, Price review's start button and End of day's Copy prompt (were 44–48px), the refill and Communications searches.
- Plain-text taps now boxes: Dashboard "See all N" and the card's "To order", Communications "N not shown" and Contact, `.ph-load-btn` (Columns · N hidden, Made in house, + Schedule a dispense...), `.ph-os-lnk` (See refill history), Stock statistics' date button and "see the N never..." link, Price review's upload link, Relocation's Edit the move and Open the full list, All templates' + New template, refill's "N below the line".
- Joined bars: Stock statistics' By month | By patient tabs (were bare text); Stocktake's order switch 32px with 6px corners.
- Circles 32px: `.ph-os-chev`, `.ph-os-dots`, `.ph-page-btn`, `.ph-arr-editbtn`, Relocation's ⋯, Suppliers' ✎.
- Menus: Settings' side menu and the template rail are 32px rows, 6px corners.
**Kept:** names and codes in list rows as plain text, code tags, table sort headers, Inventory's table cells (rows stay 32px), Stocktake's scope cards, Relocation / Settings card heads (rows), the Formula refill tabs (her 10-22 design), emoji in Settings / Stocktake / Relocation labels (not touched this round), the phone.
**Tested (sandbox, 1280):** a scan of every control on all 19 pages finds nothing off 32px / 6px outside the kept list; no page scrolls sideways; Dashboard and Inventory checked by eye. **Not tested:** pages with real data (Communications' Due list, Photos with many shots), tablet widths. **Seen, not fixed (not this work):** Formula refill's Below the line tab throws in `refSuggestRecipe` when a prescription has no `ingredients` array (`t.ingredients.length`), found on sandbox data.

## Emoji out of the pages + Formula refill crash fix (2026-10-23, LIVE build 20261023-120300)
Her words: "yes fix it and take the emoji out, push".
- **Crash fix:** `refSuggestRecipe` (and one Formula refill filter beside it) read `t.ingredients.length` on every prescription; one with no `ingredients` array threw and blanked Formula refill's Below the line tab. Now `(t.ingredients || []).length`. Tested: the tab draws its 9 rows on the sandbox data that crashed it, no console error.
- **Emoji out (rule: icons are the app's sprite, never emoji):** Settings' side menu (`PH_SETTINGS_GROUPS` icons no longer printed) and every Settings card title (`PH_SETTINGS_CARDS` labels); All templates' tabs (`PH_ALLTPL_TABS`); page and section titles (Supplements, Intake review, Scheduled dispenses, Where I buy from, Made in house, Past price reviews, Stocktake history, Stock statistics' Single herbs / Formulas groups, Inventory's kind label, Treatment Care Plan templates, the plan lead line); Stocktake's scope cards; Relocation's list heads (the list icon is no longer shown, and the Add a list form lost its emoji icon picker; stored `icon` values are left as they are) and its move-to chips; Suggest from codes, Export CSV / Export as PDF, Filters, the duplicate-photos button, Choose the Cliniko export, Save as template. Inventory's 🛒 column head and the Prescriptions list's 💊 Dispense button now use the sprite's cart and pill icons.
- **Left on purpose:** ⚠ on below-zero stock (a safety warning), ↔ on the "wider than the screen" scroll note (an arrow), code comments.
**Tested (sandbox, 1280):** a sweep of the visible text on all 22 pages (Settings with every group opened) finds no emoji left except the two kept above; no page throws. **Not tested:** pages with her real data, letters / print output, the phone.

## 2026-10-23 · Concept pictures on the anatomical body (Yang, Yin deficiency, Heat)
Her words: "i like this style of animation. the details, proportions, scale and medical accuracy. use this as the main model for all animations" (her screenshot of the body chart with its organs), then "build it and push" on mock `yang-anatomical-mock-2026-10-23.html`.

- `anatPot(S, svg, kind)` replaces the pot + cartoon person for **Yang deficiency, Yin deficiency and Heat** (the `pot` helper and `PO` table are gone; the person `body` helper stays for Qi deficiency). Balanced = Ming Men fire between the kidneys and warmth rising, labelled. Yang: fire low and flickering, the rise stalls, pale face, cold hands / feet / belly, organs lose colour, a shiver burst. Yin: the water at the kidneys shrinks, empty heat wisps rise, hot cheeks / palms / soles, night sweats. Heat: fire grows and rises, the body flushes red, heat over the head, sweat.
- `PH_PP.TALL` lists the 300 x 200 pictures; `phPpSvg` gives them that viewBox (the others stay 300 x 130). All motion off under reduced motion (the runner's `RM()`).
- Labels are DRAFT wording.
- Tested in the sandbox: all three mount, both Balanced and pattern sides, no new console errors. Not tested: on her phone, a male patient's figure (code path only), the Care Plan Diagnosis row and Fang Bing Ren plates in a real patient.
- **Not yet moved to the anatomical model:** the other pattern pictures (Blood stasis, Blood deficiency, Qi stagnation, Qi deficiency, Damp, Cold-damp, Kidney yang, Cold channels, Plum-pit, Middle cold), the 29 condition pictures and the 22 formula pictures. Next batches.

## 2026-10-24 · Medical history: rows that held two conditions split
Her words: "audit the medical history signs the same way", then "split all as you picked". Every row already started with a capital. Five rows named two things; three split, two kept (do not re-ask).
- **Split:** "Asthma or respiratory condition" → **Asthma** (`asthma`) + **Other lung condition** (`lung_condition`); "Kidney or liver condition" → **Kidney condition** (`kidney_condition`) + **Liver condition** (`liver_condition`); Family history "Endometriosis or PCOS" → **Endometriosis** (`fh_endometriosis`) + **PCOS** (`fh_pcos`). **Kept:** Family history "Heart disease or stroke" (both blood-vessel disease), "Tinea / fungal skin (ringworm, athlete's foot)" (one condition, its kinds named). The list is 42 rows now.
- **Old answers** (`PH_HX_SPLIT`, `phHxSplitCarry`, run once per record by the boot pass `phHxSplitCarryAll` after `phMigrateHistoryV2`): old No → both rows No; old Yes → the row its note names (the smart-paste words), or both rows when the note names neither or both, the note copied; a row already answered is never touched. Kidney / liver: her two separate answers from before 2026-08 (`rec.medicalHistory.kidney_disease` / `liver_disease`) decide each organ while they still add up to the joined answer. The old key is never deleted; `rec.historyMoved[old]` holds the answer carried, so a later answer under the old key carries again. A carry does not stamp `updatedAt` (so it never beats a real edit from the other computer in a sync; the other device carries at its own boot). An intake form filled before the split answers the old key: `phHxSplitHist` makes it stand for each new row the form left blank (review and apply both).
- **Wired:** `PH_HISTORY_GROUPS` and intake.html's list; the herb-caution keywords (`PH_CLINICAL_KEYWORDS`); the body figure (`PH_HX_ORGAN_MAP`: Kidney lights the kidneys only, Liver the liver only, both lung rows the lungs); smart paste (`PH_HX_PASTE_RULES` / `_FAMILY`; "renal" is a whole word now, so "adrenal" no longer ticks Kidney); Fang Bing Ren "Breathing: wheezing" pre-fill (`PH_TRI_PREFILL_HX.wheeze_tend`) reads **Asthma only** ("Other lung condition" does not always wheeze), so only Asthma wears "Feeds Fang Bing Ren".

## 2026-10-24 · Session grid follows the cycle; Cycle page loses the Usual period card
Her words: "today is her cd16 and its putting the appointment during menstrual phase, this is incorrect. make the table sync with the cycle page. when i click on a date in the cycle page, it opens that page. audit the page to merge repeating chips and data input - i dont need the usual period with the cycle data input - make mock first before building". Mock `cycle-merge-grid-sync-mock-2026-10-24.html`; her 8 picks, all the recommended ones.
- **The bug:** a new cycle-synced plan cascades every phase it passes through to today (since = done = today), so Menstruation's window held today and claimed today's booking (Luteal counted it too), and to-come squares ran on "+7 days" from today regardless of cycle day.
- **Fix (`phTpCycPhaseOn`):** on a `PH_TP_CYCLE_SYNC` plan, a booking or logged visit dated from her current period on is filed on the phase the cycle names that day (`phTpCycleEffectivePhase`, the band's own read; up to today the cycle never rolls, a day ahead is projected by `phCycleCompAsOf`). `phTpPhaseVisitRows` and `phTpPhaseSessionDates` use it; a hand-filed `a.phaseId`, earlier cycles, an overdue cycle and a plan moved past the wait by hand keep the phase windows.
- **To-come squares (pick: next cycle's days):** with no cycle-day cadence, a phase's squares land on its own CD window (`phTpCdCtx`) from its first CD by its rhythm, this cycle if the window is still ahead, else the next (`phTpCdDates`). Each to-come square shows its CD under the date and opens the Cycle page on that day (pick), the calendar on that month with the day ringed (`data-tp-cyc-go`); a past day opens its day card. Template row order is kept (pick).
- **Cycle page:** the Usual period card is gone from the page (`presCycleSectionBodyHtml`). The footer asks the length once: the stepper while Regular, her shortest–longest range (the same `rec.usual.period.len` store and `data-pfw-range` save) when Irregular. A **Usually** line under it is worked out from her logged periods (`phCycUsual` / `phCycUsualHtml`: each period's heaviest flow, most-picked colour, clots, pain; discharge and PMS over all days), "from N periods"; with fewer than 2 logged periods her old answers stand in ("from her answers"). The day card's "usual" rings read the same `phCycUsual`; its "Edit her usual pattern" button and the length / cold hands strip are gone. Cold hands was already on Ask her ("Cold hands or feet", same signs). Wheel / Chart / Play went with the card (pick). Saved answers are kept and still count where they did.
- Tested (sandbox, synthetic patient on CD16, Natural fertility, booking today): today sits in Luteal only (1 of 2), Menstruation ~6 Nov CD1, Follicular 1 ~11 Nov CD6, Follicular 2 ~18 Nov CD13, Ovulation ~19 Nov CD14, Luteal 2 ~31 Oct CD23 (squares 54×46, content fits); a square tap lands on the Cycle page with 31 Oct ringed; the Usually line from 3 logged periods; Irregular shows the range and saves 26–33; the day card rings Creamy / Irritable / Bloating as usual; no console errors; 375px no sideways scroll. **Not tested:** real patients, an IVF plan (unchanged by design), a booking filed by hand, the phone by eye. **Seen, not changed:** the phases the new plan passed through still read "Complete · 24 Oct" in This week; Low mood logged on a day (stored as the day's mood, not in its PMS list) is not counted in the Usually PMS.

## Records → Photos: a tap never moves the page (2026-10-25)
Her words, with a screenshot of the Records → Photos tiles: "when i click dropdown to change tongue to something else, the screen jumps up. stop this".
- **Cause** (reproduced in the sandbox, 3672 px → 197 px): after a label, `phRecPhotosLabel` rang the next photo still needing a label and `phRecPhotosRing` scrolled to it. When the photos after it were all labelled, the next one wrapped round to the top of the page, and the page jumped there. A second, smaller jump: opening one tile's type list folds away another tile's open list, and a list folding above the tapped tile pulled that tile up.
- **Now:** a tap (type list, shot chips, Undo) keeps the page still. `phRecPhotosFocusNext(id, still)` rings the next photo needing a label only while it is fully on screen (`phRecPhotosOnScreen`); otherwise no photo is rung. The keys (1-9, Enter, arrows) still walk the page to the next one, as before (her 2026-10-15 key flow; `byKey` on `phRecPhotosLabel`). `phRecPhotosKeepStill(id, paint)` scrolls back by however far a repaint moved the tapped tile (opening a list, a label repaint). A label that moves a photo to another group in By type keeps the scroll where it was. `phRecPhotosTypePick` is the one type write for a tap and for the 1-9 keys with the list open.
- This changes her 2026-10-14 pick "the next photo is rung and scrolled to": a tap no longer scrolls (her newer word).
- **Tested** (sandbox, 1280 × 800, 54 seeded photos, removed after): labelling the last unlabelled photo near the bottom stays at 3584 px (it was 3672 → 197 before the fix); four labels in a row, shot chips and type list, stay at 3584 px, the ring moves to a photo on screen or none; opening a second tile's list while another is open above keeps the tapped tile at 542 px; By type: opening and picking keep 1200 px; the keys still scroll to the next photo (0 → 44 px); no console errors from this code. **Not tested:** her real photos or a touch device; the phone width (no layout or CSS changed); the Prescriptions search (this change touches only the Records → Photos code).
- Pages that show this fact, checked: Records → Photos (both groupings). The patient's own Photos page and the photo viewer read the same store and were not changed.

## 2026-10-24 · Pulse page: tabs, the chart moves itself
**Her words:** "make this pulse page more ui interactive friendly and space saving" (screenshot of the Pulse page). Mock `pulse-page-mock-2026-10-24.html`; her 8 picks, all the recommended one: quality chips as tabs (one row shows); the chart moves itself (no second chart); a box tapped with nothing picked opens its qualities; one tab under the chart; the Fang Bing Ren tab = the linked qualities like Tongue; Referred stays under each wrist; the bar stays in view; a picked quality stays until tapped off.
- **`phHxConstitPulseHtml`** (every host that draws the pulse chart: the Pulse page, the constitution tab): `.ph-pls-tool` = a tab bar in the Fang Bing Ren switch shape (Concavity · Radius · Intensity · Texture, a count each, `presPulseGrp`) + "✕ Erase", and the open group's chips as one joined bar (`.ph-tng2-seg`). Sticky under the name band (`--ph-pls-top`, `phPulseStickyTop`; `.ph-pres-panel:has(.ph-pls) { overflow: clip }` as for Today's visit); not sticky on a phone.
- **Picking:** nothing is picked at first (`presHxConstitPulseStamp = null`, was "thin"); a quality tap picks it, a second tap or Esc lets go (Esc is caught first so the app's own Esc no longer closes the panel then). With nothing picked, a box tap opens `phPulsePopHtml` beside it (same 4 tabs, `presPulsePopGrp`; a chip = `phPulseCellToggle` on that box; "Clear box"); placed by `phPulsePopPlace` after every repaint (right, else left, else under, inside the chart's box); a tap outside or Esc closes it.
- **▶ In action** on the legend line (`data-pa-play`, `PH_PA.play`, view only) with 72 / min | Slowed: `phPaOverHtml` puts the beat svg over each wrist's own grid (absolute, so it never takes a grid cell); `phPaLay` measures the chart's column and row labels and draws only today's arches, in `--ph-blue`; the chart's static today drawing is hidden while it runs (`.pa-on`). `phPulseAnimHtml` and `phPaSideHtml` (the second chart) are gone, with their CSS.
- **Under the chart** one tab (`presPulseBelow`): Summary (Depth / Diameter / Amplitude + the normal resting rate for her age, always shown now; the "Normal range" toggle is gone; SHPD notes), Rate & rhythm (`phVisitHammerHtml(..., bare)`: open, no fold), Note (the copy box), Fang Bing Ren (gold "N ticked", `phPulseFbrTicked`): the qualities that reach a pulse sign (`phPulseChipLinks`, off the pulse specs of `PH_FBR_CHART_PREFILL` by the same substring rule as `phFbrChartKnown`), lit when drawn, signs on hover, a tap picks it to paint. Linked chips carry `.tfp-lk` everywhere. The Today | Usually sign list (`phTriInlineHtml(name, "pulse")`) left this page; the function stays (its refresh path still exists).
- **Tested** (sandbox, 1280 and 375): painting with a picked quality, let go by tap and by Esc (panel stays open), box popup open / tab / write / clear / outside tap / Esc / second tap, ▶ off and on, the beat over the right cells, all four lower tabs, the Fang Bing Ren tab's chips and count (thin → "Pulse: thin"), the sticky bar under the name band, no page overflow at 375, no new console errors, Prescriptions search narrows and returns.
- **Not tested:** the beat actually moving (the pane is hidden, so the runner does not tick; a still frame was checked); a real touch device; the constitution tab host by eye. **Seen, not mine:** a 15px horizontal scrollbar under the chart at this sandbox width, the same in the committed version.
- **Pages checked:** Pulse page; Today's visit and Care Plan findings read `phVisitPulseSummaryText`, unchanged.

## 2026-10-25 · Readable text: app-wide contrast audit
- **Her ask:** "audit app to correct text that is not readable" (screenshot: the Fang Bing Ren Quick assess bar, white words on a near-white box). Audit = measure and fix all, one pass.
- **How it was measured:** a throwaway script walked every visible text run in `#pharmacyPage` (22 tabs, 24 patient pages, Quick assess open), with animations finished first, against its real composited background: 4.5:1, or 3:1 for large text. 77 spots failed at first; about 15 were not real (white text on a navy gradient the script cannot read, the Messages tone switch's sliding thumb, the composer's invisible text mirror, the Fang Bing Ren bar's clipped overlay label).
- **Fixed:**
  - Quick assess bar: the chip sweep had put a paper box behind white words; it is now a faint white box on the navy header (`.ph-qa2-tog`).
  - Fang Bing Ren colours: Fu Zi and Huang Lian now take ink words, Chai Hu (`#478052`) and Ma Huang (`#8C7046`) are a shade deeper so white words read, the grey fallback takes ink. `fgOf` picks white or ink at the true crossover (0.207, was 0.34), and a formula shade in the dead band (0.18–0.24, readable with neither) is nudged a step lighter or darker (`readable`). A ticked row's ✕ and point pill are solid, no 75% fade, no white wash.
  - Amber text: new `--ph-low-press #8A5708` (5.4:1 on the amber tint); all 87 text uses of `--ph-low-deep` read it ("Low", "Not dispensed yet", "low", stock %, price review badge, restock bands). Fills and lines keep `--ph-low-deep`.
  - Treatment's green and navy blocks and the visit table's green rows: their soft / faint words were 82% / 62% white, now white / 94% white; the "+ Add" formula action is a see-through chip there (it was white on white).
  - Patterns: green, orange and sand patterns put ink on their colour (`--on`), so "Agree with this pattern", the bubbles and Likely tags read.
  - Faded labels lost their fade: page header counts (`.ph-shell-sub`), Fang Bing Ren group counts, All templates counts, Dispense band counts; the pain-map caption under the idle figure stays readable (only the drawing fades); the Compass "Goal" and "–", the relocation date label, the Messages tab tag and the Stats "Usage" head now read the faint / soft ink.
  - Count badges on a picked tab (Tongue, Pulse, Fang Bing Ren switches, To Order segments) darken instead of whitening.
  - Appointments now-line time: ink on gold. Find the Ren ★: gold-deep. The "i" dots: ink on grey.
  - Cycle calendar days from the next month: 80% (was 50% × 85%).
- **Left on purpose:** disabled controls (the empty price-review start button, future cycle days): WCAG exempts them.
- **Tested** (sandbox): full rescan at 1280 (22 tabs, 24 patient pages, Quick assess open) and 375 (13 tabs + 4 patient pages): no real failures left; Quick assess bar by eye; Prescriptions search narrows (Tess shown, Zara hidden) and returns.
- **Not tested:** pages that need data the seed lacks (an IVF round, a full stock list with every status, Settings editors opened deep), by eye at phone width beyond the scan.
- **Pages checked:** every tab above and Patient details, Today's visit, Ask her, Tongue, Pulse, Abdomen, Musculoskeletal, Cycle, Fang Bing Ren, Patterns, Care Plan, Treatment, Log, Notes, Medical history, Ren, Supplements, Blood tests, IVF history, Visit checks, Photos, Appointments, Messages.

## Abdomen page: the Pulse page's way (2026-10-25)
- **Her ask:** "build the abdomen batch" → asked which; she picked **"Abdomen like new Pulse page"**. Mock `abdomen-page-mock-2026-10-25.html` (artifact PVqhUm3kFPnxwpXBcpZfQB). Her 10 picks, every one the recommended option:
  1. Seen · Temp · Sounds · Tension · Feel are one tab bar (the Pulse page's), only the open group's chips show.
  2. A picked sign stays picked (plum ring) and taps places on and off; tap it again or Esc lets go. The "— where" bars went.
  3. A place tapped with nothing picked opens its own signs beside it: a tab per group, only the signs that can sit there, Clear place.
  4. "Whole abdomen" is a place, a chip under the charts: flat, tension and whole-belly signs.
  5. The tab panel sits beside the charts; the photo tile is a Photo tab.
  6. The Fang Bing Ren gold count sits on its tab only; the header keeps "Abdomen" and "Nothing unusual".
  7. The °C steppers (CV12, CV4) sit in the Temp row.
  8. The sign bar stays in view under the name band.
  9. A ✕ Erase brush, like Pulse.
  10. The first tab is "Summary"; the sentence still starts "Reads as".
- **Where:** `phVisitAbdHtml(name, draft, panels)` draws the bar, the charts (+ `phAbdPopHtml`, the Whole abdomen chip) and the tab panel; `phAbdHtml` builds the Summary / Note / Photo bodies. View state: `presAbdGrp`, `presAbdBrush` (a sign key or `"erase"`), `presAbdPop` + `presAbdPopFrom` / `presAbdPopGrp`, `presAbdBelow` (reset per patient in `phVisitFndResetDraft`). Rules: `phAbdOkAt` (water at umb, gas at `PH_ABD_SPOTS`, tight hypochondrium at `PH_ABD_HYPO`, flat and tension whole only; a wrong place gives `phAbdWhyNot` in the toast and writes nothing), `phAbdAt` (a Seen sign sits by row), `phAbdSits`, `phAbdToggleAt` (one temperature per place via `phAbdTempOnly`), `phAbdClearAt` (Erase / Clear place; a side zone leaves its row's Seen signs, and the popup's footer lists only what Clear place clears). Writes: `phAbdClick` → `phAbdPlaceTap` / `phAbdChartWrite` (a copy of the chart, changed, saved through `phAbdWrite`); view-only taps repaint with `phAbdRepaint`, writes with `phAbdRefresh` (+ rail, earlier visits, side panel); `phAbdAfterPaint` sets the sticky top (`phPulseStickyTop` now also sets `.ph-abd3`) and places the popup (`phAbdPopPlace`: beside the place inside the card, under it when there is no room, inside the screen when wider than the card). Esc and the outside tap: capture listeners like the Pulse page's.
- **Data:** shapes unchanged. The Whole abdomen place stores `"whole"` in a sign's list; an empty list still reads as whole, and adding a zone to an empty list keeps `"whole"`. `phAbdTWashes`: a zone with no temperature of its own takes the whole abdomen's (front, side and every read-only mini chart). The side view gets clear tap bands per row when `opts.tap` (`data-abd-band`, `.ph-abd3-band`). Visible pulsation saved only at "whole" draws at epi as an empty list did.
- **Removed:** the shared `[data-visit-abd2]` handler and the `.ph-visit-abd-map` branch of `[data-visit-zone]` (only the old abdomen page used them), `phAbdExam` and its `presVisitFndRefresh` hand-back, `presVisitAbdRegion / Pick / Brush`, the header `.ph-abd-fbrn` and its CSS. `.ph-abd2-wh` / `.ph-abd2-spots` / `.ph-abd2-tt` CSS stays (unused now; harmless).
- **Motion** (all off under reduced motion): the popup rises in on open (`.in`), the tapped chip pops (`.pop`), the mark just put down pops in once (`[data-m].in`; each front-chart mark sits in its own `data-m="sign|place"` group).
- **Phone (≤640):** the sign tabs a 2-column grid like the Pulse page, the bar does not stick (the Pulse rule), chip rows and tab bars wrap, the panel under the charts, the popup the chart area's width inside the card, "Nothing unusual" a lone 32px chip (it was drawn as a bar segment with only its right line).
- **Tested** (sandbox, "Mock Abdo", 1280 and 375): pick tender → tap lower on / off → saved; Esc lets go without closing the patient; popup on L hypo (Seen / Temp / Feel only, no Sounds), tight hypochondrium from the popup, Clear place, outside tap and Esc close it, a second place moves it; the side band (umb) opens Umbilical; Whole abdomen popup (no Sounds), tension from it; Erase at epi clears epi and its row's Seen signs; water one tap off / on; gas at R hypo refused with the toast, at L flank saved; °C stepper and a typed °C; Note saves; Photo tab shows the tile; Fang Bing Ren tab groups its chips and a chip there picks it and opens its group; the bar sticks at the name band's bottom; no tap moves the page (presPanel scroll kept); thin wall + cool on Whole abdomen → `["whole"]`, cool washes the 7 zones without their own temperature; no app errors in the console; 375: no page overflow, no clipped tab, popup inside the card; Prescriptions search: no match → 0 rows, "mock" → its row, cleared → back (the sandbox has one script only).
- **Pages checked:** Abdomen, Today's visit (the Abdomen tile's sentence reads the whole place), Treatment Care Plan (findings mini chart shows the whole wash), Findings trend (Seen / Temp / Sounds / Tension / Feel rows), Fang Bing Ren (opens, no errors).
- **Not tested:** a real touch phone and the Key2's 360 × 469; the Visit checks table and the printed letter with a whole-abdomen mark (they read `phAbd2Summary`, which says "whole abdomen"); a patient with only old `a.picks`.

## 2026-10-25 · Pictures open big; a smaller person; the tongue drawn from its words
- **Her ask** (screenshots of the Guide card's key-sign pictures and the Zhen Wu Tang formula picture): "i really like these diagrams make it expandable. make the model diagram slightly smaller, make the tongue more accurately depicted e.g. swollen".
- **Expand:** `phPpXpHtml` puts one 32px round button (the new `i-expand` sprite icon) in the top-left corner of every formula picture (`phFpHtml`), pattern picture (`phPpHtml`) and the Guide card's big key-sign picture. It sits on the drawing's own grid area when the picture is a grid. A tap opens the one sheet (`#phPpSheet`, `.ph-pp-card`, now up to 1100px wide, the drawing capped to the window height): `phFpSheetOpen` / `phPpSheetOpen` as before, and the new `phKsSheetOpen(group)` for a sign, with its words and weight dots. Top-left because the tongue inset sits top right. Not on the thumbs (they already open big) or inside the sheet (`o.big`).
- **Smaller person:** `body()` puts the person, the burner lines and the scene's group in one group at `ZK = .88`, shrunk toward the top of the head; a label's leader dot and the UPPER / MIDDLE / LOWER names follow through `ZP`. Every scene is unchanged.
- **Tongue:** `tngD(s, t)` draws the outline from two amounts. s = swollen: wider, with a broad round tip (the side runs root to mid as a curve, then a quarter ellipse to the tip). t = teeth marks: the side edge dips in between five bulges, deepest mid-side. `tngFeat` reads the scene's trouble words: swollen / puffy = s 1, t 1; wet / slippery = s .35 (wet t .5) plus a shine; "red tip" = red points at the tip; "dry" = cracks. `tg.words(a, b, T)` now takes the scene's trouble amount, so the shape grows in with Trouble and goes back to the chart's own outline on Settled. The coat is clipped to the shape.
- **Tested** (sandbox, 1280 and 375): Zhen Wu Tang inline and big ("Pale, swollen": wide, scalloped); Settled goes back to the normal outline; the four hand-drawn scenes run with no error; the key-sign sheet (title, moving picture); the Gui Zhi pattern picture opens big; the sheet fits at 375 with no sideways scroll; Prescriptions search narrows and returns. Shapes also rendered on their own (normal / wet / swollen) with headless Edge.
- **Not tested:** every one of the 22 formulas by eye (the shape only reads words, so a formula with none of them keeps the normal outline); a real touch device.
- **Pages checked:** Guide card (formula, pattern and key-sign pictures), Dispense and the Care Plan formula cell use the same `phFpHtml` / thumbs.

Only the lists read it: the Guide mosaic (`phGdfMosaicHtml`), the Fang Bing Ren family cards (`mosaicHtml`), the Ren family list and its group pick (`phRenFamMembers`, the group spot), and the all-signs sheet (`phFbrAllGroupsHtml`).
